\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\frac{\kappa^{2}}{2\pi}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r}\right\|_{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}\exp\left(-\frac{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}\right)^{2}-2\left({\mathbf{u}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}_{r^{\prime}}\right)\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}\right)\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}\right)+\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}\right)^{2}}{2\kappa^{2}\left(\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left({\mathbf{u}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}_{r^{\prime}}\right)^{2}\right)}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\frac{\kappa}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r}\right\|_{2}\cdot{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}\cdot\hat{T}_{1,r,r^{\prime}}+\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}\cdot{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}\cdot\hat{T}_{2,r,r^{\prime}}\right)

where
\hat{T}_{1,r,r^{\prime}},\hat{T}_{2,r,r^{\prime}}
are defined as

\displaystyle\hat{T}_{1,r,r^{\prime}}=\exp\left(-\frac{\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}\right)^{2}}{2\kappa^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r}\right\|_{2}^{2}}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r}\right\|_{2}^{2}\cdot{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}-{\mathbf{u}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}_{r^{\prime}}\cdot{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r}\right\|_{2}\left(\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left({\mathbf{u}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}_{r^{\prime}}\right)^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right)

\displaystyle\hat{T}_{2,r,r^{\prime}}=\exp\left(-\frac{\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}\right)^{2}}{2\kappa^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}^{2}}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\cdot{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}-{\mathbf{u}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}_{r^{\prime}}\cdot{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}\left(\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left({\mathbf{u}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}_{r^{\prime}}\right)^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right)

For the simplicity of notations, we define
T_{1,r,r^{\prime}}=\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r}\right\|_{2}\cdot{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}\cdot\hat{T}_{1,r,r^{\prime}}
and
T_{2,r,r^{\prime}}=\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}\cdot{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}\cdot\hat{T}_{2,r,r^{\prime}}
. Moreover, we define

E_{r,r^{\prime}}=\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r}\right\|_{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}\exp\left(-\frac{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}\right)^{2}-2\left({\mathbf{u}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}_{r^{\prime}}\right)\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}\right)\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}\right)+\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}\right)^{2}}{2\kappa^{2}\left(\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left({\mathbf{u}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}_{r^{\prime}}\right)^{2}\right)}\right)

Lastly, we use the definition of the Gaussian Copula function
\mathcal{C}\left(a,b,\rho\right)=\bm{\Phi}_{2}\left(a,b,\rho\right)-\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(a\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(b\right)
and define

C_{r,r^{\prime}}=\left(\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}\right)\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}\right)+\kappa^{2}{\mathbf{u}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}_{r^{\prime}}\right)\mathcal{C}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r}\right\|_{2}},\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}},\frac{{\mathbf{u}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}_{r^{\prime}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r}\right\|_{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}}\right)

Under these definitions, we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sigma\left({\mathbf{c}}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}_{r}\right)\sigma\left({\mathbf{c}}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}_{r^{\prime}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\left(\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}\right)\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}\right)+\kappa^{2}{\mathbf{u}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}_{r^{\prime}}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r}\right\|_{2}}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+C_{r,r^{\prime}}+\frac{\kappa^{2}}{2\pi}E_{r,r^{\prime}}+\frac{\kappa}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(T_{1,r,r^{\prime}}+T_{2,r,r^{\prime}}\right)

\displaystyle=\hat{\sigma}_{\kappa}\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r},{\mathbf{x}}\right)\hat{\sigma}_{\kappa}\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}},{\mathbf{x}}\right)+\kappa^{2}{\mathbf{u}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}_{r^{\prime}}\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r}\right\|_{2}}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+C_{r,r^{\prime}}+\frac{\kappa^{2}}{2\pi}E_{r,r^{\prime}}+\frac{\kappa}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(T_{1,r,r^{\prime}}+T_{2,r,r^{\prime}}\right)

Plugging back into the expression of
\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}}\left[f\left(\bm{\theta},{\mathbf{x}}\odot{\mathbf{c}}\right)^{2}\right]
gives

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}}\left[f\left(\bm{\theta},{\mathbf{x}}\odot{\mathbf{c}}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\quad=\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r,r^{\prime}=1}^{m}a_{r}a_{r^{\prime}}\hat{\sigma}_{\kappa}\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r},{\mathbf{x}}\right)\hat{\sigma}_{\kappa}\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}},{\mathbf{x}}\right)+\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r,r^{\prime}=1}^{m}a_{r}a_{r^{\prime}}\kappa^{2}{\mathbf{u}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}_{r^{\prime}}\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r}\right\|_{2}}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad+\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r,r^{\prime}=1}^{m}a_{r}a_{r^{\prime}}\left(C_{r,r^{\prime}}+\frac{\kappa^{2}}{2\pi}E_{r,r^{\prime}}+\frac{\kappa}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(T_{1,r,r^{\prime}}+T_{2,r,r^{\prime}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad=\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r=1}^{m}a_{r}\hat{\sigma}_{\kappa}\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r},{\mathbf{x}}\right)\right)^{2}+\kappa^{2}\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r=1}^{m}a_{r}{\mathbf{u}}_{r}\bm{\Phi}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r}\right\|_{2}}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad+\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r,r^{\prime}=1}^{m}a_{r}a_{r^{\prime}}\left(C_{r,r^{\prime}}+\frac{\kappa^{2}}{2\pi}E_{r,r^{\prime}}+\frac{\kappa}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(T_{1,r,r^{\prime}}+T_{2,r,r^{\prime}}\right)\right)

Combining the expression of
\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}}\left[f\left(\bm{\theta},{\mathbf{x}}\odot{\mathbf{c}}\right)^{2}\right]
and
\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}}\left[f\left(\bm{\theta},{\mathbf{x}}\odot{\mathbf{c}}\right)\right]
, and noticing
T_{1,r,r^{\prime}}=T_{2,r^{\prime},r}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}}\left[\left(f\left(\bm{\theta},{\mathbf{x}}\odot{\mathbf{c}}\right)-y\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r=1}^{m}a_{r}\hat{\sigma}_{\kappa}\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r},{\mathbf{x}}\right)\right)^{2}+\kappa^{2}\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r=1}^{m}a_{r}{\mathbf{u}}_{r}\bm{\Phi}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r}\right\|_{2}}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r,r^{\prime}=1}^{m}a_{r}a_{r^{\prime}}\left(C_{r,r^{\prime}}+\frac{\kappa^{2}}{2\pi}E_{r,r^{\prime}}+\frac{\kappa}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(T_{1,r,r^{\prime}}+T_{2,r,r^{\prime}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad-\frac{2y}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r=1}^{m}a_{r}\hat{\sigma}_{\kappa}\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r},{\mathbf{x}}\right)-\frac{2\kappa y}{\sqrt{2\pi m}}\sum_{r=1}^{m}a_{r}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r}\right\|_{2}\exp\left(-\frac{\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}\right)^{2}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r}\right\|_{2}^{2}}\right)+y^{2}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r=1}^{m}a_{r}\hat{\sigma}_{\kappa}\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r},{\mathbf{x}}\right)-y\right)^{2}+\frac{\kappa^{2}}{m}\left\|\sum_{r=1}^{m}a_{r}{\mathbf{u}}_{r}\bm{\Phi}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r}\right\|_{2}}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r,r^{\prime}=1}^{m}a_{r}a_{r^{\prime}}\left(C_{r,r^{\prime}}+\frac{\kappa^{2}}{2\pi}E_{r,r^{\prime}}+\frac{2\kappa}{\sqrt{2\pi}}T_{1,r,r^{\prime}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad-\frac{2\kappa y}{\sqrt{2\pi m}}\sum_{r=1}^{m}a_{r}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r}\right\|_{2}\exp\left(-\frac{\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}\right)^{2}}{2\kappa^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r}\right\|_{2}^{2}}\right)

To extend to the case of
{\mathbf{x}}_{i},y_{i}
, we need to re-define

\displaystyle C_{i,r,r^{\prime}}=\left(\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)+\kappa^{2}{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right)\mathcal{C}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}},\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}},\frac{{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle E_{i,r,r^{\prime}}=\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}\exp\left(-\frac{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)^{2}-2\left({\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right)\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)+\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)^{2}}{2\kappa^{2}\left(\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left({\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right)^{2}\right)}\right)

\displaystyle T_{i,r,r^{\prime}}={\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\cdot\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}^{2}\cdot{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}-{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\cdot{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}\left(\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left({\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right)^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right)

\displaystyle G_{i,r}=\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}\exp\left(-\frac{\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)^{2}}{2\kappa^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r}\right\|_{2}^{2}}\right)

Moreover, let
\hat{f}\left(\bm{\theta},{\mathbf{x}}\right)=\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r=1}^{m}a_{r}\hat{\sigma}_{\kappa}\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r},{\mathbf{x}}\right)
. Then we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{C}}}\left[{\mathcal{L}}_{{\mathbf{C}}}\left(\bm{\theta}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\hat{f}\left(\bm{\theta},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-y_{i}\right)_{2}^{2}+\frac{\kappa^{2}}{2m}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\|\sum_{r=1}^{m}a_{r}{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\bm{\Phi}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\frac{1}{m}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{r,r^{\prime}=1}^{m}a_{r}a_{r^{\prime}}\left(C_{i,r,r^{\prime}}+\frac{\kappa^{2}}{2\pi}E_{i,r,r^{\prime}}+\kappa\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}T_{i,r,r^{\prime}}G_{i,r}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad-\kappa y\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi m}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{r=1}^{m}a_{r}G_{i,r}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\hat{f}\left(\bm{\theta},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-y_{i}\right)_{2}^{2}+\frac{\kappa^{2}}{2m}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\|\sum_{r=1}^{m}a_{r}{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\bm{\Phi}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\frac{1}{m}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{r,r^{\prime}=1}^{m}a_{r}a_{r^{\prime}}\left(C_{i,r,r^{\prime}}+\frac{\kappa^{2}}{2\pi}E_{i,r,r^{\prime}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\frac{2\kappa}{\sqrt{2\pi m}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{r=1}^{m}a_{r}G_{i,r}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{m}a_{r}^{\prime}T_{i,r,r^{\prime}}-y\right)

∎

B.2
Proof of Theorem
4.2

Proof.

Let
{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}={\mathbf{w}}_{r}\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}
. By Lemma
B.1
, we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{C}}}\left[{\mathcal{L}}_{{\mathbf{C}}}\left(\bm{\theta}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\hat{f}\left(\bm{\theta},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-y_{i}\right)_{2}^{2}+\frac{\kappa^{2}}{2m}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\|\sum_{r=1}^{m}a_{r}{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\bm{\Phi}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\frac{1}{m}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{r,r^{\prime}=1}^{m}a_{r}a_{r^{\prime}}\left(C_{i,r,r^{\prime}}+\frac{\kappa^{2}}{2\pi}E_{i,r,r^{\prime}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\frac{2\kappa}{\sqrt{2\pi m}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{r=1}^{m}a_{r}G_{i,r}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{m}a_{r}^{\prime}T_{i,r,r^{\prime}}-y\right)

where
C_{i,r,r^{\prime}},E_{i,r,r^{\prime}},T_{i,r,r^{\prime}}
and
G_{i,r}
are defined as

\displaystyle C_{i,r,r^{\prime}}=\left(\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)+\kappa^{2}{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right)\mathcal{C}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}},\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}},\frac{{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle E_{i,r,r^{\prime}}=\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}\exp\left(-\frac{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)^{2}-2\left({\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right)\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)+\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)^{2}}{2\kappa^{2}\left(\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left({\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right)^{2}\right)}\right)

\displaystyle T_{i,r,r^{\prime}}={\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\cdot\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}^{2}\cdot{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}-{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\cdot{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}\left(\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left({\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right)^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right)

\displaystyle G_{i,r}=\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}\exp\left(-\frac{\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)^{2}}{2\kappa^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{r}\right\|_{2}^{2}}\right)

Therefore, the proof of the theorem relies on the upper bound of
C_{i,r,r^{\prime}},E_{i,r,r^{\prime}},T_{i,r,r^{\prime}}
and
G_{i,r}
. To upper-bound
C_{i,r,r^{\prime}}
, we utilize the result in

[
]

that

\left|C\left(a,b,\rho\right)\right|\leq\frac{\left|\arcsin\rho\right|}{2\pi}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}-2\rho ab+b^{2}}{2\left(1-\rho^{2}\right)}\right)\leq\frac{|\rho|}{4}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}+b^{2}}{4}\right)

where we used Lemma
D.19
that
|\arcsin x|\leq\frac{\pi}{2}\cdot|x|
. Plugging in
a=\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}},b=\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}}
and
\rho=\frac{{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}}
gives

\displaystyle\left|C_{i,r,r^{\prime}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)+\kappa^{2}{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right|\cdot\frac{\left|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right|}{4\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}}\exp\left(-\frac{1}{4\kappa^{2}}\left(\frac{\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)^{2}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}^{2}}+\frac{\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)^{2}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}^{2}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{4}\left(\left|\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)\right|+\kappa^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}\right)\phi\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{2\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|}_{2}\right)\phi\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{2\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\kappa^{2}}{4}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}\left(\frac{\left|{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right|}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}}\cdot\frac{\left|{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right|}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}}+1\right)\phi\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{2\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}}\right)\phi\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{2\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\kappa^{2}}{4}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}\left(\psi\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{2\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}}\right)\psi\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{2\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}}\right)+\phi\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{2\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}}\right)\phi\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{2\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}}\right)\right)

where we use the definition
P_{i,r}=\left|{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right|\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)^{2}}{4\kappa^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}^{2}}\right)
.
For the term
E_{i,r,r^{\prime}}
, we notice that by letting
a=\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}},b=\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}}
and
\rho=\frac{{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right\|}
, we have

\exp\left(-\frac{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)^{2}-2\left({\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right)\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)+\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)^{2}}{2\kappa^{2}\left(\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left({\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right)^{2}\right)}\right)=\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}-2\rho ab+b^{2}}{2\left(1-\rho^{2}\right)}\right)

Using
\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}-2\rho ab+b^{2}}{2\left(1-\rho^{2}\right)}\right)\leq\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}+b^{2}}{4}\right)
, we have that

\displaystyle\left|E_{i,r,r^{\prime}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}\exp\left(-\frac{1}{4\kappa^{2}}\left(\frac{\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)^{2}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}^{2}}+\frac{\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)^{2}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}^{2}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}\phi\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{2\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}}\right)\phi\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{2\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}}\right)

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\left|C_{i,r,r^{\prime}}+\frac{\kappa^{2}}{2\pi}E_{i,r,r^{\prime}}\right|

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\leq\frac{\kappa^{2}}{4}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}\left(\psi\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{2\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}}\right)\psi\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{2\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}}\right)+\phi\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{2\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}}\right)\phi\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{2\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}}\right)\right)

This gives that

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{r,r^{\prime}=1}^{m}a_{r}a_{r^{\prime}}\left(C_{i,r,r^{\prime}}+\frac{\kappa^{2}}{2\pi}E_{i,r,r^{\prime}}\right)\right|

(21)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\kappa^{2}}{4}\left(\left(\sum_{r=1}^{m}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}\psi\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{2\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}}\right)\right)^{2}+\left(\sum_{r=1}^{m}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}\phi\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{2\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}}\right)\right)^{2}\right)

By definition, we have
\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}\leq R_{{\mathbf{u}}}
. Therefore

\left|\sum_{r,r^{\prime}=1}^{m}a_{r}a_{r^{\prime}}\left(C_{i,r,r^{\prime}}+\frac{\kappa^{2}}{2\pi}E_{i,r,r^{\prime}}\right)\right|\leq\frac{1}{4}\kappa^{2}R_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{2}\left(\left(\sum_{r=1}^{m}\psi\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{2\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}}\right)\right)^{2}+\left(\sum_{r=1}^{m}\phi\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{2\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}}\right)\right)^{2}\right)

Next, we focus on the term
T_{i,r,r^{\prime}}
and
G_{i,r}
. By the property of CDF, we have that
\left|T_{i,r,r^{\prime}}\right|\leq\left|{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right|\leq\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{r}\right\|_{2}
. Therefore

\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{m}a_{r^{\prime}}T_{i,r,r^{\prime}}-y_{i}\right|\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{m}\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}\right\|_{2}+\left|y_{i}\right|\leq\sqrt{m}R_{{\mathbf{w}}}+B_{y}\leq 2\sqrt{m}R_{{\mathbf{w}}}

where we applied
\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{r}\right\|_{2}\leq R_{{\mathbf{w}}}
and
B_{y}\leq 3\sqrt{m}R_{{\mathbf{w}}}
. Thus

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{r=1}^{m}a_{r}G_{i,r}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{m}a_{r^{\prime}}T_{i,r,r^{\prime}}-y_{i}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{r=1}^{m}G_{i,r}\cdot 2\sqrt{m}R_{{\mathbf{w}}}\leq\sqrt{m}R_{{\mathbf{w}}}\sum_{r=1}^{m}\phi\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{2\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}}\right)

where we used
\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}\leq R_{{\mathbf{u}}}
.
Combining the inequality above and (
21
), we have

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{E}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{n\kappa^{2}R_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{2}}{4m}\left(\left(\sum_{r=1}^{m}\psi\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{2\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}}\right)\right)^{2}+\left(\sum_{r=1}^{m}\phi\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{2\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}}\right)\right)^{2}\right)+\frac{n\kappa R_{{\mathbf{w}}}}{2}\sum_{r=1}^{m}\phi\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{2\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{i,r}\right\|_{2}}\right)

Applying the definition of
\psi_{\max}
and
\phi_{\max}
gives the desired results.
∎

B.3
Proof of Theorem
4.9

Proof.

By the form of the gradient, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{C}}_{k}}\left[\nabla_{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}}{\mathcal{L}}_{{\mathbf{C}}}\left(\bm{\theta}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{a_{r}}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{C}}}\left[\left(f\left(\bm{\theta},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\odot{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\right)-y_{i}\right){\mathbf{x}}_{i}\odot{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\mathbb{I}\left\{\left\langle{\mathbf{w}}_{r},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\odot{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\right\rangle\geq 0\right\}\right]

(22)

\displaystyle=\frac{a_{r}}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}_{i}}\left[f\left(\bm{\theta},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\odot{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\right){\mathbf{x}}_{i}\odot{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\mathbb{I}\left\{\left\langle{\mathbf{w}}_{r},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\odot{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\right\rangle\geq 0\right\}\right]}_{\mathcal{T}_{1,i}}

\displaystyle-\frac{a_{r}}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}y_{i}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}_{i}}\left[{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\odot{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\mathbb{I}\left\{\left\langle{\mathbf{w}}_{r},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\odot{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\right\rangle\geq 0\right\}\right]}_{\mathcal{T}_{2,i}}

Let
{\mathbf{u}}_{r,i}={\mathbf{w}}_{r}\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}
. For
\mathcal{T}_{1,i}
, we further have

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{1,i}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{m}a_{r^{\prime}}\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}_{i}}\left[\sigma\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}\left({\mathbf{x}}_{i}\odot{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\right)\right){\mathbf{x}}_{i}\odot{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}\left({\mathbf{x}}_{i}\odot{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\right)\geq 0\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{m}a_{r^{\prime}}\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}_{i}}\left[\left({\mathbf{x}}_{i}\odot{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\right)\left({\mathbf{x}}_{i}\odot{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\right)^{\top}{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}\left({\mathbf{x}}_{i}\odot{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\right)\geq 0;{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}\left({\mathbf{x}}_{i}\odot{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\right)\geq 0\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{m}a_{r^{\prime}}\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}_{i}}\left[{\mathbf{c}}_{i}{\mathbf{c}}_{i}^{\top}\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}\left({\mathbf{x}}_{i}\odot{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\right)\geq 0;{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}\left({\mathbf{x}}_{i}\odot{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\right)\geq 0\right\}\right]\odot\left({\mathbf{x}}_{i}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}^{\top}\right)\right){\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{m}a_{r^{\prime}}\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}_{i}}\left[{\mathbf{c}}_{i}{\mathbf{c}}_{i}^{\top}\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{u}}_{r,i}^{\top}{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\geq 0;{\mathbf{u}}_{r^{\prime},i}^{\top}{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\geq 0\right\}\right]\odot\left({\mathbf{x}}_{i}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}^{\top}\right)\right){\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r=1}^{m}a_{r^{\prime}}\text{Diag}\left({\mathbf{x}}\right)_{i}\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}_{i}}\left[{\mathbf{c}}_{i}{\mathbf{c}}_{i}^{\top}\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{u}}_{r,i}^{\top}{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\geq 0;{\mathbf{u}}_{r^{\prime},i}^{\top}{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\geq 0\right\}\right]{\mathbf{u}}_{r^{\prime},i}

For
\mathcal{T}_{2,i}
, we can easily obtain

\mathcal{T}_{2,i}=\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}_{i}}\left[{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{u}}_{r,i}^{\top}{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\geq 0\right\}\right]\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}

Abstractly, we are thus interested in the following quantity:

\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}}\left[{\mathbf{c}}{\mathbf{c}}^{\top}\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{c}}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}\geq 0;{\mathbf{c}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\geq 0\right\}\right];\quad\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}}\left[{\mathbf{c}}\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{c}}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}\geq 0\right\}\right]

where
{\mathbf{c}}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(\bm{\mu},\kappa^{2}{\mathbf{I}}\right)
, and
{\mathbf{u}},{\mathbf{v}}
are fixed vectors. Let
z_{1}={\mathbf{c}}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}
and
z_{2}={\mathbf{c}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}
. Then we have

z_{1}\sim\mathcal{N}\left({\mathbf{c}}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}},\kappa^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right);\quad z_{2}\sim\mathcal{N}\left({\mathbf{c}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}},\kappa^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)

According to Lemma
D.11
and Lemma
D.12
, and by defining
\bm{\mu}=\bm{1}
, we have that
\bm{\Delta}^{(1)}_{k,r,i}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
and
\bm{\Delta}^{(2)}_{k,r,r^{\prime},i}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
defined below

\displaystyle\bm{\Delta}^{(1)}_{k,r,i}

\displaystyle:=\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}_{i}}\left[{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{c}}_{i}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}_{r,i}\geq 0\right\}\right]-\bm{1}\cdot\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{r}\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right\|_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\bm{\Delta}_{k,r,r^{\prime},i}

\displaystyle:=\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}}\left[{\mathbf{c}}{\mathbf{c}}^{\top}\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{u}}_{r,i}^{\top}{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\geq 0;{\mathbf{u}}_{r^{\prime},i}^{\top}{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\geq 0\right\}\right]{\mathbf{u}}_{r^{\prime},i}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad-\left(\bm{1}\bm{1}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}_{r^{\prime},i}+3\kappa^{2}{\mathbf{u}}_{r^{\prime},i}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{r}\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right\|_{2}}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right\|_{2}}\right)

satisfies

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{\Delta}^{(1)}_{r,i}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\kappa R_{{\mathbf{u}}}\phi_{\max}

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{\Delta}^{(2)}_{r,r^{\prime},i}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq 4\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}\left(\sqrt{d}\phi_{\max}+\psi_{\max}\right)

Here we used
\left\|\bm{\mu}\right\|_{\infty}=1
and
\bm{\mu}^{\top}\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r}\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)={\mathbf{w}}_{k,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}
when
\bm{\mu}=1
.
Therefore, for
\mathcal{T}_{1,i}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{1,i}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{m}a_{r^{\prime}}\text{Diag}\left({\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)\left(\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\cdot\bm{1}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}s\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right\|_{2}}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right\|_{2}}\right)+\bm{\Delta}_{r,r^{\prime},i}^{(2)}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\frac{3\kappa^{2}}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{m}a_{r^{\prime}}\text{Diag}\left({\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r}\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{r}\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right\|_{2}}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right\|_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{m}a_{r^{\prime}}{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\cdot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}s\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right\|_{2}}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right\|_{2}}\right)+\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{m}a_{r^{\prime}}\left({\mathbf{x}}_{i}\odot\bm{\Delta}_{r,r^{\prime},i}^{(2)}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\frac{3\kappa^{2}}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{m}a_{r^{\prime}}\text{Diag}\left({\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)^{2}{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}s\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right\|_{2}}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right\|_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=f\left(\bm{\theta},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right){\mathbf{x}}_{i}\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0\right\}+\frac{3\kappa^{2}}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{m}a_{r^{\prime}}\text{Diag}\left({\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)^{2}{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0;{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0\right\}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{m}a_{r^{\prime}}\left({\mathbf{x}}_{i}\odot\bm{\Delta}_{r,r^{\prime},i}^{(2)}\right)+{\mathbf{g}}_{1,i}+{\mathbf{g}}_{2,i}

where

\displaystyle{\mathbf{g}}_{1,i}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{m}a_{r^{\prime}}{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\cdot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\left(\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}s\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right\|_{2}}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right\|_{2}}\right)-\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0;{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0\right\}\right)

\displaystyle{\mathbf{g}}_{2,i}=\frac{3\kappa^{2}}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{m}a_{r^{\prime}}\text{Diag}\left({\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)^{2}{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}\left(\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}s\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right\|_{2}}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right\|_{2}}\right)-\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0;{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0\right\}\right)

Likely, for
\mathcal{T}_{2,i}
we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{2,i}

\displaystyle=\left(\bm{1}\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{r}\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right\|_{2}}\right)+\bm{\Delta}^{(1)}_{r,i}\right)\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}

\displaystyle={\mathbf{x}}_{i}\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{r}\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right\|_{2}}\right)+\bm{\Delta}^{(1)}_{r,i}\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}

\displaystyle={\mathbf{x}}_{i}\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0\right\}+\bm{\Delta}^{(1)}_{r,i}\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}+{\mathbf{g}}_{3,i}

where
{\mathbf{g}}_{3,i}={\mathbf{x}}_{i}\cdot\left(\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{r}\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right\|_{2}}\right)-\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0\right\}\right)
.
Therefore, the final gradient is given by

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{C}}}\left[\nabla_{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}}{\mathcal{L}}_{{\mathbf{C}}}\left(\bm{\theta}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{a_{r}}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(f\left(\bm{\theta},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-y_{i}\right){\mathbf{x}}_{i}\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\frac{a_{r}}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{m}a_{r^{\prime}}\left({\mathbf{x}}_{i}\odot\bm{\Delta}_{r,r^{\prime},i}^{(2)}\right)+y_{i}\bm{\Delta}^{(1)}_{r,i}\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\frac{3\kappa^{2}}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{m}a_{r^{\prime}}\text{Diag}\left({\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)^{2}{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0;{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0\right\}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\frac{a_{r}}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left({\mathbf{g}}_{1,i}+{\mathbf{g}}_{2,i}-y_{i}\cdot{\mathbf{g}}_{3,i}\right)

\displaystyle=\nabla_{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}}{\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}\right)+\frac{3\kappa^{2}}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{m}a_{r^{\prime}}\text{Diag}\left({\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)^{2}{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0;{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0\right\}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\underbrace{\frac{a_{r}}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{m}a_{r^{\prime}}\left({\mathbf{x}}_{i}\odot\bm{\Delta}_{r,r^{\prime},i}^{(2)}\right)+y_{i}\bm{\Delta}^{(1)}_{r,i}\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)}_{{\mathbf{g}}_{4}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\frac{a_{r}}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left({\mathbf{g}}_{1,i}+{\mathbf{g}}_{2,i}-y_{i}\cdot{\mathbf{g}}_{3,i}\right)

Notice that we can re-write
{\mathbf{g}}_{1,i}
as

\displaystyle{\mathbf{g}}_{1,i}

\displaystyle={\mathbf{x}}_{i}\left(\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{r}\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right\|_{2}}\right)-\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0\right\}\right)\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{m}a_{r^{\prime}}{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\mathbb{I}\left\{\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{r}\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right\|_{2}}\right)\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{m}a_{r^{\prime}}{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\left(\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right\|_{2}}\right)-\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0\right\}\right)

\displaystyle={\mathbf{x}}_{i}\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{r}\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right\|_{2}}\right)\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{m}a_{r^{\prime}}{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\left(\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right\|_{2}}\right)-\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\left(\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{r}\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right\|_{2}}\right)-\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0\right\}\right)\cdot f\left(\bm{\theta},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)

Then, by the definition of
{\mathbf{g}}_{3,i}
, we have that

\displaystyle{\mathbf{g}}_{1,i}-y_{i}\cdot{\mathbf{g}}_{3,i}

\displaystyle={\mathbf{x}}_{i}\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{r}\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right\|_{2}}\right)\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{m}a_{r^{\prime}}{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\left(\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right\|_{2}}\right)-\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\left(f\left(\bm{\theta},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-y_{i}\right){\mathbf{x}}_{i}\left(\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{r}\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right\|_{2}}\right)-\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0\right\}\right)

Using Lemma
D.4
, we have that

\left|\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(a\right)-\mathbb{I}\left\{a\geq 0\right\}\right|\leq\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}}{2}\right)\leq\phi\left(\frac{a}{2}\right)

Therefore, we have that

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left({\mathbf{g}}_{1,i}-y_{i}\cdot{\mathbf{g}}_{3,i}\right)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{n}{\sqrt{m}}\left\|\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{m}a_{r^{\prime}}{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\left(\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right\|_{2}}\right)-\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0\right\}\right)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(f\left(\bm{\theta},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-y_{i}\right)\cdot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\left(\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{r}\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right\|_{2}}\right)-\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0\right\}\right)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{n}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{m}\left|{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right|\phi\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{2\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right\|_{2}}\right)+\left\|\text{Diag}\left(\bm{\Delta}\right){\mathbf{X}}\left(f\left(\bm{\theta}-{\mathbf{y}}\right)\right)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\kappa\sqrt{m}R_{{\mathbf{u}}}\psi_{\max}+\sigma_{\max}\left({\mathbf{X}}\right)\phi_{\max}{\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

Moreover, we can bound
{\mathbf{g}}_{2,i}
as

\displaystyle\left\|{\mathbf{g}}_{2,i}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{3\kappa^{2}}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{m}\left\|{\mathbf{x}}\right\|_{\infty}^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{r}\right\|\cdot 2\phi_{\max}\leq 6\kappa^{2}\sqrt{m}B_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}R_{{\mathbf{w}}}\phi_{\max}

Lastly, we can bound
{\mathbf{g}}_{3}
as

\displaystyle\left\|{\mathbf{g}}_{3}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{m}\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\odot\bm{\Delta}_{r,r^{\prime},i}^{(2)}\right\|+\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|y_{i}\right|\left\|\bm{\Delta}^{(1)}_{r,i}\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{m}\left\|\bm{\Delta}_{r,r^{\prime},i}^{(2)}\right\|_{\infty}\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right\|_{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|y_{i}\right|\left\|\bm{\Delta}_{r,r^{\prime},i}^{(1)}\right\|_{\infty}\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{m}\left\|\bm{\Delta}_{r,r^{\prime},i}^{(2)}\right\|_{\infty}+\frac{B_{y}}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\|\bm{\Delta}_{r,r^{\prime},i}^{(1)}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq 4n\kappa R_{{\mathbf{u}}}\left(\sqrt{d}\phi_{\max}+\psi_{\max}\right)+\frac{B_{y}}{\sqrt{m}}\cdot n\kappa R_{{\mathbf{u}}}\phi_{\max}

\displaystyle\leq 5n\kappa R_{{\mathbf{u}}}\left(\sqrt{d}\phi_{\max}+\psi_{\max}\right)

when
B_{y}\leq\sqrt{md}
. Therefore, we have that

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{C}}}\left[\nabla_{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}}{\mathcal{L}}_{{\mathbf{C}}}\left(\bm{\theta}\right)\right]-\left(\nabla_{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}}{\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}\right)+\frac{3\kappa^{2}}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{m}a_{r^{\prime}}\text{Diag}\left({\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)^{2}{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0;{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0\right\}\right)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\leq n\kappa R_{{\mathbf{u}}}\psi_{\max}+\frac{\sigma_{\max}\left({\mathbf{X}}\right)\phi_{\max}}{\sqrt{m}}{\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+6n\kappa^{2}B_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}R_{{\mathbf{w}}}\phi_{\max}+5n\kappa R_{{\mathbf{u}}}\left(\sqrt{d}\phi_{\max}+\psi_{\max}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\leq\left(\frac{\sigma_{\max}\left({\mathbf{X}}\right)}{\sqrt{m}}{\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+6n\kappa^{2}B_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}R_{{\mathbf{w}}}+5n\kappa R_{{\mathbf{u}}}\sqrt{d}\right)\phi_{\max}+6n\kappa R_{{\mathbf{u}}}\psi_{\max}

∎

Appendix C
Proofs in Section
5

C.1
Proof of Theorem
5.2

Proof.

To start the proof, we define the following quantity in the standard NTK-based analysis of two-layer ReLU neural network. Let
R=C_{1}\cdot\frac{\tau\lambda_{0}}{n}
for some
C_{1}>0
, we define event
A_{i,r}
and set
S_{i},S_{i}^{\perp}
as

\displaystyle A_{i,r}=\left\{\exists{\mathbf{w}}\in\mathcal{B}\left({\mathbf{w}}_{0,r},R\right):\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}_{0,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0\right\}\neq\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0\right\}\right\}

(23)

\displaystyle S_{i}=\left\{r\in[m]:\neg A_{i,r}\right\};\quad S_{i}^{\perp}=[m]\setminus S_{i}

(24)

Lemma 16 from

[
]

shows that with probability at least
1-n\exp\left(-\frac{mR}{\tau}\right)
, we have that
\left|S_{i}^{\perp}\right|\leq\frac{4mR}{\tau}
. In the following of the proof, we assume that such event holds.
Define
K^{\prime}=\min\left\{k\in\mathbb{N}:\exists r\in[m]\text{ s.t. }\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{k,r}-{\mathbf{w}}_{0,r}\right\|_{2}>R\right\}
. Then for all
k<K^{\prime}
, we have that
{\mathbf{w}}_{k,r}\in\mathcal{B}\left({\mathbf{w}}_{0,r},R\right)
. Fix any
k<K^{\prime}-1
. Consider the expansion of
{\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k+1}\right)
as the following

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k+1}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(f\left(\bm{\theta}_{k+1},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-y_{i}\right)^{2}

(25)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\left(f\left(\bm{\theta}_{k+1},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-f\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)\right)+\left(f\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-y_{i}\right)\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(f\left(\bm{\theta}_{k+1},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-f\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)\right)^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(f\left(\bm{\theta}_{k+1},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-f\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)\right)\left(f\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-y_{i}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(f\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-y_{i}\right)^{2}

We will analyze the three terms separately. To start, notice that

\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(f\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-y_{i}\right)^{2}={\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k}\right)

(26)

For the first term, by the definition of
f\left(\bm{\theta},{\mathbf{x}}\right)
, we have

\displaystyle\left|f\left(\bm{\theta}_{k+1},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-f\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r=1}^{m}a_{r}\left(\sigma\left({\mathbf{w}}_{k+1,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-\sigma\left({\mathbf{w}}_{k,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r=1}^{m}\left|\sigma\left({\mathbf{w}}_{k+1,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-\sigma\left({\mathbf{w}}_{k,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r=1}^{m}\left|\left({\mathbf{w}}_{k+1}-{\mathbf{w}}_{k}\right)^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r=1}^{m}\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{k+1}-{\mathbf{w}}_{k}\right\|

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r=1}^{m}\left\|\nabla_{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}}\hat{{\mathcal{L}}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},\bm{\xi}_{k}\right)\right\|_{2}

where in the first inequality we use the fact that
a=\pm 1
, and in the second inequality we use the
1
-Lipschitzness of ReLU. Applying Assumption
5.1
, we have that

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(f\left(\bm{\theta}_{k+1},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-f\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\eta^{2}}{m}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\sum_{r=1}^{m}\left\|\nabla_{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}}\hat{{\mathcal{L}}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},\bm{\xi}_{k}\right)\right\|_{2}\right)

(27)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\eta^{2}n}{m}\cdot\left(m\cdot\sqrt{\gamma\hat{{\mathcal{L}}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},\bm{\xi}_{k}\right)}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\eta^{2}mn\gamma\hat{{\mathcal{L}}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},\bm{\xi}_{k}\right)

Lastly, to analyze the second term, we use the following definition of
I_{i,k}
and
I_{i,k}^{\perp}

\displaystyle I_{i,k}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r\in S_{i}}a_{r}\sigma\left({\mathbf{w}}_{k,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right);\quad I_{i,k}^{\perp}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r\in S_{i}^{\perp}}a_{r}\sigma\left({\mathbf{w}}_{k,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)

Then we have that
f\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)=I_{i,k}+I_{i,k}^{\perp}
. Therefore

f\left(\bm{\theta}_{k+1},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-f\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)=\left(I_{i,k+1}-I_{i,k}\right)+\left(I_{i,k+1}^{\perp}-I_{i,k}^{\perp}\right)

By the
1
-Lipschitzness of ReLU, we have that

\displaystyle\left|I_{i,k+1}^{\perp}-I_{i,k}^{\perp}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r\in S_{i}^{\perp}}a_{r}\left(\sigma\left({\mathbf{w}}_{k+1,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-\sigma\left({\mathbf{w}}_{k,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r\in S_{i}^{\perp}}\left|\sigma\left({\mathbf{w}}_{k+1,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-\sigma\left({\mathbf{w}}_{k,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r\in S_{i}^{\perp}}\left|\left({\mathbf{w}}_{k+1,r}-{\mathbf{w}}_{k,r}\right)^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\eta}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r\in S_{i}^{\perp}}\left\|\nabla_{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}}\hat{{\mathcal{L}}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},\bm{\xi}_{k}\right)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\eta\sqrt{\gamma}}{\sqrt{m}}\left|S_{i}^{\perp}\right|\hat{{\mathcal{L}}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},\bm{\xi}_{k}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

Applying
\left|S_{i}^{\perp}\right|\leq\frac{4mR}{\tau}
gives
\left|I_{i,k+1}^{\perp}-I_{i,k}^{\perp}\right|\leq\frac{4\eta R}{\tau}\sqrt{\gamma m}\hat{{\mathcal{L}}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},\bm{\xi}_{k}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}
.This gives that

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(f\left(\bm{\theta}_{k+1},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-f\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)\right)\left(f\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-y_{i}\right)

(28)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(I_{i,k+1}-I_{i,k}\right)\left(f\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-y_{i}\right)+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(I_{i,k+1}^{\perp}-I_{i,k}^{\perp}\right)\left(f\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-y_{i}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(I_{i,k+1}-I_{i,k}\right)\left(f\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-y_{i}\right)+\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(I_{i,k+1}^{\perp}-I_{i,k}^{\perp}\right)^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(f\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-y_{i}\right)^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(I_{i,k+1}-I_{i,k}\right)\left(f\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-y_{i}\right)+\frac{4\eta R}{\tau}\sqrt{\gamma mn}\hat{{\mathcal{L}}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},\bm{\xi}_{k}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

Plugging (
26
), (
27
), and (
28
) into (
25
) gives

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k+1}\right)

\displaystyle\leq{\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k}\right)+\eta^{2}mn\gamma\hat{{\mathcal{L}}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},\bm{\xi}_{k}\right)+\frac{4\eta R}{\tau}\sqrt{\gamma mn}\hat{{\mathcal{L}}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},\bm{\xi}_{k}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(I_{i,k+1}-I_{i,k}\right)\left(f\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-y_{i}\right)

Under Jensen’s inequality, we have that
\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\xi}_{k}}\left[\hat{{\mathcal{L}}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},\bm{\xi}_{k}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right]\leq\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\xi}_{k}}\left[\hat{{\mathcal{L}}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},\bm{\xi}_{k}\right)\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}
. Using the property that

\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\xi}_{k}}\left[\hat{{\mathcal{L}}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},\bm{\xi}_{k}\right)\right]\leq 2{\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k}\right)+\varepsilon_{1}

from Assumption
5.1
, we can also obtain that

\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\xi}_{k}}\left[\hat{{\mathcal{L}}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},\bm{\xi}_{k}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right]\leq\left(2{\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k}\right)+\varepsilon_{1}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

Therefore, taking the expectation of
{\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k+1}\right)
gives

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\xi}_{k}}\left[{\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k+1}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq{\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k}\right)+\eta^{2}mn\gamma\left(2{\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k}\right)+\varepsilon\right)+\frac{4\eta R}{\tau}\sqrt{\gamma mn}\left(2{\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k}\right)+\varepsilon_{1}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

(29)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\xi}_{k}}\left[I_{i,k+1}-I_{i,k}\right]\left(f\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-y_{i}\right)

\displaystyle\leq{\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k}\right)+\eta^{2}mn\gamma\left(2{\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k}\right)+\varepsilon_{1}\right)+\frac{10\eta R}{\tau}\sqrt{\gamma mn}{\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k}\right)+\frac{4\eta R}{\tau}\sqrt{\gamma mn}\cdot\varepsilon_{1}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\xi}_{k}}\left[I_{i,k+1}-I_{i,k}\right]\left(f\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-y_{i}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(1+2\eta^{2}mn\gamma+10C\eta\lambda_{0}\sqrt{\frac{\gamma m}{n}}\right){\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k}\right)+\left(\eta^{2}mn\gamma+4C\eta\lambda_{0}\sqrt{\frac{\gamma m}{n}}\right)\varepsilon_{1}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\xi}_{k}}\left[I_{i,k+1}-I_{i,k}\right]\left(f\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-y_{i}\right)

where in the last inequality we use the property that
\sqrt{a(a+b)}\leq\frac{5}{4}a+b
.
Recall that
{\mathbf{w}}_{k+1,r},{\mathbf{w}}_{k,r}\in\mathcal{B}\left({\mathbf{w}}_{0,r},R\right)
. Therefore, for
r\in S_{i}
, we must have that
\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}_{k+1,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0\right\}=\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}_{0,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0\right\}=\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}_{k,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0\right\}
. Thus, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\xi}_{k}}\left[I_{i,k+1}-I_{i,k}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r\in S_{i}}a_{r}\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\xi}_{k}}\left[{\mathbf{w}}_{k+1,r}-{\mathbf{w}}_{k,r}\right]^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}_{k,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0\right\}

(30)

\displaystyle=-\frac{\eta}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r\in S_{i}}a_{r}\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\xi}_{k}}\left[\nabla_{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}}\hat{{\mathcal{L}}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},\bm{\xi}_{k}\right)\right]^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}_{k,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0\right\}

Let
{\mathbf{g}}_{k,r}=\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\xi}_{k}}\left[\nabla_{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}}\hat{{\mathcal{L}}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},\bm{\xi}_{k}\right)\right]-\nabla_{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}}{\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k}\right)
. Then by Assumption
5.1
we have that
\left\|{\mathbf{g}}_{k,r}\right\|_{2}\leq\varepsilon_{3}{\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\varepsilon_{2}
. Using
{\mathbf{g}}_{k,r}
, we can write (
30
) as

\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\xi}_{k}}\left[I_{i,k+1}-I_{i,k}\right]=-\eta\sum_{r\in S_{i}}\frac{a_{r}}{\sqrt{m}}\nabla_{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}}{\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k}\right)^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}_{k,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0\right\}-\frac{\eta}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r\in S_{i}}a_{r}{\mathbf{g}}_{k,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}_{k,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0\right\}

(31)

By definition, we have

\nabla_{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}}{\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k}\right)=\frac{a_{r}}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(f\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-y_{i}\right){\mathbf{x}}_{j}\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}_{k,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{j}\geq 0\right\}

Therefore, we have that

\frac{a_{r}}{\sqrt{m}}\nabla_{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}}{\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k}\right)^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}_{k,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0\right\}=\frac{1}{m}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(f\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-y_{i}\right){\mathbf{x}}_{i}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{j}\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}_{k,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0{\mathbf{w}}_{k,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{j}\geq 0\right\}

Combining with (
31
), we have that

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\xi}_{k}}\left[I_{i,k+1}-I_{i,k}\right]\left(f\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-y_{i}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad=-\frac{\eta}{m}\sum_{i,j=1}^{n}\sum_{r\in S_{i}}\left(f\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-y_{i}\right)\left(f\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},{\mathbf{x}}_{j}\right)-y_{j}\right){\mathbf{x}}_{i}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{j}\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}_{k,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0;{\mathbf{w}}_{k,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{j}\geq 0\right\}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad-\frac{\eta}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{r\in S_{i}}\left(f\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-y_{i}\right)a_{r}{\mathbf{g}}_{k,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}_{k,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0\right\}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\leq-\eta\sum_{i,j=1}^{n}\left(f\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-y_{i}\right)\underbrace{\left(\frac{{\mathbf{x}}_{i}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{j}}{m}\sum_{r\in S_{i}}\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}_{k,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0;{\mathbf{w}}_{k,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{j}\geq 0\right\}\right)}_{{\mathbf{H}}_{k,ij}}\left(f\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},{\mathbf{x}}_{j}\right)-y_{j}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\frac{\eta}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\sum_{r=1}^{n}\left|f\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-y_{i}\right|\left\|{\mathbf{g}}_{k,r}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\leq-\eta\lambda_{\min}\left({\mathbf{H}}_{k}\right)\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(f\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-y_{i}\right)^{2}+\eta\varepsilon_{2}\sqrt{mn}\left(\sum_{r=1}^{n}\left(f\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-y_{i}\right)^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\eta\varepsilon_{3}\sqrt{mn}{\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k}\right)

\displaystyle=-\left(2\eta\lambda_{\min}\left({\mathbf{H}}_{k}\right)+\eta\varepsilon_{3}\sqrt{mn}\right){\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k}\right)+2\eta\varepsilon_{2}\sqrt{mn}{\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

Using the property that
ab\leq\frac{a^{2}}{2}+\frac{b^{2}}{2}
, we have that for any
C^{\prime}>0
,

2\eta\varepsilon_{2}\sqrt{mn}{\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq C^{\prime}\eta\lambda_{0}\sqrt{\frac{\gamma m}{n}}+\frac{\eta\varepsilon_{2}^{2}n}{C^{\prime}\lambda_{0}}\sqrt{\frac{mn}{\gamma}}

Moreover, by Lemma
C.1
, we have that when
m=\Omega\left(\frac{n^{2}}{\lambda_{0}^{2}}\log\frac{n}{\delta}\right)
, with probability at least
1-\delta-n^{2}\exp{-\frac{mR}{\tau}}
, it holds that

\left\|{\mathbf{H}}_{k}-{\mathbf{H}}^{\infty}\right\|_{F}\leq\frac{\lambda_{0}}{6}+\frac{1}{m}\left(\sum_{i,j=1}^{n}\left|S_{i}^{\perp}\right|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\frac{2nR}{\tau}

Plugging in
\left|S_{i}^{\perp}\right|\leq\frac{4mR}{\tau}
and
R\leq C_{1}\cdot\frac{\tau\lambda_{0}}{n}
, we have that
\left\|{\mathbf{H}}_{k}-{\mathbf{H}}^{\infty}\right\|_{F}\leq\frac{\lambda_{0}}{2}
for small enough
C_{1}
. Thus, we have that
\lambda_{\min}\left({\mathbf{H}}_{k}\right)\geq\frac{\lambda_{0}}{2}
.
Therefore, we have that

\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\xi}_{k}}\left[I_{i,k+1}-I_{i,k}\right]\left(f\left(\bm{\theta}_{k},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-y_{i}\right)\leq\left(C^{\prime}\eta\lambda_{0}\sqrt{\frac{\gamma m}{n}}+\eta\varepsilon_{3}\sqrt{mn}-\eta\lambda_{0}\right){\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k}\right)+\frac{\eta\varepsilon_{2}^{2}n}{C^{\prime}\lambda_{0}}\sqrt{\frac{mn}{\gamma}}

Plugging this back into (
31
) gives

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\xi}_{k}}\left[{\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k+1}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+2\eta^{2}mn\gamma+10C\eta\lambda_{0}\sqrt{\frac{\gamma m}{n}}\right){\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k}\right)+\left(\eta^{2}mn\gamma+4C\eta\lambda_{0}\sqrt{\frac{\gamma m}{n}}\right)\varepsilon_{1}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\left(C^{\prime}\eta\lambda_{0}\sqrt{\frac{\gamma m}{n}}+\eta\varepsilon_{3}\sqrt{mn}-\eta\lambda_{0}\right){\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k}\right)+\frac{\eta\varepsilon_{2}^{2}n}{C^{\prime}\lambda_{0}}\sqrt{\frac{mn}{\gamma}}

\displaystyle=\left(1-\eta\lambda_{0}+\eta\varepsilon_{3}\sqrt{mn}+2\eta^{2}mn\gamma+\left(10C+C^{\prime}\right)\eta\lambda_{0}\sqrt{\frac{\gamma m}{n}}\right){\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\left(\eta^{2}mn\gamma+4C\eta\lambda_{0}\sqrt{\frac{\gamma m}{n}}\right)\varepsilon_{1}+\frac{\eta\varepsilon_{2}^{2}n}{C^{\prime}\lambda_{0}}\sqrt{\frac{mn}{\gamma}}

Apply
\varepsilon_{3}\leq C_{\varepsilon}\cdot\frac{\lambda_{0}}{\sqrt{mn}},\gamma=C_{1}\cdot\frac{n}{m}
and
\eta=C_{2}\cdot\frac{\lambda_{0}}{n^{2}}
gives

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\xi}_{k}}\left[{\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k+1}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\left(1-2C_{1}C_{2}-\left(10C+C^{\prime}\right)\sqrt{C_{1}}\right)\eta\lambda_{0}\right){\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\eta\lambda_{0}\left(\frac{mn\varepsilon_{2}^{2}}{C^{\prime}\sqrt{C_{1}}\lambda_{0}^{2}}+\left(C_{1}C_{2}+4C\sqrt{C_{1}}\right)\varepsilon_{1}\right)

Choosing a small enough
C_{1},C_{2},C,C^{\prime}
gives

\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\xi}_{k}}\left[{\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k+1}\right)\right]\leq\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{2}\right){\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}_{k}\right)+\frac{1}{2}\hat{C}\eta\lambda_{0}\left(\frac{mn}{\lambda_{0}^{2}}\cdot\varepsilon_{2}^{2}+\varepsilon_{1}\right)

(32)

for a large enough
\hat{C}
. Thus, unrolling the iterations gives

\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\xi}_{0},\dots,\bm{\xi}_{k-1}}\left[{\mathcal{L}}\left({\mathbf{W}}_{k}\right)\right]\leq\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{2}\right)^{k}{\mathcal{L}}\left({\mathbf{W}}_{0}\right)+\hat{C}\left(\frac{mn}{\lambda_{0}^{2}}\cdot\varepsilon_{2}^{2}+\varepsilon_{1}\right)

(33)

for all
k<K^{\prime}
. Next, we shall lower bound
K^{\prime}
. For all
k\leq K^{\prime}
, we have that

\displaystyle\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{k,r}-{\mathbf{w}}_{0,r}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=0}^{k-1}\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{t+1,r}-{\mathbf{w}}_{t,r}\right\|_{2}=\eta\sum_{t=0}^{k-1}\left\|\nabla_{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}}\hat{{\mathcal{L}}}\left({\mathbf{W}}_{t},\bm{\xi}_{t}\right)\right\|_{2}\leq\eta\sqrt{\gamma}\sum_{t=0}^{k-1}\hat{{\mathcal{L}}}\left({\mathbf{W}}_{t},\bm{\xi}_{t}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

By (
33
), we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\xi}_{0},\dots,\bm{\xi}_{t-1}}\left[\hat{{\mathcal{L}}}\left({\mathbf{W}}_{t},\bm{\xi}_{t}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left({\mathcal{L}}\left({\mathbf{W}}_{t}\right)+\varepsilon_{1}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(2\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{2}\right)^{t}{\mathcal{L}}\left({\mathbf{W}}_{0}\right)+\left(\hat{C}+1\right)\left(\frac{mn}{\lambda_{0}^{2}}\cdot\varepsilon_{2}^{2}+\varepsilon_{1}\right)\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4}\right)^{t}{\mathcal{L}}\left({\mathbf{W}}_{0}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\sqrt{\hat{C}+1}\left(\frac{\varepsilon_{2}}{\lambda_{0}}\sqrt{mn}+\sqrt{\varepsilon_{1}}\right)

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\xi}_{0},\dots,\bm{\xi}_{k-1}}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{k,r}-{\mathbf{w}}_{0,r}\right\|_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\eta\sqrt{\gamma}\sum_{t=0}^{k-1}\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\xi}_{0},\dots,\bm{\xi}_{t-1}}\left[\hat{{\mathcal{L}}}\left({\mathbf{W}}_{t},\bm{\xi}_{t}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2\eta\sqrt{\gamma}{\mathcal{L}}\left({\mathbf{W}}_{0}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4}\right)^{t}+k\sqrt{\left(\hat{C}+1\right)\gamma}\left(\frac{\varepsilon_{2}}{\lambda_{0}}\sqrt{mn}+\sqrt{\varepsilon_{1}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{8\sqrt{\gamma}}{\lambda_{0}}{\mathcal{L}}\left({\mathbf{W}}_{0}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+k\sqrt{\left(\hat{C}+1\right)\gamma}\left(\frac{\varepsilon_{2}}{\lambda_{0}}\sqrt{mn}+\sqrt{\varepsilon_{1}}\right)

By Lemma 26 in

[
]

, we have that
\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{W}}_{0},{\mathbf{a}}}\left[{\mathcal{L}}\left({\mathbf{W}}_{0}\right)^{2}\right]=O\left(n\right)
.
\gamma=C_{1}\cdot\frac{n}{m}
, we have that

\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{W}}_{0},{\mathbf{a}},\bm{\xi}_{0},\dots,\bm{\xi}_{k-1}}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{k,r}-{\mathbf{w}}_{0,r}\right\|_{2}\right]\leq O\left(\frac{n}{\lambda_{0}}\sqrt{m}\right)+O\left(k\left(\frac{\varepsilon_{2}n}{\lambda_{0}}+\sqrt{\frac{\varepsilon_{1}n}{m}}\right)\right)

Thus, by Markov’s inequality, we have that with probability at least
1-\frac{\delta}{3K}
,

\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{k,r}-{\mathbf{w}}_{0,r}\right\|_{2}\leq\underbrace{O\left(\frac{nK}{\lambda_{0}\delta\sqrt{m}}\right)}_{\mathcal{T}_{1}}+\underbrace{O\left(\frac{K^{2}}{\delta}\left(\frac{\varepsilon_{2}n}{\lambda_{0}}+\sqrt{\frac{\varepsilon_{1}n}{m}}\right)\right)}_{\mathcal{T}_{2}}

Setting
m=\Omega\left(\frac{n^{4}}{\lambda_{0}^{4}\delta^{2}\tau^{2}}\right)
guarantees that
T_{1}\leq\frac{C_{1}}{2}\cdot\frac{\tau\lambda_{0}}{n}=\frac{R}{2}
and set
\varepsilon_{2}\leq O\left(\frac{\delta\lambda_{0}}{nK^{2}}\right),\varepsilon_{1}\leq O\left(\frac{\delta m}{K^{4}n}\right)
gives that
T_{2}\leq\frac{C_{1}}{2}\cdot\frac{\tau\lambda_{0}}{n}=\frac{R}{2}
. Combining the bound on
\mathcal{T}_{1}
and
\mathcal{T}_{2}
and taking a union bound gives that, with probability at least
1-\frac{\delta}{3}
, it holds that

\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{k,r}-{\mathbf{w}}_{0,r}\right\|_{2}\leq R;\quad\forall k\in[K]

This shows that we must have
K^{\prime}>K
, which completes the proof.
∎

Lemma C.1
.

Let
{\mathbf{H}}^{\infty}
be defined in (
11
), and let
{\mathbf{H}}_{k}
be defined as

{\mathbf{H}}_{k,ij}=\frac{{\mathbf{x}}_{i}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{j}}{m}\sum_{r\in S_{i}}\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}_{k,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0;{\mathbf{w}}_{k,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{j}\geq 0\right\}

Fix any
R
. Assume that
{\mathbf{w}}_{k,r}\in\mathcal{B}\left({\mathbf{w}}_{0,r},R\right)
for all
r\in[m]
. If
{\mathbf{w}}_{0,r}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(\bm{0},\tau^{2}{\mathbf{I}}\right)
, and
m=\Omega\left(\right)
, then we have that

\left\|{\mathbf{H}}_{k}-{\mathbf{H}}^{\infty}\right\|_{F}\leq\frac{\lambda_{0}}{6}+\frac{1}{m^{2}}\sum_{i,j=1}^{n}\left|S_{i}^{\perp}\right|^{2}+\frac{2nR}{\tau}

Proof.

We define
\hat{{\mathbf{H}}}_{k}
as follows

\hat{{\mathbf{H}}}_{k,ij}=\frac{{\mathbf{x}}_{i}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{j}}{m}\sum_{r=1}^{m}\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}_{k,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0;{\mathbf{w}}_{k,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{j}\geq 0\right\}

Then we have that

\left\|{\mathbf{H}}_{k}-{\mathbf{H}}^{\infty}\right\|_{F}\leq\left\|{\mathbf{H}}_{k}-\hat{{\mathbf{H}}}_{k}\right\|_{F}+\left\|\hat{{\mathbf{H}}}_{k}-\hat{{\mathbf{H}}}_{0}\right\|_{F}+\left\|\hat{{\mathbf{H}}}_{0}-{\mathbf{H}}^{\infty}\right\|_{F}

By Lemma 3.1 in
Du et al. 2018
, we have that with probability at least
1-\delta
, we have that
\left\|\hat{{\mathbf{H}}}_{0}-{\mathbf{H}}^{\infty}\right\|_{F}\leq\frac{\lambda_{0}}{6}
when
m=\Omega\left(\frac{n^{2}}{\lambda_{0}^{2}}\log\frac{n}{\delta}\right)
. By Lemma 3.2 in
Song and
Yang 2020
, we have that with probability at least
1-n^{2}\exp{-\frac{mR}{\tau}}
, it holds that
\left\|\hat{{\mathbf{H}}}_{k}-\hat{{\mathbf{H}}}_{0}\right\|\leq\frac{2nR}{\tau}
. Lastly, for the first term, we have

\displaystyle\left\|{\mathbf{H}}_{k}-\hat{{\mathbf{H}}}_{k}\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i,j=1}^{n}\left({\mathbf{H}}_{k,ij}-\hat{{\mathbf{H}}}_{k,ij}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{m^{2}}\sum_{i,j=1}^{n}\left({\mathbf{x}}_{i}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{j}\sum_{r\in S_{i}^{\perp}}\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}_{k,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0;{\mathbf{w}}_{k,r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{j}\geq 0\right\}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{m^{2}}\sum_{i,j=1}^{n}\left|S_{i}^{\perp}\right|^{2}

Combining the three bounds gives the desired result.
∎

C.2
Proof of Theorem
5.6

We view Gaussian input masking as a special case of the general stochastic
training framework in Section
5
. Recall that in that
framework, the randomness at iteration
k
is denoted by
\bm{\xi}_{k}
, and the
update rule is

{\mathbf{W}}_{k+1}={\mathbf{W}}_{k}-\eta\nabla_{{\mathbf{W}}}\hat{{\mathcal{L}}}({\mathbf{W}}_{k},\bm{\xi}_{k}).

In the Gaussian-masked setting we take

\bm{\xi}_{k}\equiv{\mathbf{C}}_{k},\qquad\hat{{\mathcal{L}}}({\mathbf{W}},\bm{\xi}_{k})\equiv{\mathcal{L}}_{{\mathbf{C}}_{k}}({\mathbf{W}}),

where
{\mathbf{C}}_{k}
is the multiplicative Gaussian mask at iteration
k
and

{\mathcal{L}}_{{\mathbf{C}}_{k}}
is the masked loss. Thus

\nabla_{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}}\hat{{\mathcal{L}}}({\mathbf{W}},\bm{\xi}_{k})\equiv\nabla_{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}}{\mathcal{L}}_{{\mathbf{C}}_{k}}({\mathbf{W}}),

and the update (
7
) coincides with the masked gradient
descent rule (
1
). Therefore, to apply
Theorem
5.2
to training with Gaussian input masks, it
suffices to verify that Assumption
5.1
holds with suitable

\varepsilon_{1},\varepsilon_{2},\varepsilon_{3},\gamma
, and that these parameters
satisfy the smallness conditions of Theorem
5.2
under the
constraints (
14
)–(
15
).

Throughout the proof we condition on the high-probability NTK event of
Theorem
5.2
, on which

•

the minimum eigenvalue of the empirical NTK satisfies

\lambda_{\min}({\mathbf{H}}_{k})\geq\lambda_{0}/2
for all
k\in[K]
,

•

all first-layer weights remain in a ball of radius

R=C_{1}\tau\lambda_{0}/n
around their initialization, i.e.,

\|{\mathbf{w}}_{k,r}-{\mathbf{w}}_{0,r}\|_{2}\leq R
for all
k\in[K],r\in[m]
,

•

the data are bounded as in Assumption
3.1
.

The probability of this event is at least

1-2\delta-n^{2}\exp(-n^{3}/(\delta^{2}\tau^{2}\lambda_{0}^{3}))
, as in
Theorem
5.2
. All inequalities below hold on this event.

Comparing Corollary
5.3
with
Assumption
5.1
(
8
), we identify

\varepsilon_{1}({\mathbf{W}})=2mn\kappa^{2}R_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{2}+mn\big(\kappa^{2}R_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{2}+\kappa R_{{\mathbf{w}}}\big)\phi_{\max}({\mathbf{W}})^{2}+mn\kappa^{2}\big(R_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{2}+1\big)\psi_{\max}({\mathbf{W}})^{2}.

(34)

On the NTK event, the weights stay close to initialization, hence their norms
are uniformly bounded; using Assumption
3.1
and the definition of

R_{{\mathbf{w}}}
and
R_{{\mathbf{u}}}
, we obtain:

R_{{\mathbf{w}}}({\mathbf{W}}_{k}):=\max_{r\in[m]}\|{\mathbf{w}}_{k,r}\|_{2}\leq C_{w}\tau\sqrt{d}

(35)

R_{{\mathbf{u}}}({\mathbf{W}}_{k}):=\max_{r\in[m],i\in[n]}\|{\mathbf{w}}_{k,r}\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\|_{2}\leq C_{u}\tau\sqrt{d}.

(36)

for constants
C_{w},C_{u}>0
.

Lemma C.2

(Bound on
R_{{\mathbf{w}}}
)
.

On the NTK event of Theorem
5.2
, there exists an absolute constant
C_{w}>0
such that for all iterations
k\leq K
:

R_{{\mathbf{w}}}(\mathbf{W}_{k}):=\max_{r\in[m]}\|\mathbf{w}_{k,r}\|_{2}\leq C_{w}\tau\sqrt{d}.

(37)

Proof.

Recall that the first–layer weights are initialized as
\mathbf{w}_{0,r}\sim\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{0},\tau^{2}\mathbf{I}_{d})
for
r=1,\dots,m
. During training, the NTK event of Theorem
5.2
ensures that each row stays in a small ball around its initialization:

\|\mathbf{w}_{k,r}-\mathbf{w}_{0,r}\|_{2}\leq R,\qquad R:=C_{1}\tau\frac{\lambda_{0}}{n},\qquad\forall k\leq K,r\in[m].

(38)

We define
\mathbf{z}_{r}:=\frac{1}{\tau}\mathbf{w}_{0,r}
. Each coordinate satisfies
(z_{r})_{j}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,1)
, making
\mathbf{z}_{r}
a standard Gaussian vector
\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{0},\mathbf{I}_{d})
. Its squared norm follows a chi-square distribution:
\|\mathbf{z}_{r}\|_{2}^{2}\sim\chi^{2}_{d}
. Using the Laurent–Massart concentration inequality, for
t=d
:

\Pr\big(\|\mathbf{z}_{r}\|_{2}^{2}\geq 5d\big)\leq e^{-d}.

Thus, with high probability,
\|\mathbf{z}_{r}\|_{2}\leq\sqrt{5d}
. Defining
C_{0}=\sqrt{5}
, we obtain the initialization bound:

\|\mathbf{w}_{0,r}\|_{2}=\tau\|\mathbf{z}_{r}\|_{2}\leq C_{0}\tau\sqrt{d}.

(39)

By a union bound over
r\in[m]
, this holds for all rows with probability at least
1-me^{-d}
. Combining the triangle inequality with (
38
) and (
39
), we find:

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{w}_{k,r}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{w}_{0,r}\|_{2}+\|\mathbf{w}_{k,r}-\mathbf{w}_{0,r}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\tau\sqrt{d}+C_{1}\tau\frac{\lambda_{0}}{n}

\displaystyle=\tau\sqrt{d}\left(C_{0}+\frac{C_{1}\lambda_{0}}{n\sqrt{d}}\right).

Since
\lambda_{0}/n
is
O(1)
and
d\geq 1
, we define the absolute constant
C_{w}:=C_{0}+\frac{C_{1}\lambda_{0}}{n\sqrt{d}}
. Taking the maximum over
r\in[m]
yields:

R_{w}(\mathbf{W}_{k})=\max_{r\in[m]}\|\mathbf{w}_{k,r}\|_{2}\leq C_{w}\tau\sqrt{d}.

(40)

Intuitively, since the movement term
\frac{C_{1}\lambda_{0}}{n\sqrt{d}}
vanishes as
d\to\infty
, the weights remain on the same scale as their initialization throughout training.
∎

Lemma C.3

(Bound on
R_{u}
)
.

Suppose Assumption
3.1
holds, such that the input data is bounded in
\ell_{\infty}
-norm by
B_{x}:=\max_{i\in[n]}\|x_{i}\|_{\infty}
. On the NTK event of Theorem
5.2
, there exists an absolute constant
C_{u}>0
such that, for all iterations
k\leq K
:

R_{u}(W_{k}):=\max_{r\in[m],i\in[n]}\|w_{k,r}\odot x_{i}\|_{2}\leq C_{u}\tau\sqrt{d}.

(41)

Proof.

Consider any iteration
k\leq K
, neuron
r\in[m]
, and sample index
i\in[n]
. We analyze the squared
\ell_{2}
-norm of the Hadamard product by pulling out the maximum coordinate of the input vector:

\displaystyle\|w_{k,r}\odot x_{i}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{d}w_{k,r,j}^{2}x_{i,j}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\max_{1\leq j\leq d}x_{i,j}^{2}\right)\sum_{j=1}^{d}w_{k,r,j}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|x_{i}\|_{\infty}^{2}\|w_{k,r}\|_{2}^{2}.

Taking the square root of both sides, we obtain the inequality:

\|w_{k,r}\odot x_{i}\|_{2}\leq\|x_{i}\|_{\infty}\|w_{k,r}\|_{2}\leq B_{x}\|w_{k,r}\|_{2}.

Taking the maximum over all
r\in[m]
and
i\in[n]
yields:

R_{u}(W_{k})\leq B_{x}R_{w}(W_{k}).

(42)

From the weight stability bound previously established (Proof of
R_{w}
), we know that on the NTK event, the weights are bounded by
R_{w}(W_{k})\leq C_{w}\tau\sqrt{d}
, where
C_{w}
is an absolute constant. Substituting this into (
42
):

R_{u}(W_{k})\leq B_{x}(C_{w}\tau\sqrt{d}).

Defining the absolute constant
C_{u}:=B_{x}C_{w}
completes the proof. Note that since
B_{x}
is a fixed property of the dataset and
C_{w}
is independent of
k
,
C_{u}
is a valid absolute constant for the problem.
∎

We an iteration
k\in[K]
and denote, for brevity,

R_{{\mathbf{w}}}:=R_{{\mathbf{w}}}({\mathbf{W}}_{k}),\quad R_{{\mathbf{u}}}:=R_{{\mathbf{u}}}({\mathbf{W}}_{k}),\quad\phi_{k}:=\phi_{\max}({\mathbf{W}}_{k}),\quad\psi_{k}:=\psi_{\max}({\mathbf{W}}_{k}).

Then (
34
) becomes

\varepsilon_{1}({\mathbf{W}}_{k})=2mn\kappa^{2}R_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{2}+mn\big(\kappa^{2}R_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{2}+\kappa R_{{\mathbf{w}}}\big)\phi_{k}^{2}+mn\kappa^{2}\big(R_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{2}+1\big)\psi_{k}^{2}.

(43)

We now bound each of the three terms on the right-hand side using
(
35
),(
36
).

(i) First term.

Using
R_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{2}\leq C_{u}^{2}\tau^{2}d
from (
36
), we get

\displaystyle 2mn\kappa^{2}R_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2mn\kappa^{2}\cdot C_{u}^{2}\tau^{2}d

\displaystyle=(2C_{u}^{2})\kappa^{2}\tau^{2}mnd=O\big(\kappa^{2}\tau^{2}mnd\big).

(44)

(ii) Middle term.

mn\big(\kappa^{2}R_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{2}+\kappa R_{{\mathbf{w}}}\big)\phi_{k}^{2}=mn\kappa^{2}R_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{2}\phi_{k}^{2}+mn\kappa R_{{\mathbf{w}}}\phi_{k}^{2}.

For the
\kappa^{2}R_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{2}\phi_{k}^{2}
component, we use
R_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{2}\leq C_{u}^{2}\tau^{2}d
from (
36
):

\displaystyle mn\kappa^{2}R_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{2}\phi_{k}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq mn\kappa^{2}(C_{u}^{2}\tau^{2}d)\phi_{k}^{2}

\displaystyle=C_{u}^{2}\kappa^{2}\tau^{2}mnd\phi_{k}^{2}=O\big(\kappa^{2}\tau^{2}mnd\phi_{k}^{2}\big).

(45)

For the
\kappa R_{{\mathbf{w}}}\phi_{k}^{2}
component, we use
R_{{\mathbf{w}}}\leq C_{w}\tau\sqrt{d}
from (
35
):

\displaystyle mn\kappa R_{{\mathbf{w}}}\phi_{k}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq mn\kappa(C_{w}\tau\sqrt{d})\phi_{k}^{2}

\displaystyle=C_{w}\kappa\tau mn\sqrt{d}\phi_{k}^{2}=O\big(\kappa\tau mn\sqrt{d}\phi_{k}^{2}\big).

(46)

(iii) Last term.

For the last term, using (
36
), it is true that

R_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{2}+1\leq C_{u}^{2}\tau^{2}d+1
, so there exists a constant
C_{u}^{\prime}>0

such that
R_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{2}+1\leq C_{u}^{\prime}\tau^{2}d
for big enough
d
.

Hence,

\displaystyle mn\kappa^{2}\big(R_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{2}+1\big)\psi_{k}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq mn\kappa^{2}(C_{u}^{\prime}\tau^{2}d)\psi_{k}^{2}

\displaystyle=C_{u}^{\prime}\kappa^{2}\tau^{2}mnd\psi_{k}^{2}=O\big(\kappa^{2}\tau^{2}mnd\psi_{k}^{2}\big).

(47)

Combining (
43
) with
(
44
), (
45
), (
46
),
and (
47
), we obtain

\displaystyle\varepsilon_{1}({\mathbf{W}}_{k})

\displaystyle\leq O\big(\kappa^{2}\tau^{2}mnd\big)+O\big(\kappa^{2}\tau^{2}mnd\phi_{k}^{2}\big)+O\big(\kappa\tau mn\sqrt{d}\phi_{k}^{2}\big)+O\big(\kappa^{2}\tau^{2}mnd\psi_{k}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle=O\big(\kappa^{2}\tau^{2}mnd\big)+O\big(\kappa^{2}\tau^{2}mnd(\phi_{k}^{2}+\psi_{k}^{2})\big)+O\big(\kappa\tau mn\sqrt{d}\phi_{k}^{2}\big).

(48)

We denote

\hat{\phi}_{\max}:=\max_{k\in[K]}\phi_{\max}({\mathbf{W}}_{k}),\qquad\hat{\psi}_{\max}:=\max_{k\in[K]}\psi_{\max}({\mathbf{W}}_{k}),

so that for each
k
,

\phi_{k}^{2}\leq\hat{\phi}_{\max}^{2},\qquad\psi_{k}^{2}\leq\hat{\psi}_{\max}^{2}.

Substituting these into (
48
) yields

\displaystyle\varepsilon_{1}({\mathbf{W}}_{k})

\displaystyle\leq O\big(\kappa^{2}\tau^{2}mnd\big)+O\big(\kappa^{2}\tau^{2}mnd(\hat{\phi}_{\max}^{2}+\hat{\psi}_{\max}^{2})\big)+O\big(\kappa\tau mn\sqrt{d}\hat{\phi}_{\max}^{2}\big),\qquad\forall k\in[K].

(49)

Taking the maximum over
k\in[K]
does not change the right-hand side,
so

\varepsilon_{1}:=\max_{k\in[K]}\varepsilon_{1}({\mathbf{W}}_{k})\leq O\big(\kappa^{2}\tau^{2}mnd\big)+O\big(\kappa^{2}\tau^{2}mnd(\hat{\phi}_{\max}^{2}+\hat{\psi}_{\max}^{2})\big)+O\big(\kappa\tau mn\sqrt{d}\hat{\phi}_{\max}^{2}\big).

(50)

or equivalently:

\boxed{\varepsilon_{1}\leq O\big(\kappa^{2}\tau^{2}mnd(\hat{\phi}_{\max}^{2}+\hat{\psi}_{\max}^{2}+1)\big)+O\big(\kappa\tau mn\sqrt{d}\hat{\phi}_{\max}^{2}\big)}

(51)

Matching Corollary
5.4
with Assumption
5.1
(
9
), we read off

\varepsilon_{3}({\mathbf{W}})=O\Big(\frac{\sigma_{\max}({\mathbf{X}})\phi_{\max}({\mathbf{W}})}{\sqrt{m}}\Big)

and

\displaystyle\varepsilon_{2}({\mathbf{W}})

\displaystyle=O\big((n\kappa^{2}B_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}R_{{\mathbf{w}}}+n\kappa R_{{\mathbf{u}}}\sqrt{d})\phi_{\max}({\mathbf{W}})\big)

\displaystyle\quad+O\big(n\kappa R_{{\mathbf{u}}}\psi_{\max}({\mathbf{W}})+\kappa^{2}\sqrt{m}B_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}R_{{\mathbf{w}}}\big).

(52)

Using
\|{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\|_{2}\leq 1
from Assumption
3.1
, we have

B_{{\mathbf{x}}}\leq 1
. On the NTK event, for all
k\in[K]
we have the uniform
bounds

R_{{\mathbf{w}}}({\mathbf{W}}_{k})\leq C_{w}\tau\sqrt{d},\qquad R_{{\mathbf{u}}}({\mathbf{W}}_{k})\leq C_{u}\tau\sqrt{d},

for some absolute constants
C_{w},C_{u}>0
(cf. the bounds proved earlier for
