R_{{\mathbf{w}}}
and
R_{{\mathbf{u}}}
).
Substituting these into (
52
) yields:

\displaystyle\varepsilon_{2}({\mathbf{W}}_{k})

\displaystyle\leq O\Big(\big(n\kappa^{2}C_{w}\tau\sqrt{d}+n\kappa C_{u}\tau\sqrt{d}\sqrt{d}\big)\phi_{\max}({\mathbf{W}}_{k})\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+O\Big(n\kappa C_{u}\tau\sqrt{d}\psi_{\max}({\mathbf{W}}_{k})+\kappa^{2}\sqrt{m}C_{w}\tau\sqrt{d}\Big)

\displaystyle=O\big(n\kappa^{2}\tau\sqrt{d}\phi_{\max}({\mathbf{W}}_{k})\big)+O\big(n\kappa\tau d\phi_{\max}({\mathbf{W}}_{k})\big)

\displaystyle\quad+O\big(n\kappa\tau\sqrt{d}\psi_{\max}({\mathbf{W}}_{k})\big)+O\big(\kappa^{2}\tau\sqrt{md}\big).

(53)

Since
d\geq 1
, we have
\sqrt{d}\leq d
, and hence each term containing

\sqrt{d}
can be upper bounded by the corresponding expression with
d
in
place of
\sqrt{d}
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\varepsilon_{2}({\mathbf{W}}_{k})

\displaystyle\leq O\big(n\kappa^{2}\tau d\phi_{\max}({\mathbf{W}}_{k})\big)+O\big(n\kappa\tau d\phi_{\max}({\mathbf{W}}_{k})\big)

\displaystyle\quad+O\big(n\kappa\tau d\psi_{\max}({\mathbf{W}}_{k})\big)+O\big(\kappa^{2}\tau\sqrt{m}d\big)

\displaystyle\leq O\big(\kappa\tau nd\big(\phi_{\max}({\mathbf{W}}_{k})+\psi_{\max}({\mathbf{W}}_{k})\big)\big)

\displaystyle\quad+O\big(\kappa^{2}\tau d\big(n\phi_{\max}({\mathbf{W}}_{k})+\sqrt{m}\big)\big).

(54)

To obtain a uniform bound over the whole training trajectory, define

\hat{\phi}_{\max}:=\max_{k\in[K]}\phi_{\max}({\mathbf{W}}_{k}),\qquad\hat{\psi}_{\max}:=\max_{k\in[K]}\psi_{\max}({\mathbf{W}}_{k}).

Taking the maximum over
k
in (
54
) yields

\varepsilon_{2}:=\max_{k\in[K]}\varepsilon_{2}({\mathbf{W}}_{k})\leq O\big(\kappa\tau nd(\hat{\phi}_{\max}+\hat{\psi}_{\max})\big)+O\big(\kappa^{2}\tau d\big(n\hat{\phi}_{\max}+\sqrt{m}\big)\big).

We can simplify
\varepsilon_{2}
further as:

\displaystyle O\big(\kappa\tau nd(\hat{\phi}_{\max}+\hat{\psi}_{\max})\big)+O\big(\kappa^{2}\tau d\big(n\hat{\phi}_{\max}+\sqrt{m}\big)\big)

\displaystyle=O\big(\kappa\tau nd(\hat{\phi}_{\max}+\hat{\psi}_{\max})+\kappa^{2}\tau d\big(n\hat{\phi}_{\max}+\sqrt{m}\big)\big)

\displaystyle=O\big(\kappa\tau nd(\hat{\phi}_{\max}+\hat{\psi}_{\max})+\kappa^{2}\tau dn\hat{\phi}_{\max}+\kappa^{2}\tau d\sqrt{m}\big)

\displaystyle\leq O\big(\kappa\tau nd(\hat{\phi}_{\max}+\hat{\psi}_{\max})+\kappa\tau dn(\hat{\phi}_{\max}+\hat{\psi}_{\max})+\kappa^{2}\tau d\sqrt{m}\big)

\displaystyle=O\big(2\kappa\tau nd(\hat{\phi}_{\max}+\hat{\psi}_{\max})+\kappa^{2}\tau d\sqrt{m}\big)

Thus,

\boxed{\varepsilon_{2}\leq O\big(\kappa\tau nd(\hat{\phi}_{\max}+\hat{\psi}_{\max})\big)+O\big(\kappa^{2}\tau d\sqrt{m}\big)}

(55)

Theorem
5.2
requires

\varepsilon_{2}
to satisfy, for some absolute constant
c_{0}>0
,

\varepsilon_{2}\;\leq\;c_{0}\frac{\delta\lambda_{0}}{nK^{2}}

(56)

Combining (
55
) and
(
56
), we get that the following inequality must hold:

C_{1}\kappa\tau nd\bigl(\hat{\phi}_{\max}+\hat{\psi}_{\max}\bigr)+C_{2}\kappa^{2}\tau d\sqrt{m}\;\leq\;c_{0}\frac{\delta\lambda_{0}}{nK^{2}}.

(57)

Let us denote

a:=C_{1}\kappa\tau nd\bigl(\hat{\phi}_{\max}+\hat{\psi}_{\max}\bigr),\qquad b:=C_{2}\kappa^{2}\tau d\sqrt{m},\qquad R:=c_{0}\frac{\delta\lambda_{0}}{nK^{2}}.

Then (
57
) can be written as
a+b\leq R
.

A sufficient way to enforce this inequality is to
require that each term
a
and
b
is at most
R/2
:

a\;\leq\;\frac{R}{2},\qquad b\;\leq\;\frac{R}{2}

(58)

Indeed, if (
58
) holds, then

a+b\;\leq\;\frac{R}{2}+\frac{R}{2}=R,

so (
57
) is automatically satisfied.

Imposing
a\leq R/2
yields:

C_{1}\kappa\tau nd\bigl(\hat{\phi}_{\max}+\hat{\psi}_{\max}\bigr)\;\leq\;\frac{c_{0}}{2}\frac{\delta\lambda_{0}}{nK^{2}}.

Thus,

\kappa\;\leq\;\kappa_{\mathrm{lin}_{2}}:=\frac{c_{0}}{2C_{1}}\frac{\delta\lambda_{0}}{\tau n^{2}dK^{2}\bigl(\hat{\phi}_{\max}+\hat{\psi}_{\max}\bigr)}.

(59)

Similarly, imposing
b\leq R/2
gives:

\displaystyle C_{2}\kappa^{2}\tau d\sqrt{m}\;\leq\;\frac{c_{0}}{2}\frac{\delta\lambda_{0}}{nK^{2}}\kern 5.0pt\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\kappa^{2}\;\leq\;\frac{c_{0}}{2C_{2}}\frac{\delta\lambda_{0}}{\tau d\sqrt{m}nK^{2}}

and therefore

\kappa\;\leq\;\kappa_{\mathrm{quad}_{2}}:=\sqrt{\frac{c_{0}}{2C_{2}}}\sqrt{\frac{\delta\lambda_{0}}{\tau d\sqrt{m}nK^{2}}}.

(60)

To ensure (
56
) holds, it is sufficient that
(
59
) and (
60
) both hold.
Equivalently,

\kappa\;\leq\;\min\{\kappa_{\mathrm{lin}_{2}},\kappa_{\mathrm{quad}_{2}}\}.

In big-
O
notation we may write this as

\kappa=O\!\left(\frac{\delta\lambda_{0}}{\tau n^{2}dK^{2}(\hat{\phi}_{\max}+\hat{\psi}_{\max})}\right)\quad\text{and}\quad\kappa=O\!\left(\sqrt{\frac{\delta\lambda_{0}}{\tau d\sqrt{m}nK^{2}}}\right).

(61)

Similarly, for
\varepsilon_{1}
the general stochastic convergence theorem requires that

\varepsilon_{1}\;\leq\;c_{1}\frac{\delta m}{nK^{4}},

for some absolute constant
c_{1}>0
.

Combining (
51
) and (
C.2
) we get that the following must hold:

\displaystyle O\big(\kappa^{2}\tau^{2}mnd(\hat{\phi}_{\max}^{2}+\hat{\psi}_{\max}^{2}+1)\big)+O\big(\kappa\tau mn\sqrt{d}\hat{\phi}_{\max}^{2}\big)\;\leq\;c_{1}\frac{\delta m}{nK^{4}}\kern 5.0pt\Rightarrow

\displaystyle C_{a}\kappa^{2}\tau^{2}nd\bigl(\hat{\phi}_{\max}^{2}+\hat{\psi}_{\max}^{2}+1\bigr)+C_{b}\kappa\tau n\sqrt{d}\hat{\phi}_{\max}^{2}\;\leq\;c_{1}\frac{\delta}{nK^{4}}.

Similarly, we impose

\displaystyle C_{a}\kappa^{2}\tau^{2}nd\bigl(1+\hat{\phi}_{\max}^{2}+\hat{\psi}_{\max}^{2}\bigr)\;\leq\;\frac{c_{1}}{2}\frac{\delta}{nK^{4}}\kern 5.0pt\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\kappa^{2}\;\leq\;\frac{c_{1}}{2C_{a}}\frac{\delta}{\tau^{2}n^{2}dK^{4}\bigl(\hat{\phi}_{\max}^{2}+\hat{\psi}_{\max}^{2}+1\bigr)}\Rightarrow

\kappa\;\leq\;\kappa_{\mathrm{quad}_{1}}:=\sqrt{\frac{c_{1}}{2C_{a}}}\cdot\frac{\sqrt{\delta}}{\tau n\sqrt{d}K^{2}\sqrt{\hat{\phi}_{\max}^{2}+\hat{\psi}_{\max}^{2}+1}}.

(62)

and

\displaystyle C_{b}\kappa\tau n\sqrt{d}\hat{\phi}_{\max}^{2}\;\leq\;\frac{c_{1}}{2}\frac{\delta}{nK^{4}}\kern 5.0pt\Rightarrow

\kappa\;\leq\;\kappa_{\mathrm{lin_{1}}}:=\frac{c_{1}}{2C_{b}}\frac{\delta}{\tau n^{2}\sqrt{d}K^{4}\hat{\phi}_{\max}^{2}}.

(63)

Thus, the
\varepsilon_{1}
requirement
(
C.2
) is guaranteed whenever

\kappa\;\leq\;\min\bigl\{\kappa_{\mathrm{quad}_{1}},\;\kappa_{\mathrm{lin}_{1}}\bigr\}.

Combining all constraints from
\varepsilon_{1}

and
\varepsilon_{2}
, we see that a sufficient set of conditions is

\kappa\;\leq\;\min\bigl\{\kappa_{\mathrm{lin}_{1}},\kappa_{\mathrm{quad}_{1}},\kappa_{\mathrm{lin}_{2}},\kappa_{\mathrm{quad}_{2}}\bigr\}.

Equivalently, in big-
O
notation,

\kappa=O\!\left(\min\left\{\frac{\delta\lambda_{0}}{\tau n^{2}dK^{2}(\hat{\phi}_{\max}+\hat{\psi}_{\max})},\;\frac{\delta}{\tau n^{2}\sqrt{d}K^{4}\hat{\phi}_{\max}^{2}},\;\sqrt{\frac{\delta\lambda_{0}}{\tau d\sqrt{m}nK^{2}}},\;\sqrt{\frac{\delta}{\tau^{2}n^{2}dK^{4}(\hat{\phi}_{\max}^{2}+\hat{\psi}_{\max}^{2}+1)}}\right\}\right).

(64)

Instead of carrying this minimum in the theorem statement, we define a slightly more restrictive but cleaner condition that implies all of the above bounds:

\kappa\;=\;O\!\left(\frac{\sqrt{\delta\lambda_{0}}}{\tau^{2}K^{2}\bigl(m^{1/4}\sqrt{d}+nd\bigr)\big(\hat{\phi}_{\max}+\hat{\psi}_{\max}\big)}\right).

(65)

For
\varepsilon_{3}
, we combine (
9
) and (
5.4
) which yields that:

\varepsilon_{3}=O\Big(\frac{\sigma_{\max}({\mathbf{X}})\phi_{\max}}{\sqrt{m}}\Big)

We assume that

\sigma_{\max}({\mathbf{X}})\hat{\phi}_{\max}\leq C\lambda_{0}/\sqrt{n}

for some constant
C>0
(assumption (
15
)), and therefore

\varepsilon_{3}\leq O\Big(\frac{\lambda_{0}}{\sqrt{mn}}\Big),

(66)

which is exactly the form required in Theorem
5.2
.

Theorem
5.2
includes the factor

\displaystyle O\left(\frac{mn}{\lambda_{0}^{2}}\cdot\varepsilon_{2}^{2}+\varepsilon_{1}\right)\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{eq:eps2_scaling_correct},\eqref{eq:eps1_before_simplify}}}{{=}}

So we study the quantity
\frac{mn}{\lambda_{0}^{2}}\cdot\varepsilon_{2}^{2}
.

\displaystyle\frac{mn}{\lambda_{0}^{2}}\cdot\varepsilon_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{mn}{\lambda_{0}^{2}}\cdot\Big(\kappa^{2}\tau^{2}n^{2}d^{2}(\hat{\phi}_{\max}+\hat{\psi}_{\max})^{2}+\kappa^{4}\tau^{2}d^{2}m\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\lambda_{0}^{2}}\kappa^{2}\tau^{2}n^{3}d^{2}m(\hat{\phi}_{\max}^{2}+\hat{\psi}_{\max}^{2})+\frac{1}{\lambda_{0}^{2}}\kappa^{4}\tau^{2}d^{2}m^{2}n

\displaystyle={\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}O\left(\kappa\tau^{2}mn^{3}d^{2}\big(\hat{\phi}_{\max}^{2}+\hat{\psi}_{\max}^{2}\big)\right)}+{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}O\left(\kappa^{2}\tau^{2}m^{2}nd^{2}\right)}

(67)

because
(a+b)^{2}\leq 2(a^{2}+b^{2})
and
\kappa^{4}\leq\kappa^{2}\leq\kappa
for
\kappa\leq 1

Furthermore,

\displaystyle\varepsilon_{1}

\displaystyle=O\big(\kappa^{2}\tau^{2}mnd\big)+O\big(\kappa^{2}\tau^{2}mnd(\hat{\phi}_{\max}^{2}+\hat{\psi}_{\max}^{2})\big)+O\big(\kappa\tau mn\sqrt{d}\hat{\phi}_{\max}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}O\big(\kappa^{2}\tau^{2}m^{2}nd^{2}\big)}+{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}O\big(\kappa\tau^{2}mn^{3}d^{2}(\hat{\phi}_{\max}^{2}+\hat{\psi}_{\max}^{2})\big)}+{\color[rgb]{1,0.5,0}O\big(\kappa\tau mn\sqrt{d}\hat{\phi}_{\max}^{2}\big)}

(68)

Comment:

Tried bounding
O\big(\kappa\tau mn\sqrt{d}\hat{\phi}_{\max}^{2}\big)
in the term
O\big(\kappa\tau^{2}mn^{3}d^{2}(\hat{\phi}_{\max}^{2}+\hat{\psi}_{\max}^{2})\big)
as
\hat{\phi}_{\max}^{2}\leq\hat{\phi}_{\max}^{2}+\hat{\psi}_{\max}^{2}
and
\sqrt{d}\leq d^{2}
(since in our case it definitely holds that
d>1
) but it does not necessarily hold that
\tau\leq\tau^{2}

Therefore,

\displaystyle O\left(\frac{mn}{\lambda_{0}^{2}}\cdot\varepsilon_{2}^{2}+\varepsilon_{1}\right)\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{eq:eps1_bigO},\eqref{eq:eps2_bigO}}}{{=}}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}O\big(\kappa^{2}\tau^{2}m^{2}nd^{2}\big)}+{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}O\big(\kappa\tau^{2}mn^{3}d^{2}(\hat{\phi}_{\max}^{2}+\hat{\psi}_{\max}^{2})\big)}+{\color[rgb]{1,0.5,0}O\big(\kappa\tau mn\sqrt{d}\hat{\phi}_{\max}^{2}\big)}

Thus, the expected loss is bounded by:

\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{W}_{K})]\leq\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{2}\right)^{K}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{W}_{0})+{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,0.5}O\big(\kappa^{2}\tau^{2}m^{2}nd^{2}\big)}+{\color[rgb]{0.75,0,0.25}O\big(\kappa\tau^{2}mn^{3}d^{2}(\hat{\phi}_{\max}^{2}+\hat{\psi}_{\max}^{2})\big)}+{\color[rgb]{1,0.5,0}O\big(\kappa\tau mn\sqrt{d}\hat{\phi}_{\max}^{2}\big)}

C.2.1
Proof of Corollary
5.3

Proof.

By Theorem
4.2
, we have that

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{C}}}\left[{\mathcal{L}}_{{\mathbf{C}}}\left(\bm{\theta}\right)\right]-{\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\left|\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\left(\hat{f}\left(\bm{\theta},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-y_{i}\right)^{2}-\left({f}\left({\mathbf{W}},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-y_{i}\right)^{2}\right)\right|}_{\mathcal{T}_{1}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\underbrace{\frac{\kappa^{2}}{2m}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\|\sum_{r=1}^{m}a_{r}\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r}\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{r}\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right\|_{2}}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}}_{\mathcal{T}_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+mn\left(\kappa^{2}R_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{2}\psi_{\max}^{2}+\left(\kappa^{2}R_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{2}+\kappa R_{{\mathbf{w}}}\right)\phi_{\max}^{2}\right)

Now, we can bound
\mathcal{T}_{1}
and
\mathcal{T}_{2}
separately. For
\mathcal{T}_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\left(\hat{f}\left(\bm{\theta},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-y_{i}\right)^{2}-\left({f}\left({\mathbf{W}},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-y_{i}\right)^{2}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\leq\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\left(\hat{f}\left(\bm{\theta},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-{f}\left({\mathbf{W}},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)\right)^{2}+2\left|\left(\hat{f}\left(\bm{\theta},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-{f}\left({\mathbf{W}},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)\right)\left(\hat{f}\left(\bm{\theta},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-{f}\left({\mathbf{W}},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)\right)\right|\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\leq\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\hat{f}\left(\bm{\theta},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-{f}\left({\mathbf{W}},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)\right)^{2}+{\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}\right)

Here, we can bound
\hat{f}\left({\mathbf{W}},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-{f}\left({\mathbf{W}},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)
as

\displaystyle\left|\hat{f}\left({\mathbf{W}},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-{f}\left({\mathbf{W}},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r=1}^{m}\left|\hat{\sigma}\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)-\sigma\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r=1}^{m}\left|{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right|\left(\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0\right\}-\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{r}\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right\|_{2}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r=1}^{m}\left|{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right|\exp\left(-\frac{\left({\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)^{2}}{2\kappa^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{r}\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right\|_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r=1}^{m}\psi\left(\frac{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}}{2\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right\|_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{m}\psi_{\max}

where in the third inequality we used Lemma
D.4
. Therefore,
\mathcal{T}_{1}
can be bounded as

\mathcal{T}_{1}\leq{\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}\right)+mn\psi_{\max}

For
\mathcal{T}_{2}
, we an bound it as

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{2}\leq 2\kappa^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{r=1}^{m}\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{r}\odot{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right\|\leq 2\kappa^{2}mnR_{{\mathbf{u}}}^{2}

Plugging in
\mathcal{T}_{1}
and
\mathcal{T}_{2}
gives the desired result.
∎

C.2.2
Proof of Corollary
5.4

Proof.

By Theorem
4.9
, we have that

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{C}}}\left[\nabla_{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}}{\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}\right)\right]-\nabla_{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}}{\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}\right)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|{\mathbf{g}}_{r}+\frac{3\kappa^{2}}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{m}a_{r^{\prime}}\text{Diag}\left({\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)^{2}{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0;{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0\right\}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|{\mathbf{g}}_{r}\right\|_{2}+\frac{3\kappa^{2}}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{m}\left\|a_{r^{\prime}}\text{Diag}\left({\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right)^{2}{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0;{\mathbf{w}}_{r^{\prime}}^{\top}{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\geq 0\right\}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|{\mathbf{g}}_{r}\right\|_{2}+\frac{3\kappa^{2}}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{m}\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right\|_{\infty}^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{w}}_{r}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\sigma_{\max}\left({\mathbf{X}}\right)}{\sqrt{m}}{\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+6n\kappa^{2}B_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}R_{{\mathbf{w}}}+5n\kappa R_{{\mathbf{u}}}\sqrt{d}\right)\phi_{\max}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+6n\kappa R_{{\mathbf{u}}}\psi_{\max}+3\kappa^{2}\sqrt{m}B_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}R_{{\mathbf{w}}}

Theorem
4.9
states that the norm
\left\|{\mathbf{g}}_{r}\right\|_{2}
satisfies

\left\|{\mathbf{g}}_{r}\right\|_{2}\leq\left(6n\kappa^{2}B_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}R_{{\mathbf{w}}}+5n\kappa R_{{\mathbf{u}}}\sqrt{d}\right)\phi_{\max}+\frac{\sigma_{\max}\left({\mathbf{X}}\right)}{\sqrt{m}}\phi_{\max}{\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+6n\kappa R_{{\mathbf{u}}}\psi_{\max}

Moreover, by Assumption
3.1
, we have that
\left\|{\mathbf{x}}\right\|_{\infty}\leq B_{{\mathbf{x}}}
. Therefore, we obtain that

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{C}}}\left[\nabla_{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}}{\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}\right)\right]-\nabla_{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}}{\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}\right)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\sigma_{\max}\left({\mathbf{X}}\right)}{\sqrt{m}}{\mathcal{L}}\left(\bm{\theta}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+6n\kappa^{2}B_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}R_{{\mathbf{w}}}+5n\kappa R_{{\mathbf{u}}}\sqrt{d}\right)\phi_{\max}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+6n\kappa R_{{\mathbf{u}}}\psi_{\max}+3\kappa^{2}\sqrt{m}B_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}R_{{\mathbf{w}}}

∎

C.2.3
Proof of Lemma
D.21

Proof.

By the form of
\nabla_{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}}{\mathcal{L}}_{{\mathbf{C}}}\left(\mathbf{W}\right)
in (
2
), we have:

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla_{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}}{\mathcal{L}}_{{\mathbf{C}}}\left(\mathbf{W}\right)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\frac{a_{r}}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(f\left(\mathbf{W},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\odot{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\right)-y_{i}\right)\left({\mathbf{x}}_{i}\odot{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\right)\underbrace{\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}\left({\mathbf{x}}_{i}\odot{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\right)\geq 0\right\}}_{\leq 1}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\left|a_{r}\right|}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|f\left(\mathbf{W},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\odot{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\right)-y_{i}\right|\cdot\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\odot{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sqrt{n}}{\sqrt{m}}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}(f\left(\mathbf{W},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\odot{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\right)-y_{i})^{2}\right)^{1/2}\cdot\|{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\|_{\infty}\|{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\|_{2}\kern 5.0pt\text{using \ref{lem:CS}}

\displaystyle\leq C\frac{\sqrt{n}}{\sqrt{m}}\left(2{\mathcal{L}}_{{\mathbf{C}}}\left(\mathbf{W}\right)\right)^{1/2}\kern 5.0pt\text{using}\kern 5.0pt\|{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\|_{\infty}\leq C\kern 5.0pt\text{and}\kern 5.0pt\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right\|_{2}\leq 1

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{2}\frac{\sqrt{n}}{\sqrt{m}}{\mathcal{L}}_{{\mathbf{C}}}\left(\mathbf{W}\right)^{1/2}

where, in the first inequality, we use the fact that the indicator function is upper-bounded by 1 and in the second inequality, we use the fact that
a_{r}=\pm 1
.

and so,

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla_{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}}{\mathcal{L}}_{{\mathbf{C}}}\left(\mathbf{W}\right)\right\|^{2}_{2}\leq 2C^{2}\cdot\frac{n}{m}{\mathcal{L}}_{{\mathbf{C}}}\left(\mathbf{W}\right)

∎

Appendix D
Auxiliary Results

D.1
Gaussian Random Variables

D.1.1
Conditional Expectation and Covariance

Lemma D.1
.

Let
{\mathbf{c}}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(\bm{\mu},\kappa^{2}{\mathbf{I}}\right)
, and let
z={\mathbf{c}}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}},z^{\prime}={\mathbf{c}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}
. Then we have that

\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}}\left[{\mathbf{c}}\mid z\right]=\bm{\mu}+\frac{{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}}\left(z-\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}\right);\quad\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}}\left[{\mathbf{c}}\mid z,z^{\prime}\right]=\bm{\mu}+{\mathbf{s}}_{1}\left(z-\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}\right)+{\mathbf{s}}_{2}\left(z^{\prime}-\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\right)

where the vectos
{\mathbf{s}}_{1},{\mathbf{s}}_{2}
are defined as

{\mathbf{s}}_{1}=\frac{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}{\mathbf{u}}-{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{v}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left({\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\right)^{2}};\quad{\mathbf{s}}_{2}=\frac{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}{\mathbf{v}}-{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left({\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\right)^{2}}

Proof.

By the formula of conditional expectation, we have

\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}}\left[{\mathbf{c}}\mid z\right]=\mathbb{E}[{\mathbf{c}}]+\text{Cov}\left({\mathbf{c}},z\right)\text{Var}\left(z\right)^{-1}\left(z-\mathbb{E}[z]\right)

By the definition of
{\mathbf{c}}
, we have
\mathbb{E}[{\mathbf{c}}]=\bm{\mu}
. Moreover, since
z={\mathbf{c}}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}
, by Lemma
D.13
, we have
\mathbb{E}[z]=\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}
and
\text{Var}\left(z\right)=\kappa^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}
. Therefore, the covariance between
{\mathbf{c}}
and
z
is given by

\text{Cov}\left({\mathbf{c}},z\right)=\mathbb{E}[\left({\mathbf{c}}-\bm{\mu}\right)\left(z-\left\langle\bm{\mu},{\mathbf{v}}\right\rangle\right)]=\mathbb{E}\left[z{\mathbf{c}}\right]-\bm{\mu}\left\langle\bm{\mu},{\mathbf{v}}\right\rangle

where

\mathbb{E}[z{\mathbf{c}}]_{j}=\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{d}\mathbb{E}[c_{j}c_{j^{\prime}}u_{j^{\prime}}]=\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{d}\left(\mu_{j}\mu_{j^{\prime}}+\mathbb{I}\{j=j^{\prime}\}\kappa^{2}\right)u_{j^{\prime}}=\mu_{j}\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}+\kappa^{2}u_{j}

This gives

\mathbb{E}[z{\mathbf{c}}]=\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}\cdot\bm{\mu}+\kappa^{2}{\mathbf{u}}

Therefore

\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}}\left[{\mathbf{c}}\mid z\right]=\bm{\mu}+\frac{{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}}\left(z-\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}\right)

Similarly, since
z^{\prime}={\mathbf{c}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}
. Then

\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}}\left[{\mathbf{c}}\mid z,z^{\prime}\right]=\mathbb{E}[{\mathbf{c}}]+\text{Cov}\left({\mathbf{c}},[z,z^{\prime}]\right)\text{Cov}\left(z,z^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\left([z,z^{\prime}]^{\top}-\mathbb{E}[[z,z^{\prime}]]^{\top}\right)

where

\text{Cov}\left({\mathbf{c}},[z,z^{\prime}]\right)=\kappa^{2}\begin{bmatrix}{\mathbf{u}}\\
{\mathbf{v}}\end{bmatrix};\quad\text{Cov}\left(z,z^{\prime}\right)=\kappa^{2}\begin{bmatrix}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}&{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\\
{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}&\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\end{bmatrix}

This gives

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}}\left[{\mathbf{c}}\mid z,z^{\prime}\right]

\displaystyle=\bm{\mu}+\begin{bmatrix}{\mathbf{u}}\\
{\mathbf{v}}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}&{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\\
{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}&\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\end{bmatrix}^{-1}\begin{bmatrix}z-\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}\\
z^{\prime}-\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\bm{\mu}+\frac{1}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left({\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\right)^{2}}\begin{bmatrix}{\mathbf{u}}\\
{\mathbf{v}}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}&-{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\\
-{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}&\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\end{bmatrix}^{-1}\begin{bmatrix}z-\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}\\
z^{\prime}-\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\bm{\mu}+\frac{\left(\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}{\mathbf{u}}-{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{v}}\right)\left(z-\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}\right)+\left(\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}{\mathbf{v}}-{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{u}}\right)\left(z^{\prime}-\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\right)}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left({\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle=\bm{\mu}+{\mathbf{s}}_{1}\left(z-\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}\right)+{\mathbf{s}}_{2}\left(z^{\prime}-\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\right)

where the vectos
{\mathbf{s}}_{1},{\mathbf{s}}_{2}
are defined as

{\mathbf{s}}_{1}=\frac{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}{\mathbf{u}}-{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{v}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left({\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\right)^{2}};\quad{\mathbf{s}}_{2}=\frac{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}{\mathbf{v}}-{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left({\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\right)^{2}}

∎

Lemma D.2
.

Let
{\mathbf{c}}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(\bm{\mu},\kappa^{2}{\mathbf{I}}\right)
, and let
z={\mathbf{c}}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}},z^{\prime}={\mathbf{c}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}
. Then we have

\text{Cov}\left({\mathbf{c}}\mid z,z^{\prime}\right)=\kappa^{2}{\mathbf{I}}-\frac{\kappa^{2}\left({\mathbf{u}}{\mathbf{v}}^{\top}-{\mathbf{v}}{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}\right)^{2}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left({\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\right)^{2}}

Proof.

The conditional covariance of Gaussian random variables is given by

\text{Cov}\left({\mathbf{c}}\mid z,z^{\prime}\right)=\text{Cov}\left({\mathbf{c}}\right)-\text{Cov}\left({\mathbf{c}},[z,z^{\prime}]\right)\text{Cov}\left(z,z^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\text{Cov}\left({\mathbf{c}},[z,z^{\prime}]\right)^{\top}

Recall that in the previos lemma we have computed

\displaystyle\text{Cov}\left({\mathbf{c}},[z,z^{\prime}]\right)=\kappa^{2}\begin{bmatrix}{\mathbf{u}}\\
{\mathbf{v}}\end{bmatrix};\quad\text{Cov}\left(z,z^{\prime}\right)=\kappa^{2}\begin{bmatrix}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}&{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\\
{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}&\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\end{bmatrix}

Thus, we have

\displaystyle\text{Cov}\left({\mathbf{c}}\mid z,z^{\prime}\right)

\displaystyle=\kappa^{2}{\mathbf{I}}-\kappa^{2}\begin{bmatrix}{\mathbf{u}}\\
{\mathbf{v}}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}&{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\\
{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}&\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\end{bmatrix}^{-1}\begin{bmatrix}{\mathbf{u}}&{\mathbf{v}}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\kappa^{2}{\mathbf{I}}-\frac{\kappa^{2}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left({\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\right)^{2}}\begin{bmatrix}{\mathbf{u}}\\
{\mathbf{v}}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}&-{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\\
-{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}&\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}{\mathbf{u}}&{\mathbf{v}}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\kappa^{2}{\mathbf{I}}-\frac{\kappa^{2}\left({\mathbf{u}}{\mathbf{v}}^{\top}-{\mathbf{v}}{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}\right)^{2}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left({\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\right)^{2}}

∎

D.1.2
Approximation of CDF

In approximation of the Gaussian CDF, we use the following property

Lemma D.3

(
chu1955Bounds
)
.

Let
x\geq 0
be given, then the following inequality holds

\frac{1}{2}\left(1-\exp\left(-\frac{x^{2}}{2}\right)\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\int_{0}^{x}\exp\left(-\frac{t^{2}}{2}\right)dt\leq\frac{1}{2}\left(1-\exp\left(-\frac{2x^{2}}{\pi}\right)\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

To start, we analyze the CDF of a single variable Gaussian random variable

Lemma D.4
.

Let
\bm{\Phi}_{1}
be the CDF of a standard Gaussian random variable. Then we have

\left|\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\alpha\right)-\mathbb{I}\left\{\alpha\geq 0\right\}\right|\leq\exp\left(-\frac{\alpha^{2}}{2}\right)

Proof.

Let
\nu(x)=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\int_{0}^{x}\exp\left(-\frac{t^{2}}{2}\right)dt
for
x\geq 0
. We study the case of
\alpha\geq 0
and
\alpha<0
separately. For
\alpha\geq 0
, we have

\displaystyle\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\alpha\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\int_{-\infty}^{\alpha}\exp\left(-\frac{t^{2}}{2}\right)dt

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}+\nu(\alpha)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{2}\left(1-\exp\left(-\frac{\alpha^{2}}{2}\right)\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\geq 1-\exp\left(-\frac{\alpha^{2}}{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{I}\left\{\alpha\geq 0\right\}-\exp\left(-\frac{\alpha^{2}}{2}\right)

Since
\bm{\Phi}_{1}\leq 1=\mathbb{I}\left\{\alpha\geq 0\right\}
when
\alpha\geq 0
, we must have that, for
\alpha\geq 0
,
\left|\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\alpha\right)-\mathbb{I}\left\{\alpha\geq 0\right\}\right|\leq\exp\left(-\frac{\alpha^{2}}{2}\right)
. Similarly, for
\alpha<0
, we have

\displaystyle\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\alpha\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\int_{-\infty}^{\alpha}\exp\left(-\frac{t^{2}}{2}\right)dt

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}-\nu(\alpha)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{2}\left(1-\exp\left(-\frac{\alpha^{2}}{2}\right)\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(-\frac{\alpha^{2}}{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{I}\left\{\alpha\geq 0\right\}+\exp\left(-\frac{\alpha^{2}}{2}\right)

Since
\bm{\Phi}_{1}\geq 0=\mathbb{I}\left\{\alpha\geq 0\right\}
when
\alpha<0
, we must have that, for
\alpha<0
,
\left|\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\alpha\right)-\mathbb{I}\left\{\alpha\geq 0\right\}\right|\leq\exp\left(-\frac{\alpha^{2}}{2}\right)
.
∎

Lemma D.5
.

Let
\bm{\Phi}_{2}\left(\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2},\rho\right)
be the joint CDF of two standard Gaussian random variables with covariance
\rho
at
\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2}
. Then we have

\left|\bm{\Phi}_{2}\left(\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2},\rho\right)-\mathbb{I}\left\{\alpha_{1}\geq 0;\alpha_{2}\geq 0\right\}\right|\leq 2\exp\left(-\frac{\min\left\{\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2}\right\}^{2}}{2}\right)

Proof.

Since
z_{1},z_{2}
are standard Gaussian random variables with covariance
\rho
, we have
z_{1}\mid z_{2}=\zeta\sim\mathcal{N}\left(\rho\zeta,1-\rho^{2}\right)
. According to the definition of CDF,

\displaystyle\bm{\Phi}_{2}\left(\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2},\rho\right)

\displaystyle=\int_{-\infty}^{\alpha_{2}}\int_{-\infty}^{\alpha_{1}}f_{z_{1},z_{2}}\left(\zeta_{1},\zeta_{2},\rho\right)d\zeta_{1}d\zeta_{2}

\displaystyle=\int_{-\infty}^{\alpha_{2}}\int_{-\infty}^{\alpha_{1}}f_{z_{1}\mid z_{2}=\zeta_{2}}\left(\zeta_{1}\right)d\zeta_{1}f_{z_{2}}\left(\zeta_{2}\right)d\zeta_{2}

Focusing on the inner integral, we substitute
\zeta^{\prime}=\frac{\zeta_{1}-\rho\zeta_{2}}{\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}}
. Then we have
d\zeta_{1}=\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}d\zeta^{\prime}
. Thus

\displaystyle\int_{-\infty}^{\alpha_{1}}f_{z_{1}\mid z_{2}=\zeta_{2}}\left(\zeta_{1}\right)d\zeta_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi(1-\rho^{2})}}\int_{-\infty}^{\alpha_{1}}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(\zeta_{1}-\rho\zeta_{2}\right)^{2}}{2(1-\rho^{2})}\right)d\zeta_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\int_{-\infty}^{\frac{\alpha_{1}-\rho\zeta_{2}}{\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}}}\exp\left(-\frac{\zeta^{\prime 2}}{2}\right)d\zeta^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\Phi_{1}\left(\frac{\alpha_{1}-\rho\zeta_{2}}{\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{I}\left\{\frac{\alpha_{1}-\rho\zeta_{2}}{\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}}\geq 0\right\}+\varepsilon\left(\frac{\alpha_{1}-\rho\zeta_{2}}{\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{I}\left\{\alpha_{1}\geq\rho\zeta_{2}\right\}+\varepsilon\left(\frac{\alpha_{1}-\rho\zeta_{2}}{\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}}\right)

Where
\left|\varepsilon\left(\frac{\alpha_{1}-\rho\zeta_{2}}{\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}}\right)\right|\leq\exp\left(-\frac{\left(\alpha_{1}-\rho\zeta_{2}\right)^{2}}{2(1-\rho^{2})}\right)
. Therefore

\displaystyle\bm{\Phi}_{2}\left(\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2},\rho\right)

\displaystyle=\int_{-\infty}^{\alpha_{2}}\left(\mathbb{I}\left\{\alpha_{1}\geq\rho\zeta_{2}\right\}+\varepsilon\left(\frac{\alpha_{1}-\rho\zeta_{2}}{\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}}\right)\right)f_{z_{2}}\left(\zeta_{2}\right)d\zeta_{2}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\int_{-\infty}^{\alpha_{2}}\mathbb{I}\left\{\alpha_{1}\geq\rho\zeta_{2}\right\}f_{z_{2}}\left(\zeta_{2}\right)d\zeta_{2}}_{\mathcal{I}_{1}}+\underbrace{\int_{-\infty}^{\alpha_{2}}\varepsilon\left(\frac{\alpha_{1}-\rho\zeta_{2}}{\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}}\right)f_{z_{2}}\left(\zeta_{2}\right)d\zeta_{2}}_{\mathcal{I}_{2}}

For
\mathcal{I}_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{1}

\displaystyle=\int_{-\infty}^{\alpha_{2}}\mathbb{I}\left\{\alpha_{1}\geq\rho\zeta_{2}\right\}f_{z_{2}}\left(\zeta_{2}\right)d\zeta_{2}

\displaystyle=\int_{-\infty}^{\min\left\{\frac{\alpha_{1}}{\rho},\alpha_{2}\right\}}f_{z_{2}}\left(\zeta_{2}\right)d\zeta_{2}

\displaystyle=\Phi_{1}\left(\min\left\{\frac{\alpha_{1}}{\rho},\alpha_{2}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{I}\left\{\min\left\{\frac{\alpha_{1}}{\rho},\alpha_{2}\right\}\geq 0\right\}+\varepsilon\left(\min\left\{\frac{\alpha_{1}}{\rho},\alpha_{2}\right\}\right)

Notice that, when
\alpha_{1}\geq 0
and
\alpha_{2}\geq 0
, we must have
\min\left\{\frac{\alpha_{1}}{\rho},\alpha_{2}\right\}\geq 0
. Conversely, when
\min\left\{\frac{\alpha_{1}}{\rho},\alpha_{2}\right\}\geq 0
, we must have that
\alpha_{1}\geq 0
and
\alpha_{2}\geq 0
. Therefore
\mathbb{I}\left\{\min\left\{\frac{\alpha_{1}}{\rho},\alpha_{2}\right\}\geq 0\right\}=\mathbb{I}\left\{\alpha_{1}\geq 0;\alpha_{2}\geq 0\right\}
. Thus, we have

\bm{\Phi}_{2}\left(\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2},\rho\right)=\mathbb{I}\left\{\alpha_{1}\geq 0;\alpha_{2}\geq 0\right\}+\varepsilon\left(\min\left\{\frac{\alpha_{1}}{\rho},\alpha_{2}\right\}\right)+\mathcal{I}_{2}

For
\mathcal{I}_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{I}_{2}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\int_{-\infty}^{\alpha_{2}}\left|\varepsilon\left(\frac{\alpha_{1}-\rho\zeta_{2}}{\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}}\right)\right|f_{z_{2}}\left(\zeta_{2}\right)d\zeta_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{-\infty}^{\alpha_{2}}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(\alpha_{1}-\rho\zeta_{2}\right)^{2}}{2(1-\rho^{2})}\right)f_{z_{2}}\left(\zeta_{2}\right)d\zeta_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\int_{-\infty}^{\alpha_{2}}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(\alpha_{1}-\rho\zeta_{2}\right)^{2}}{2(1-\rho^{2})}-\frac{\zeta_{2}^{2}}{2}\right)d\zeta_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{\alpha_{1}^{2}}{2}\right)\int_{-\infty}^{\alpha_{2}}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(\zeta_{2}-\rho\alpha_{1}\right)^{2}}{2\left(1-\rho^{2}\right)}\right)d\zeta_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(-\frac{\alpha_{1}^{2}}{2}\right)

Moreover, since
\left|\varepsilon\left(\min\left\{\frac{\alpha_{1}}{\rho},\alpha_{2}\right\}\right)\right|\leq\exp\left(-\frac{1}{2}\min\left\{\frac{\alpha_{1}}{\rho},\alpha_{2}\right\}^{2}\right)
, we have that

\displaystyle\left|\bm{\Phi}_{2}\left(\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2},\rho\right)-\mathbb{I}\left\{\alpha_{1}\geq 0;\alpha_{2}\geq 0\right\}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(-\frac{1}{2}\min\left\{\frac{\alpha_{1}}{\rho},\alpha_{2}\right\}^{2}\right)+\exp\left(-\frac{\alpha_{1}^{2}}{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(-\frac{\min\left\{\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2}\right\}^{2}}{2}\right)+\exp\left(-\frac{\alpha_{1}^{2}}{2}\right)

Exchanging
\alpha_{1}
and
\alpha_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\bm{\Phi}_{2}\left(\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2},\rho\right)-\mathbb{I}\left\{\alpha_{1}\geq 0;\alpha_{2}\geq 0\right\}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(-\frac{1}{2}\min\left\{\frac{\alpha_{1}}{\rho},\alpha_{2}\right\}^{2}\right)+\exp\left(-\frac{\alpha_{1}^{2}}{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(-\frac{\min\left\{\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2}\right\}^{2}}{2}\right)+\exp\left(-\frac{\alpha_{2}^{2}}{2}\right)

Thus, we have

\left|\bm{\Phi}_{2}\left(\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2},\rho\right)-\mathbb{I}\left\{\alpha_{1}\geq 0;\alpha_{2}\geq 0\right\}\right|\leq 2\exp\left(-\frac{\min\left\{\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2}\right\}^{2}}{2}\right)

∎

D.1.3
Uni-variate Coupled Expectation

Lemma D.6
.

let
z_{1},z_{2}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(0,1\right)
with
\text{Cov}\left(z_{1},z_{2}\right)=\rho
. Let
a,b\in\mathbb{R}
. Define

T_{1}=\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}}{2}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho a-b}{\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}}\right);\quad T_{2}=\exp\left(-\frac{b^{2}}{2}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho b-a}{\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}}\right)

Then we have that

\mathbb{E}\left[z_{1}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq a;z_{2}\geq b\right\}\right]=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(T_{1}+\rho T_{2}\right);\quad\mathbb{E}\left[z_{2}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq a;z_{2}\geq b\right\}\right]=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(T_{2}+\rho T_{1}\right)

Proof.

Writing the expectation in integral form, we have that

\mathbb{E}\left[z_{1}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq a;z_{2}\geq b\right\}\right]=\int_{b}^{\infty}\int_{a}^{\infty}z_{1}f\left(z_{1},z_{2}\right)dz_{1}dz_{2}=\int_{b}^{\infty}\left(\int_{a}^{\infty}z_{1}f\left(z_{1}\mid z_{2}\right)dz_{1}\right)f\left(z_{2}\right)dz_{2}

Since
z_{1},z_{2}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(0,1\right)
with covariance
\rho
, we have that
z_{1}\mid z_{2}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(\rho z_{2},1-\rho^{2}\right)
. Therefore, let
\rho^{\prime}=1-\rho^{2}
, we have

f\left(z_{1}\mid z_{2}\right)=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi\rho^{\prime}}}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(z_{1}-\rho z_{2}\right)^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)

Thus, by Lemma
D.14
, we have that

\displaystyle\int_{a}^{\infty}z_{1}f\left(z_{1}\mid z_{2}\right)dz_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi\rho^{\prime}}}\int_{a}^{\infty}z_{1}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(z_{1}-\rho z_{2}\right)^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)dz_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi\rho^{\prime}}}\left(\rho^{\prime}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(a-\rho z_{2}\right)^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)+\rho z_{2}\sqrt{2\pi\rho^{\prime}}\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho z_{2}-a}{\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{\rho^{\prime}}{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(a-\rho z_{2}\right)^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)+\rho z_{2}\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho z_{2}-a}{\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}}\right)

where we set
\kappa=\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}
and
\mu=\rho z_{2}
in Lemma
D.14
. Plugging into the original integral gives

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[z_{1}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq a;z_{2}\geq b\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=\int_{b}^{\infty}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\rho^{\prime}}{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(a-\rho z_{2}\right)^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)+\rho z_{2}\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho z_{2}-a}{\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}}\right)\right)f\left(z_{2}\right)dz_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}}{2\pi}\int_{b}^{\infty}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(a-\rho z_{2}\right)^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}-\frac{z_{2}^{2}}{2}\right)dz_{2}+\rho\int_{b}^{\infty}z_{2}\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho z_{2}-a}{\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}}\right)f\left(z_{2}\right)dz_{2}

Notice that

\exp\left(-\frac{\left(a-\rho z_{2}\right)^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}-\frac{z_{2}^{2}}{2}\right)=\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}-2\rho az_{2}+z_{2}^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)=\exp\left(-\frac{\left(z_{2}-\rho a\right)^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}}{2}\right)

From a previous result, we have an identity for the conditional probability, which expresses the CDF term as an integral:

\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho z_{2}-a}{\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}}\right)=\int_{a}^{\infty}f(z_{1}\mid z_{2})dz_{1}

(69)

and so:

\displaystyle\rho\int_{b}^{\infty}z_{2}\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho z_{2}-a}{\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}}\right)f(z_{2})dz_{2}=\rho\int_{b}^{\infty}z_{2}\int_{a}^{\infty}f(z_{1}\mid z_{2})dz_{1}f(z_{2})dz_{2}

Moreover, by applying Lemma
D.16
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[z_{1}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq a;z_{2}\geq b\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}}{2\pi}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}}{2}\right)\int_{b}^{\infty}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(z_{2}-\rho a\right)^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)dz_{2}+\rho\int_{b}^{\infty}z_{2}\int_{a}^{\infty}f(z_{1}\mid z_{2})dz_{1}f(z_{2})dz_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{\rho^{\prime}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}}{2}\right)\int_{b}^{\infty}f\left(z_{2}\mid z_{1}=a\right)dz_{2}+\rho\int_{b}^{\infty}\int_{a}^{\infty}z_{2}\underbrace{f(z_{1}\mid z_{2})f(z_{2})}_{f(z_{1},z_{2})}dz_{1}dz_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{\rho^{\prime}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}}{2}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho a-b}{\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}}\right)+\rho\mathbb{E}\left[z_{2}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq a;z_{2}\geq b\right\}\right]

Therefore, we can conclude that

\mathbb{E}\left[z_{1}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq a;z_{2}\geq b\right\}\right]-\rho\mathbb{E}\left[z_{2}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq a;z_{2}\geq b\right\}\right]=\frac{\rho^{\prime}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}}{2}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho a-b}{\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}}\right)=\frac{\rho^{\prime}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}T_{1}

(70)

Switching
z_{1},z_{2}
and
a,b
gives

\mathbb{E}\left[z_{2}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq a;z_{2}\geq b\right\}\right]-\rho\mathbb{E}\left[z_{1}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq a;z_{2}\geq b\right\}\right]=\frac{\rho^{\prime}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{b^{2}}{2}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho b-a}{\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}}\right)=\frac{\rho^{\prime}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}T_{2}

(71)

Solving for
\mathbb{E}\left[z_{1}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq a;z_{2}\geq b\right\}\right]
and
\mathbb{E}\left[z_{2}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq a;z_{2}\geq b\right\}\right]
from (
70
) and (
71
) gives

\mathbb{E}\left[z_{1}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq a;z_{2}\geq b\right\}\right]=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(T_{1}+\rho T_{2}\right);\quad\mathbb{E}\left[z_{2}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq a;z_{2}\geq b\right\}\right]=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(T_{2}+\rho T_{1}\right)

∎

Lemma D.7
.

Let
z_{1},z_{2}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(0,1\right)
with
\text{Cov}\left(z_{1},z_{2}\right)=\rho
. Let
a,b\in\mathbb{R}
. Define

T_{1}=\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}}{2}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho a-b}{\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}}\right);\quad T_{2}=\exp\left(-\frac{b^{2}}{2}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho b-a}{\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}}\right)

Then we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[z_{1}^{2}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq a;z_{2}\geq b\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{\rho\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}}{2\pi}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}-2\rho ab+b^{2}}{2\left(1-\rho^{2}\right)}\right)+\bm{\Phi}_{2}\left(-a,-b,\rho\right)+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(aT_{1}+\rho^{2}bT_{2}\right)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[z_{2}^{2}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq a;z_{2}\geq b\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{\rho\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}}{2\pi}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}-2\rho ab+b^{2}}{2\left(1-\rho^{2}\right)}\right)+\bm{\Phi}_{2}\left(-a,-b,\rho\right)+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(bT_{2}+\rho^{2}aT_{1}\right)

Proof.

Again, we write the expectation in the integral form to get that

\mathbb{E}\left[z_{1}^{2}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq a;z_{2}\geq b\right\}\right]=\int_{b}^{\infty}\int_{a}^{\infty}z_{1}^{2}f\left(z_{1},z_{2}\right)dz_{1}dz_{2}=\int_{b}^{\infty}\left(\int_{a}^{\infty}z_{1}^{2}f\left(z_{1}\mid z_{2}\right)dz_{1}\right)f\left(z_{2}\right)dz_{2}

Since
z_{1},z_{2}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(0,1\right)
with
\text{Cov}\left(z_{1},z_{2}\right)=\rho
, we have that
z_{1}\mid z_{2}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(\rho z_{2},1-\rho^{2}\right)
. Define
\rho^{\prime}=1-\rho^{2}
. By Lemma
D.15
, we have that

\displaystyle\int_{a}^{\infty}z_{1}^{2}f\left(z_{1}\mid z_{2}\right)dz_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi\rho^{\prime}}}\int_{a}^{\infty}z_{1}^{2}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(z_{1}-\rho z_{2}\right)^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)dz_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi\rho^{\prime}}}\left(\rho^{\prime}\left(a+\rho z_{2}\right)\exp\left(-\frac{\left(a-\rho z_{2}\right)^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)+\sqrt{2\pi\rho^{\prime}}\left(\rho^{\prime}+\rho^{2}z_{2}^{2}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho z_{2}-a}{\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\rho^{2}z_{2}^{2}\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho z_{2}-a}{\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}}\right)+\rho z_{2}\sqrt{\frac{\rho^{\prime}}{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(a-\rho z_{2}\right)^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+a\sqrt{\frac{\rho^{\prime}}{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(a-\rho z_{2}\right)^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)+\rho^{\prime}\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho z_{2}-a}{\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}}\right)

where we set
\kappa=\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}
and
\mu=\rho z_{2}
in Lemma
D.15
. Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[z_{1}^{2}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq a;z_{2}\geq b\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=\rho^{2}\int_{b}^{\infty}z_{2}^{2}\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho z_{2}-a}{\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}}\right)f\left(z_{2}\right)dz_{2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\rho\sqrt{\frac{\rho^{\prime}}{2\pi}}\int_{b}^{\infty}z_{2}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(a-\rho z_{2}\right)^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)f\left(z_{2}\right)dz_{2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+a\sqrt{\frac{\rho^{\prime}}{2\pi}}\int_{b}^{\infty}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(a-\rho z_{2}\right)^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)f\left(z_{2}\right)dz_{2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\rho^{\prime}\int_{b}^{\infty}\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho z_{2}-a}{\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}}\right)f\left(z_{2}\right)dz_{2}

To start, by Lemma
D.16
, we have

\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho z_{2}-a}{\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}}\right)f\left(z_{2}\right)=\int_{a}^{\infty}f\left(z_{1}\mid z_{2}\right)f\left(z_{2}\right)dz_{1}=\int_{a}^{\infty}f\left(z_{1},z_{2}\right)dz_{1}

Therefore, for the first term, we have

\int_{b}^{\infty}z_{2}^{2}\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho z_{2}-a}{\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}}\right)f\left(z_{2}\right)dz_{2}=\int_{b}^{\infty}\int_{a}^{\infty}z_{2}^{2}f\left(z_{1},z_{2}\right)dz_{2}=\mathbb{E}\left[z_{2}^{2}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq a;z_{2}\geq b\right\}\right]

For the last term, we have

\int_{b}^{\infty}\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho z_{2}-a}{\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}}\right)f\left(z_{2}\right)dz_{2}=\int_{b}^{\infty}\int_{a}^{\infty}f\left(z_{1},z_{2}\right)dz_{1}dz_{2}=\bm{\Phi}_{2}\left(-a,-b,\rho\right)

Next, we notice that

\exp\left(-\frac{\left(a-\rho z_{2}\right)^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)f\left(z_{2}\right)=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}-2\rho az_{2}+z_{2}^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}}{2}\right)\exp\left(-\frac{\left(z_{2}-\rho a\right)^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)

Therefore, for the second term, we apply Lemma
D.14
to get that

\displaystyle\int_{b}^{\infty}z_{2}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(a-\rho z_{2}\right)^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)f\left(z_{2}\right)dz_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}}{2}\right)\int_{b}^{\infty}z_{2}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(z_{2}-\rho a\right)^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)dz_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}}{2}\right)\left(\rho^{\prime}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(\rho a-b\right)^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)+\sqrt{2\pi\rho^{\prime}}\rho a\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho a-b}{\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\rho^{\prime}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}-2\rho ab+b^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)+\rho a\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}}{2}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho a-b}{\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}}\right)

where we set
\kappa=\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}
and
\mu=\rho a
in Lemma
D.14
. For the third term, we have

\displaystyle\int_{b}^{\infty}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(a-\rho z_{2}\right)^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)f\left(z_{2}\right)dz_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}}{2}\right)\int_{b}^{\infty}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(z_{2}-\rho a\right)^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)dz_{2}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}}{2}\right)\int_{b}^{\infty}f\left(z_{2}\mid z_{1}=a\right)dz_{2}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}}{2}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho a-b}{\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}}\right)

Combining all four terms gives

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[z_{1}^{2}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq a;z_{2}\geq b\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=\rho\sqrt{\frac{\rho^{\prime}}{2\pi}}\left(\frac{\rho^{\prime}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}-2\rho ab+b^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)+\rho a\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}}{2}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho a-b}{\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+a\sqrt{\frac{\rho^{\prime}}{2\pi}}\cdot\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}}{2}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho a-b}{\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\rho^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[z_{2}^{2}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq a;z_{2}\geq b\right\}\right]+\rho^{\prime}\bm{\Phi}_{2}\left(-a,-b,\rho\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\rho^{\prime\frac{3}{2}}\rho}{2\pi}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}-2\rho ab+b^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)+\frac{\rho^{\prime}a}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(\rho^{2}+1\right)\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}}{2}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho a-b}{\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\rho^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[z_{2}^{2}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq a;z_{2}\geq b\right\}\right]+\rho^{\prime}\bm{\Phi}_{2}\left(-a,-b,\rho\right)

Therefore, we can conclude that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[z_{1}^{2}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq a;z_{2}\geq b\right\}\right]-\rho^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[z_{2}^{2}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq a;z_{2}\geq b\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad=\frac{\rho^{\prime\frac{3}{2}}\rho}{2\pi}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}-2\rho ab+b^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)+\frac{\rho^{\prime}a}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(\rho^{2}+1\right)T_{1}+\rho^{\prime}\bm{\Phi}_{2}\left(-a,-b,\rho\right)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[z_{2}^{2}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq a;z_{2}\geq b\right\}\right]-\rho^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[z_{1}^{2}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq a;z_{2}\geq b\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad=\frac{\rho^{\prime\frac{3}{2}}\rho}{2\pi}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}-2\rho ab+b^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)+\frac{\rho^{\prime}b}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(\rho^{2}+1\right)T_{2}+\rho^{\prime}\bm{\Phi}_{2}\left(-a,-b,\rho\right)

Solving for
\mathbb{E}\left[z_{1}^{2}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq a;z_{2}\geq b\right\}\right]
and
\mathbb{E}\left[z_{2}^{2}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq a;z_{2}\geq b\right\}\right]
gives

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[z_{1}^{2}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq a;z_{2}\geq b\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{\rho\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}}{2\pi}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}-2\rho ab+b^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)+\bm{\Phi}_{2}\left(-a,-b,\rho\right)+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(aT_{1}+\rho^{2}bT_{2}\right)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[z_{2}^{2}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq a;z_{2}\geq b\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{\rho\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}}{2\pi}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}-2\rho ab+b^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)+\bm{\Phi}_{2}\left(-a,-b,\rho\right)+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(bT_{2}+\rho^{2}aT_{1}\right)

Write
\rho^{\prime}=1-\rho^{2}
gives the desired result.
∎

Lemma D.8
.

Let
z_{1},z_{2}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(0,1\right)
with
\text{Cov}\left(z_{1},z_{2}\right)=\rho
. Let
a,b\in\mathbb{R}
. Define

T_{1}=\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}}{2}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho a-b}{\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}}\right);\quad T_{2}=\exp\left(-\frac{b^{2}}{2}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho b-a}{\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}}\right)

Then we have that

\mathbb{E}\left[z_{1}z_{2}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq a;z_{2}\geq b\right\}\right]=\frac{\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}}{2\pi}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}-2\rho ab+b^{2}}{2\left(1-\rho^{2}\right)}\right)+\rho\bm{\Phi}_{2}\left(-a,-b,\rho\right)+\frac{\rho}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(aT_{1}+bT_{2}\right)

Proof.

To start, we write out the integral form of the expectation as

\mathbb{E}\left[z_{1}z_{2}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq a;z_{2}\geq b\right\}\right]=\int_{b}^{\infty}\int_{a}^{\infty}z_{1}z_{2}f\left(z_{1},z_{2}\right)dz_{1}dz_{2}=\int_{b}^{\infty}\left(\int_{a}^{\infty}z_{1}f\left(z_{1}\mid z_{2}\right)dz_{1}\right)z_{2}f\left(z_{2}\right)dz_{2}

Since
z_{1},z_{2}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(0,1\right)
with covariance
\rho
, we have that
z_{1}\mid z_{2}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(\rho z_{2},1-\rho^{2}\right)
. Therefore, let
\rho^{\prime}=1-\rho^{2}
, we have

f\left(z_{1}\mid z_{2}\right)=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi\rho^{\prime}}}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(z_{1}-\rho z_{2}\right)^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)

Thus, by Lemma
D.14
, we have that

\displaystyle\int_{a}^{\infty}z_{1}f\left(z_{1}\mid z_{2}\right)dz_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi\rho^{\prime}}}\int_{a}^{\infty}z_{1}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(z_{1}-\rho z_{2}\right)^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)dz_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi\rho^{\prime}}}\left(\rho^{\prime}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(a-\rho z_{2}\right)^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)+\rho z_{2}\sqrt{2\pi\rho^{\prime}}\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho z_{2}-a}{\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{\rho^{\prime}}{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(a-\rho z_{2}\right)^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)+\rho z_{2}\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho z_{2}-a}{\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}}\right)

where we set
\kappa=\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}
and
\mu=\rho z_{2}
. Therefore

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[z_{1}z_{2}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq a;z_{2}\geq b\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=\int_{b}^{\infty}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\rho^{\prime}}{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(a-\rho z_{2}\right)^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)+\rho z_{2}\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho z_{2}-a}{\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}}\right)\right)z_{2}f\left(z_{2}\right)dz_{2}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{\rho^{\prime}}{2\pi}}\int_{b}^{\infty}z_{2}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(a-\rho z_{2}\right)^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)f\left(z_{2}\right)dz_{2}+\rho\int_{b}^{\infty}z_{2}^{2}\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho z_{2}-a}{\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}}\right)f\left(z_{2}\right)dz_{2}

To start, by Lemma
D.16
, we have

\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho z_{2}-a}{\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}}\right)f\left(z_{2}\right)=\int_{a}^{\infty}f\left(z_{1}\mid z_{2}\right)dz_{1}f\left(z_{2}\right)=\int_{a}^{\infty}f\left(z_{1},z_{2}\right)dz_{1}

Therefore, for the second term, we have

\int_{b}^{\infty}z_{2}^{2}\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho z_{2}-a}{\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}}\right)f\left(z_{2}\right)dz_{2}=\int_{b}^{\infty}\int_{a}^{\infty}z_{2}^{2}f\left(z_{1},z_{2}\right)dz_{1}dz_{2}=\mathbb{E}\left[z_{2}^{2}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq a;z_{2}\geq b\right\}\right]

For the first term, we have

\exp\left(-\frac{\left(a-\rho z_{2}\right)^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)f\left(z_{2}\right)=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{z_{2}^{2}-2\rho az_{2}+a^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}}{2}\right)\exp\left(-\frac{\left(z_{2}-\rho a\right)^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)

Therefore, by Lemma
D.14
, the first term can be written as

\displaystyle\int_{b}^{\infty}z_{2}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(a-\rho z_{2}\right)^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)f\left(z_{2}\right)dz_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}}{2}\right)\int_{b}^{\infty}z_{2}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(z_{2}-\rho a\right)^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)dz_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}}{2}\right)\left(\rho^{\prime}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(\rho a-b\right)^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)+\sqrt{2\pi\rho^{\prime}}\rho a\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho a-b}{\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\rho^{\prime}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}-2\rho ab+b^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)+\rho a\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}}{2}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho a-b}{\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}}\right)

where we set
\kappa=\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}
and
\mu=\rho a
in Lemma
D.14
. Combining the two terms, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[z_{1}z_{2}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq a;z_{2}\geq b\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{\rho^{\prime}}{2\pi}}\left(\frac{\rho^{\prime}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}-2\rho ab+b^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)+\rho a\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}}{2}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho a-b}{\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\rho\mathbb{E}\left[z_{2}^{2}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq a;z_{2}\geq b\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{\rho^{\prime\frac{3}{2}}}{2\pi}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}-2\rho ab+b^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)+\frac{a\rho\rho^{\prime}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}T_{1}+\rho\mathbb{E}\left[z_{2}^{2}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq a;z_{2}\geq b\right\}\right]

From Lemma
D.7
, we have that

\mathbb{E}\left[z_{2}^{2}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq a;z_{2}\geq b\right\}\right]=\frac{\rho\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}}{2\pi}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}-2\rho ab+b^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)+\bm{\Phi}_{2}\left(-a,-b,\rho\right)+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(bT_{2}+\rho^{2}aT_{1}\right)

Therefore

\mathbb{E}\left[z_{1}z_{2}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq a;z_{2}\geq b\right\}\right]=\frac{\sqrt{\rho^{\prime}}}{2\pi}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}-2\rho ab+b^{2}}{2\rho^{\prime}}\right)+\rho\bm{\Phi}_{2}\left(-a,-b,\rho\right)+\frac{\rho}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(aT_{1}+bT_{2}\right)

Write
\rho^{\prime}=1-\rho^{2}
gives the desired result.
∎

Lemma D.9
.

Let
z_{1}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(\mu_{1},\kappa_{1}^{2}\right)
and
z_{2}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(\mu_{2},\kappa_{2}^{2}\right)
, with
\text{Cov}\left(z_{1},z_{2}\right)=\kappa_{1}\kappa_{2}\rho
. Let
a,b\in\mathbb{R}
. Then we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[z_{1}z_{2}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq a;z_{2}\geq b\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=\left(\mu_{1}\mu_{2}+\kappa_{1}\kappa_{2}\rho\right)\bm{\Phi}\left(\frac{\mu_{1}-a}{\kappa_{1}},\frac{\mu_{2}-b}{\kappa_{2}},\rho\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\kappa_{1}\kappa_{2}}{2\pi}\exp\left(-\frac{1}{2\left(1-\rho^{2}\right)}\left(\frac{\left(\mu_{1}-a\right)^{2}}{\kappa_{1}^{2}}-\frac{2\rho}{\kappa_{1}\kappa_{2}}\left(\mu_{1}-a\right)\left(\mu_{2}-b\right)+\frac{\left(\mu_{2}-b\right)^{2}}{\kappa_{2}^{2}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(\left(\kappa_{2}\rho a+\kappa_{1}\mu_{2}\right)T_{1}+\left(\kappa_{1}\rho b+\kappa_{2}\mu_{1}\right)T_{2}\right)

Here
T_{1},T_{2}
are defined as

\displaystyle T_{1}=\exp\left(-\frac{\left(a-\mu_{1}\right)^{2}}{2\kappa_{1}^{2}}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}}\left(\frac{\rho\left(a-\mu_{1}\right)}{\kappa_{1}}-\frac{b-\mu_{2}}{\kappa_{2}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle T_{2}=\exp\left(-\frac{\left(b-\mu_{2}\right)^{2}}{2\kappa_{2}^{2}}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}}\left(\frac{\rho\left(b-\mu_{2}\right)}{\kappa_{2}}-\frac{a-\mu_{1}}{\kappa_{1}}\right)\right)

Proof.

Let
\hat{z}_{1}=\frac{z_{1}-\mu_{1}}{\kappa_{1}}
and
\hat{z}_{1}=\frac{z_{2}-\mu_{2}}{\kappa_{2}}
. Then we have
\hat{z}_{1},\hat{z}_{2}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(0,1\right)
. Moreover,

\text{Cov}\left(\hat{z}_{1},\hat{z}_{2}\right)=\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{z}_{1}\hat{z}_{2}\right]=\frac{1}{\kappa_{1}\kappa_{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(z_{1}-\mu_{1}\right)\left(z_{2}-\mu_{2}\right)\right]=\rho

Since
z_{1}=\kappa_{1}\hat{z}_{1}+\mu_{1}
and
z_{2}=\kappa_{2}\hat{z}_{2}+\mu_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[z_{1}z_{2}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq a;z_{2}\geq b\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\kappa_{1}\hat{z}_{1}+\mu_{1}\right)\left(\kappa_{2}\hat{z}_{2}+\mu_{2}\right)\mathbb{I}\left\{\hat{z}_{1}\geq\frac{a-\mu_{1}}{\kappa_{1}};\hat{z}_{2}\geq\frac{b-\mu_{2}}{\kappa_{2}}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=\kappa_{1}\kappa_{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{z}_{1}\hat{z}_{2}\mathbb{I}\left\{\hat{z}_{1}\geq\hat{a};\hat{z}_{2}\geq\hat{b}\right\}\right]+\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{I}\left\{\hat{z}_{1}\geq\hat{a};\hat{z}_{2}\geq\hat{b}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\kappa_{1}\mu_{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{z}_{1}\mathbb{I}\left\{\hat{z}_{1}\geq\hat{a};\hat{z}_{2}\geq\hat{b}\right\}\right]+\kappa_{2}\mu_{1}\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{z}_{2}\mathbb{I}\left\{\hat{z}_{1}\geq\hat{a};\hat{z}_{2}\geq\hat{b}\right\}\right]

where we re-defined
\hat{a}=\frac{a-\mu_{1}}{\kappa_{1}}
and
\hat{b}=\frac{b-\mu_{2}}{\kappa_{2}}
. By Lemma
D.16
, Lemma
D.6
, and Lemma
D.8
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{z}_{1}\mathbb{I}\left\{\hat{z}_{1}\geq\hat{a};\hat{z}_{2}\geq\hat{b}\right\}\right]=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(T_{1}+\rho T_{2}\right);\quad\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{z}_{2}\mathbb{I}\left\{\hat{z}_{1}\geq\hat{a};\hat{z}_{2}\geq\hat{b}\right\}\right]=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(T_{2}+\rho T_{1}\right)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{z}_{1}\hat{z}_{2}\mathbb{I}\left\{\hat{z}_{1}\geq\hat{a};\hat{z}_{2}\geq\hat{b}\right\}\right]=\frac{\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}}{2\pi}\exp\left(-\frac{\hat{a}^{2}-2\rho\hat{a}\hat{b}+\hat{b}^{2}}{2\left(1-\rho^{2}\right)}\right)+\rho\bm{\Phi}_{2}\left(-\hat{a},-\hat{b},\rho\right)+\frac{\rho}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(\hat{a}T_{1}+\hat{b}T_{2}\right)

and
\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{I}\left\{\hat{z}_{1}\geq\hat{a};\hat{z}_{2}\geq\hat{b}\right\}\right]=\bm{\Phi}_{2}\left(-\hat{a},-\hat{b},\rho\right)
. Here,
T_{1},T_{2}
are defined as

T_{1}=\exp\left(-\frac{\hat{a}^{2}}{2}\right)\bm{\Phi}\left(\frac{\rho\hat{a}-\hat{b}}{\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}}\right);\quad T_{2}=\exp\left(-\frac{\hat{b}^{2}}{2}\right)\bm{\Phi}\left(\frac{\rho\hat{b}-\hat{a}}{\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}}\right)

Plugging in the value of
\hat{a}
and
\hat{b}
gives the desired result.
∎

D.1.4
Multi-variate Coupled Expectation

Lemma D.10
.

Let
{\mathbf{c}}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(\bm{\mu},\kappa^{2}{\mathbf{I}}\right)
, and let
{\mathbf{u}}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
. Define
z={\mathbf{c}}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}
. Then we have

\mathbb{E}\left[{\mathbf{c}}\mathbb{I}\left\{z\geq 0\right\}\right]=\bm{\mu}\Phi_{1}\left(-\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}\right)+\frac{\kappa}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}\right)^{2}}{2\kappa^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}}\right)\cdot\frac{{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}

Proof.

According to the law of total expectation,

\mathbb{E}\left[{\mathbf{c}}\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{c}}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}\geq 0\right\}\right]=\mathbb{E}_{z}\left[\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}}\left[{\mathbf{c}}\mathbb{I}\left\{z\geq 0\right\}\mid z\right]\right]=\mathbb{E}_{z}\left[\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}}\left[{\mathbf{c}}\mid z\right]\mathbb{I}\left\{z\geq 0\right\}\right]

By Lemma
D.1
, we have that
\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}}\left[{\mathbf{c}}\mid z\right]=\bm{\mu}+\frac{{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}}\left(z-\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}\right)
. Therefore

\mathbb{E}\left[{\mathbf{c}}\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{c}}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}\geq 0\right\}\right]=\frac{{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{z}\left[z\mathbb{I}\left\{z\geq 0\right\}\right]+\left(\bm{\mu}-\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}\cdot{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}}\right)\mathbb{E}_{z}\left[\mathbb{I}\left\{z\geq 0\right\}\right]

By definition,
\mathbb{E}_{z}\left[\mathbb{I}\left\{z\geq 0\right\}\right]=\Pr\left(z\geq 0\right)=1-\Pr\left(z\leq 0\right)
. Since, by Lemma
D.13
,
z\sim\mathcal{N}\left(\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}},\kappa^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)
, we have that

\Pr\left(z\leq 0\right)=\Pr\left(\frac{z-\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}\leq-\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}\right)=\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(-\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}\right)

Moreover, let
\hat{z}=\frac{z-\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}
, then we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{z}\left[z\mathbb{I}\left\{z\geq 0\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\hat{z}}\left[\left(\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}\hat{z}+\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}\right)\mathbb{I}\left\{\hat{z}\geq-\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}\mathbb{E}_{\hat{z}}\left[\hat{z}\mathbb{I}\left\{\hat{z}\geq-\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}\right\}\right]+\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}\mathbb{E}_{z}\left[z\geq 0\right]

By the PDF of
\hat{z}
, we have

\mathbb{E}_{\hat{z}}\left[\hat{z}\mathbb{I}\left\{z\geq 0\right\}\right]=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\int_{a}^{\infty}z\exp\left(-\frac{z^{2}}{2}\right)dz=-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{z^{2}}{2}\right)|_{a}^{\infty}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}}{2}\right)

Therefore

\mathbb{E}_{z}\left[z\mathbb{I}\left\{z\geq 0\right\}\right]=\frac{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}\right)^{2}}{2\kappa^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}}\right)+\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}\mathbb{E}_{z}\left[z\geq 0\right]

Plugging in gives

\mathbb{E}\left[{\mathbf{c}}\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{c}}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}\geq 0\right\}\right]=\bm{\mu}\Phi_{1}\left(-\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}\right)+\frac{\kappa}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}\right)^{2}}{2\kappa^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}}\right)\cdot\frac{{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}

∎

Lemma D.11
.

Let
\bm{c}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(\bm{\mu},\kappa^{2}{\mathbf{I}}\right)
with
\kappa\leq 1
. Let
{\mathbf{u}},{\mathbf{v}}
be given, and let
z_{1}={\mathbf{c}}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}},z_{2}={\mathbf{c}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}
. Then we have that

\left\|\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}}\left[{\mathbf{c}}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq 0\right\}\right]-\bm{\mu}\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}\right)\right\|_{\infty}\leq\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}\right)^{2}}{2\kappa^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}\right)

Proof.

Given the form of the conditional expectation, we have

\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}}\left[{\mathbf{c}}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq 0\right\}\right]=\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}}\left[{\mathbf{c}}\mid z_{1}\right]f_{1}(z_{1})dz_{1}=\int_{0}^{\infty}\left(\bm{\mu}+\frac{{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}}\left(z_{1}-\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}\right)\right)f_{1}(z_{1})dz_{1}

Since
z_{1}={\mathbf{c}}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}
, we must have that
z_{1}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}},\kappa^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)
. Define
z^{\prime}=\frac{z_{1}-\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}
, then we have that
z_{1}=\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}z^{\prime}+\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}}\left[{\mathbf{c}}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq 0\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=\int_{\frac{-\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}}^{\infty}\left(\mu+\kappa{\mathbf{u}}\cdot z^{\prime}\right)f\left(z^{\prime}\right)dz^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\mu\cdot\int_{-\infty}^{\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}}f\left(z^{\prime}\right)dz^{\prime}+\kappa{\mathbf{u}}\cdot\int_{-\infty}^{\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}}z^{\prime}f\left(z^{\prime}\right)dz^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\bm{\mu}\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}\right)+\kappa{\mathbf{u}}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}\right)^{2}}{2\kappa^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}\right)

where in the last equality we applied Lemma
D.14
with
a=0
and
\kappa=1
in the lemma. Therefore, we have that

\left\|\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}}\left[{\mathbf{c}}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq 0\right\}\right]-\bm{\mu}\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}\right)\right\|_{\infty}\leq\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{\infty}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}\right)^{2}}{2\kappa^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}\right)

∎

Now, we shall dive into
\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}}\left[{\mathbf{c}}{\mathbf{c}}^{\top}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq 0;z_{2}\geq 0\right\}\right]
.

Lemma D.12
.

Let
\bm{c}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(\bm{\mu},\kappa^{2}{\mathbf{I}}\right)
with
\left\|\bm{\mu}\right\|_{2}\geq 2
and
\kappa\leq 1
. Let
{\mathbf{u}},{\mathbf{v}}
be given, and let
z_{1}=\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}},z_{2}=\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}
. Then we have that

\left\|\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}}\left[{\mathbf{c}}{\mathbf{c}}^{\top}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq 0;z_{2}\geq 0\right\}\right]{\mathbf{v}}-\left(\bm{\mu}\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}+3\kappa^{2}{\mathbf{v}}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\bm{\mu^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}}}{2\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\bm{\mu^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}}}{2\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}\right)\right\|\leq\Delta

where
\Delta
is given by

\Delta=2\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}\left(\left\|\bm{\mu}\right\|_{2}\left(\phi\left(\frac{\bm{\mu^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}}}{2\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}\right)+\phi\left(\frac{\bm{\mu^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}}}{2\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}\right)\right)+\left\|\bm{\mu}\right\|_{\infty}\left(\psi\left(\frac{\bm{\mu^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}}}{2\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}\right)+\psi\left(\frac{\bm{\mu^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}}}{2\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}\right)\right)\right)

Proof.

We have

\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}}\left[{\mathbf{c}}{\mathbf{c}}^{\top}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq 0;z_{2}\geq 0\right\}\right]=\int_{z_{1},z_{2}\geq 0}\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}}\left[{\mathbf{c}}{\mathbf{c}}^{\top}\mid z_{1},z_{2}\right]f\left(z_{1},z_{2}\right)dz_{1}dz_{2}

Notice that

\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}}\left[{\mathbf{c}}{\mathbf{c}}^{\top}\mid z_{1},z_{2}\right]=\text{Cov}\left({\mathbf{c}}\mid z_{1},z_{2}\right)+\mathbb{E}[{\mathbf{c}}\mid z_{1},z_{2}]\mathbb{E}[{\mathbf{c}}\mid z_{1},z_{2}]^{\top}

By the form of
\text{Cov}\left({\mathbf{c}}\mid z_{1},z_{2}\right)
, we have

\text{Cov}\left({\mathbf{c}}\mid z_{1},z_{2}\right)=\kappa^{2}{\mathbf{I}}-\frac{\kappa^{2}\left({\mathbf{u}}{\mathbf{v}}^{\top}-{\mathbf{v}}{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}\right)^{2}}{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left\langle{\mathbf{v}},{\mathbf{u}}\right\rangle^{2}}:={\mathbf{M}}

By the form of
\mathbb{E}[{\mathbf{c}}\mid z_{1},z_{2}]
we have

\mathbb{E}[{\mathbf{c}}\mid z_{1},z_{2}]=z_{1}\cdot{\mathbf{s}}_{1}+z_{2}\cdot{\mathbf{s}}_{2}+{\mathbf{s}}_{3}

where

\displaystyle{\mathbf{s}}_{1}=\frac{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}{\mathbf{u}}-\left\langle{\mathbf{u}},{\mathbf{v}}\right\rangle{\mathbf{v}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left\langle{\mathbf{v}},{\mathbf{u}}\right\rangle^{2}};{\mathbf{s}}_{2}=\frac{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}{\mathbf{v}}-\left\langle{\mathbf{u}},{\mathbf{v}}\right\rangle{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left\langle{\mathbf{v}},{\mathbf{u}}\right\rangle^{2}}

\displaystyle{\mathbf{s}}_{3}=\bm{\mu}-\frac{\left(\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}{\mathbf{u}}-\left\langle{\mathbf{u}},{\mathbf{v}}\right\rangle{\mathbf{v}}\right)\left\langle\bm{\mu},{\mathbf{u}}\right\rangle+\left(\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}{\mathbf{v}}-\left\langle{\mathbf{u}},{\mathbf{v}}\right\rangle{\mathbf{u}}\right)\left\langle\bm{\mu},{\mathbf{v}}\right\rangle}{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left\langle{\mathbf{v}},{\mathbf{u}}\right\rangle^{2}}

Therefore

\mathbb{E}\left[{\mathbf{c}}\mid z_{1},z_{2}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[{\mathbf{c}}\mid z_{1},z_{2}\right]^{\top}=\left(z_{1}\cdot{\mathbf{s}}_{1}+z_{2}\cdot{\mathbf{s}}_{2}+{\mathbf{s}}_{3}\right)\left(z_{1}\cdot{\mathbf{s}}_{1}+z_{2}\cdot{\mathbf{s}}_{2}+{\mathbf{s}}_{3}\right)^{\top}

Recall that we are interested in

\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}}\left[{\mathbf{c}}{\mathbf{c}}^{\top}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq 0;z_{2}\geq 0\right\}\right]=\int_{z_{1},z_{2}\geq 0}\left(\text{Cov}\left({\mathbf{c}}\mid z_{1},z_{2}\right)+\mathbb{E}[{\mathbf{c}}\mid z_{1},z_{2}]\mathbb{E}[{\mathbf{c}}\mid z_{1},z_{2}]^{\top}\right)f(z_{1},z_{2})dz_{1}dz_{2}

Let

\hat{z}_{1}=\frac{z_{1}-\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}};\quad\hat{z}_{2}=\frac{z_{1}-\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}

Then we have

\hat{z}_{1},\hat{z}_{2}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,1);\quad\text{Cov}\left(\hat{z}_{1},\hat{z}_{2}\right)=\frac{{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}:=\rho

Thus,

\hat{z}_{1}\mid\hat{z}_{2}=\gamma_{2}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(\rho\gamma_{2},1-\rho^{2}\right);\quad\hat{z}_{2}\mid\hat{z}_{1}=\gamma_{1}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(\rho\gamma_{1},1-\rho^{2}\right)

Moreover,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[{\mathbf{c}}\mid z_{1},z_{2}]

\displaystyle=z_{1}\cdot{\mathbf{s}}_{1}+z_{2}\cdot{\mathbf{s}}_{2}+{\mathbf{s}}_{3}

\displaystyle=\left(\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}\hat{z}_{1}+\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}\right){\mathbf{s}}_{1}+\left(\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}\hat{z}_{2}+\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\right){\mathbf{s}}_{2}+{\mathbf{s}}_{3}

Redefine

\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{1}=\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}{\mathbf{s}}_{1};\quad\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{2}=\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}{\mathbf{s}}_{2};\quad\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{3}=\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}\cdot{\mathbf{s}}_{1}+\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{s}}_{2}+{\mathbf{s}}_{3}=\bm{\mu}

Then we have

\mathbb{E}[{\mathbf{c}}\mid z_{1},z_{2}]=\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{1}\hat{z}_{1}+\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{2}\hat{z}_{2}+\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{3}

In this case, let
\hat{f}
be the joint PDF of
\hat{z}_{1}
and
\hat{z}_{2}
, then we have

\displaystyle\hat{f}\left(\hat{z}_{1},\hat{z}_{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\pi\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}}\exp\left(-\frac{1}{2(1-\rho^{2})}\left(z_{1}^{2}-2\rho z_{1}z_{2}+z_{2}^{2}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\kappa^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}{2\pi\kappa^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}}\exp\left(-\frac{1}{2(1-\rho^{2})}\left(z_{1}^{2}-2\rho z_{1}z_{2}+z_{2}^{2}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\kappa^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}f\left(\hat{z}_{1},\hat{z}_{2}\right)

Therefore, since
dz_{1}=\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}d\hat{z}_{1}
, and
dz_{2}=\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}d\hat{z}_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}}\left[{\mathbf{c}}{\mathbf{c}}^{\top}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq 0;z_{2}\geq 0\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=\int_{z_{1},z_{2}\geq 0}\left(\text{Cov}\left({\mathbf{c}}\mid z_{1},z_{2}\right)+\mathbb{E}[{\mathbf{c}}\mid z_{1},z_{2}]\mathbb{E}[{\mathbf{c}}\mid z_{1},z_{2}]^{\top}\right)f(z_{1},z_{2})dz_{1}dz_{2}

\displaystyle=\int_{-\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}}^{\infty}\int_{-\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}}^{\infty}\left(\text{Cov}\left({\mathbf{c}}\mid z_{1},z_{2}\right)+\mathbb{E}[{\mathbf{c}}\mid z_{1},z_{2}]\mathbb{E}[{\mathbf{c}}\mid z_{1},z_{2}]^{\top}\right)\hat{f}(\hat{z}_{1},\hat{z}_{2})d\hat{z}_{1}d\hat{z}_{2}

\displaystyle=\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{1}\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{1}^{\top}\underbrace{\int_{-\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}}^{\infty}\int_{-\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}}^{\infty}\hat{z}_{1}^{2}\hat{f}(\hat{z}_{1},\hat{z}_{2})d\hat{z}_{1}d\hat{z}_{2}}_{\mathcal{I}_{1}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{2}\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{2}^{\top}\underbrace{\int_{-\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}}^{\infty}\int_{-\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}}^{\infty}\hat{z}_{2}^{2}\hat{f}(\hat{z}_{1},\hat{z}_{2})d\hat{z}_{1}d\hat{z}_{2}}_{\mathcal{I}_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\left(\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{1}\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{2}^{\top}+\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{2}\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{1}^{\top}\right)\underbrace{\int_{-\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}}^{\infty}\int_{-\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}}^{\infty}\hat{z}_{1}\hat{z}_{2}\hat{f}(\hat{z}_{1},\hat{z}_{2})d\hat{z}_{1}d\hat{z}_{2}}_{\mathcal{I}_{3}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\left(\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{1}\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{3}+\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{3}\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{1}^{\top}\right)\underbrace{\int_{-\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}}^{\infty}\int_{-\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}}^{\infty}\hat{z}_{1}\hat{f}(\hat{z}_{1},\hat{z}_{2})d\hat{z}_{1}d\hat{z}_{2}}_{\mathcal{I}_{4}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\left(\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{2}\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{3}^{\top}+\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{3}\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{2}^{\top}\right)\underbrace{\int_{-\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}}^{\infty}\int_{-\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}}^{\infty}\hat{z}_{2}\hat{f}(\hat{z}_{1},\hat{z}_{2})d\hat{z}_{1}d\hat{z}_{2}}_{\mathcal{I}_{5}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\left(\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{3}\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{3}^{\top}+{\mathbf{M}}\right)\underbrace{\int_{-\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}}^{\infty}\int_{-\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}}^{\infty}\hat{f}(\hat{z}_{1},\hat{z}_{2})d\hat{z}_{1}d\hat{z}_{2}}_{\mathcal{I}_{6}}

Since our goal is to study the term
\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}}\left[{\mathbf{c}}{\mathbf{c}}^{\top}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq 0;z_{2}\geq 0\right\}\right]{\mathbf{v}}
, we need to understand the terms
\mathcal{I}_{1}
to
\mathcal{I}_{6}
, as well as understanding the matrix-vector product in front of these terms. To start, under some standard computation, we have

\displaystyle\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{1}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}

\displaystyle=\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}\cdot\frac{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}-{\mathbf{v}}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}\cdot{\mathbf{v}}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left({\mathbf{v}}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}\right)^{2}}=\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{1}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}

\displaystyle=\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}\cdot\frac{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}-{\mathbf{v}}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}\cdot{\mathbf{v}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left({\mathbf{v}}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}\right)^{2}}=0

\displaystyle\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{2}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}

\displaystyle=\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}\cdot\frac{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}{\mathbf{v}}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}-{\mathbf{v}}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}\cdot{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left({\mathbf{v}}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}\right)^{2}}=0

\displaystyle\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{2}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}

\displaystyle=\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}\cdot\frac{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}{\mathbf{v}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}-{\mathbf{v}}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}\cdot{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left({\mathbf{v}}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}\right)^{2}}=\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}

Therefore, the following must holds

\displaystyle\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{1}\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{1}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}=\bm{0};\quad\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{2}\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{2}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}=\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{2};\quad\left(\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{1}\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{2}^{\top}+\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{2}\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{1}^{\top}\right){\mathbf{v}}=\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{1};

\displaystyle\left(\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{1}\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{3}^{\top}+\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{3}\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{1}^{\top}\right){\mathbf{v}}=\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\cdot\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{1};\quad\left(\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{2}\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{3}^{\top}+\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{3}\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{2}^{\top}\right){\mathbf{v}}=\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\cdot\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{2}+\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}\bm{\mu}

Lastly, we have

\displaystyle\left(\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{3}\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{3}^{\top}+{\mathbf{M}}\right){\mathbf{v}}

\displaystyle=\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\cdot\bm{\mu}+\kappa^{2}{\mathbf{v}}-\frac{\kappa^{2}}{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left({\mathbf{v}}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}\right)^{2}}\left({\mathbf{u}}{\mathbf{v}}^{\top}-{\mathbf{v}}{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}\right)^{2}{\mathbf{v}}

\displaystyle=\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\cdot\bm{\mu}+\kappa^{2}{\mathbf{v}}-\frac{\kappa^{2}}{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left({\mathbf{v}}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}\right)^{2}}\left({\mathbf{u}}{\mathbf{v}}^{\top}-{\mathbf{v}}{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}\right)\left({\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{v}}-\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}{\mathbf{u}}\right)

\displaystyle=\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\cdot\bm{\mu}+\kappa^{2}{\mathbf{v}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad-\frac{\kappa^{2}}{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left({\mathbf{v}}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}\right)^{2}}\left({\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\cdot\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}{\mathbf{u}}-\left({\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\right)^{2}{\mathbf{v}}-{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\cdot\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}{\mathbf{u}}+\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}{\mathbf{v}}\right)

\displaystyle=\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\cdot\bm{\mu}+\kappa^{2}{\mathbf{v}}+\kappa^{2}{\mathbf{v}}

\displaystyle=\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\cdot\bm{\mu}+2\kappa^{2}{\mathbf{v}}

Therefore, we can write
\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}}\left[{\mathbf{c}}{\mathbf{c}}^{\top}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq 0;z_{2}\geq 0\right\}\right]{\mathbf{v}}
as
