\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}}\left[{\mathbf{c}}{\mathbf{c}}^{\top}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq 0;z_{2}\geq 0\right\}\right]{\mathbf{v}}

\displaystyle=\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{2}\cdot\mathcal{I}_{2}+\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{1}\cdot\mathcal{I}_{3}+\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\cdot\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{1}\cdot\mathcal{I}_{4}

(72)

\displaystyle+\left(\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\cdot\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{2}+\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}\bm{\mu}\right)\mathcal{I}_{5}+\left(\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\cdot\bm{\mu}+2\kappa^{2}{\mathbf{v}}\right)\mathcal{I}_{6}

\displaystyle=\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}\left(\mathcal{I}_{2}\cdot\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{2}+\mathcal{I}_{3}\cdot\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{1}\right)+\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\left(\mathcal{I}_{4}\cdot\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{1}+\mathcal{I}_{5}\cdot\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\left(\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}\cdot\mathcal{I}_{5}+\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\cdot\mathcal{I}_{6}\right)\bm{\mu}+2\kappa^{2}\mathcal{I}_{6}{\mathbf{v}}

By the definition of
\mathcal{I}_{2}
to
\mathcal{I}_{6}
, we first notice that

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{6}

\displaystyle=\int_{-\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}}^{\infty}\int_{-\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}}^{\infty}f(\hat{z}_{1},\hat{z}_{2})d\hat{z}_{1}d\hat{z}_{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\left(\hat{z}_{1}\geq-\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}};\hat{z}_{2}\geq-\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\left(\hat{z}_{1}\leq\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}};\hat{z}_{2}\leq\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=\Phi_{2}\left(\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}},\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}},\rho\right)

Moreover, we can invoke Lemma
D.6
, Lemma
D.7
, and Lemma
D.8
to get that

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{4}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(T_{1}+\rho T_{2}\right);\quad\mathcal{I}_{5}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(T_{2}+\rho T_{1}\right)

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{2}=\frac{\rho\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}}{2\pi}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}-2\rho ab+b^{2}}{2\left(1-\rho^{2}\right)}\right)+\bm{\Phi}_{2}\left(-a,-b,\rho\right)+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(bT_{2}+\rho^{2}aT_{1}\right)

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{3}=\frac{\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}}{2\pi}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}-2\rho ab+b^{2}}{2\left(1-\rho^{2}\right)}\right)+\rho\bm{\Phi}_{2}\left(-a,-b,\rho\right)+\frac{\rho}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(aT_{1}+bT_{2}\right)

where
T_{1},T_{2}
and
a,b
are defined as

\displaystyle T_{1}=\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}}{2}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho a-b}{\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}}\right);\quad T_{2}=\exp\left(-\frac{b^{2}}{2}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho b-a}{\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}}\right)

\displaystyle a=-\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}};\quad b=-\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}};\quad\rho=\frac{{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}

To ease our computation, we define

E=\frac{1-\rho^{2}}{2\pi}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}-2\rho ab+b^{2}}{2\left(1-\rho^{2}\right)}\right);F=\bm{\Phi}_{2}\left(-a,-b,\rho\right)

Then the terms
\mathcal{I}_{2}
to
\mathcal{I}_{6}
can be written as

\begin{gathered}\mathcal{I}_{2}=\rho E+F+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(bT_{2}+\rho^{2}aT_{1}\right);\quad\mathcal{I}_{3}=E+\rho F+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(bT_{2}+aT_{1}\right)\\
\mathcal{I}_{4}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(T_{1}+\rho T_{2}\right);\quad\mathcal{I}_{5}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(T_{2}+\rho T_{1}\right);\quad\mathcal{I}_{6}=F\end{gathered}

(73)

Now, the trick of evaluating (
72
) is to re-write
\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{1}
and
\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{2}
as below

\displaystyle\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left({\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\right)^{2}}\cdot\left(\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}{\mathbf{u}}-{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{v}}\right)

(74)

\displaystyle=\frac{\kappa\left(\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}{\mathbf{u}}-{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{v}}\right)}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left(1-\rho^{2}\right)}

\displaystyle=\frac{\kappa}{1-\rho^{2}}\cdot\frac{{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}-\frac{\kappa\rho}{1-\rho^{2}}\cdot\frac{{\mathbf{v}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\kappa}{1-\rho^{2}}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}-\rho\cdot\frac{{\mathbf{v}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left({\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\right)^{2}}\cdot\left(\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}{\mathbf{v}}-{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{u}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\kappa\left(\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}{\mathbf{v}}-{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{u}}\right)}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left(1-\rho^{2}\right)}

\displaystyle=\frac{\kappa}{1-\rho^{2}}\cdot\frac{{\mathbf{v}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|}_{2}-\frac{\kappa\rho}{1-\rho^{2}}\cdot\frac{{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\kappa}{1-\rho^{2}}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{v}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}-\rho\cdot\frac{{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}\right)

Now, we can simplify (
72
) with (
73
) and (
74
). To start, for the terms
\mathcal{I}_{2}\cdot\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{2}+\mathcal{I}_{3}\cdot\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{1}
we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{2}\cdot\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{2}+\mathcal{I}_{3}\cdot\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{\kappa}{1-\rho^{2}}\left(\rho E+F+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(bT_{2}+\rho^{2}aT_{1}\right)\right)\left(\frac{{\mathbf{v}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}-\rho\cdot\frac{{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\frac{\kappa}{1-\rho^{2}}\left(E+\rho F+\frac{\rho}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(bT_{2}+aT_{1}\right)\right)\left(\frac{{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}-\rho\cdot\frac{{\mathbf{v}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\kappa E}{1-\rho^{2}}\left(\rho\left(\frac{{\mathbf{v}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}-\rho\cdot\frac{{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}\right)+\left(\frac{{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}-\rho\cdot\frac{{\mathbf{v}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\frac{\kappa F}{1-\rho^{2}}\left(\left(\frac{{\mathbf{v}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}-\rho\cdot\frac{{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}\right)+\rho\left(\frac{{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}-\rho\cdot\frac{{\mathbf{v}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\frac{\kappa\rho aT_{1}}{\sqrt{2\pi}\left(1-\rho^{2}\right)}\left(\rho\left(\frac{{\mathbf{v}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}-\rho\cdot\frac{{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}\right)+\left(\frac{{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}-\rho\cdot\frac{{\mathbf{v}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\frac{\kappa bT_{2}}{\sqrt{2\pi}\left(1-\rho^{2}\right)}\left(\left(\frac{{\mathbf{v}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}-\rho\cdot\frac{{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}\right)+\rho\left(\frac{{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}-\rho\cdot\frac{{\mathbf{v}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\kappa E\cdot\frac{{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}+\kappa F\cdot\frac{{\mathbf{v}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}+\frac{\kappa\rho aT_{1}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\cdot\frac{{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}+\frac{\kappa bT_{2}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\cdot\frac{{\mathbf{v}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}

\displaystyle=\kappa\left(\left(E+\frac{\rho aT_{1}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\right)\frac{{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}+\left(F+\frac{bT_{2}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\right)\frac{{\mathbf{v}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}\right)

Similarly, for the term
\mathcal{I}_{4}\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{1}+\mathcal{I}_{4}\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{4}\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{1}+\mathcal{I}_{4}\hat{{\mathbf{s}}}_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{\kappa}{\sqrt{2\pi}\left(1-\rho^{2}\right)}\left(\left(T_{1}+\rho T_{2}\right)\left(\frac{{\mathbf{v}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}-\rho\cdot\frac{{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}\right)+\left(T_{2}+\rho T_{1}\right)\left(\frac{{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}-\rho\cdot\frac{{\mathbf{v}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\kappa T_{1}}{\sqrt{2\pi}\left(1-\rho^{2}\right)}\left(\left(\frac{{\mathbf{v}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}-\rho\cdot\frac{{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}\right)+\rho\left(\frac{{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}-\rho\cdot\frac{{\mathbf{v}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\frac{\kappa T_{2}}{\sqrt{2\pi}\left(1-\rho^{2}\right)}\left(\rho\left(\frac{{\mathbf{v}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}-\rho\cdot\frac{{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}\right)+\left(\frac{{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}-\rho\cdot\frac{{\mathbf{v}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\kappa T_{1}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\cdot\frac{{\mathbf{v}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}+\frac{\kappa T_{2}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\cdot\frac{{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\kappa}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(T_{1}\cdot\frac{{\mathbf{v}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}+T_{2}\cdot\frac{{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}\right)

Applying these evaluations, (
72
) becomes

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}}\left[{\mathbf{c}}{\mathbf{c}}^{\top}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq 0;z_{2}\geq 0\right\}\right]{\mathbf{v}}

\displaystyle=\kappa^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}\left(\left(E+\frac{\rho aT_{1}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\right)\frac{{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}+\left(F+\frac{bT_{2}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\right)\frac{{\mathbf{v}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}\right)

(75)

\displaystyle+\frac{\kappa\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(T_{1}\cdot\frac{{\mathbf{v}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}+T_{2}\cdot\frac{{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(T_{2}+\rho T_{1}\right)\bm{\mu}+\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\cdot F\cdot\bm{\mu}+2\kappa^{2}F{\mathbf{v}}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\kappa^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}\left(E\cdot\frac{{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}+\frac{\rho aT_{1}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\cdot\frac{{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}+\frac{bT_{2}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\cdot\frac{{\mathbf{v}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}\right)}_{{\mathbf{g}}_{1}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\frac{\kappa}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\left(T_{1}\cdot\frac{{\mathbf{v}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}+T_{2}\cdot\frac{{\mathbf{u}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}\right)+\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}\left(T_{2}+\rho T_{1}\right)\bm{\mu}\right)}_{{\mathbf{g}}_{2}}

\displaystyle+F\left(\bm{\mu}\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}+3\kappa^{2}{\mathbf{v}}\right)

Then we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}}\left[{\mathbf{c}}{\mathbf{c}}^{\top}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq 0;z_{2}\geq 0\right\}\right]{\mathbf{v}}-\left(\bm{\mu}\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}+3\kappa^{2}{\mathbf{v}}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}\right)

(76)

\displaystyle={\mathbf{g}}_{1}+{\mathbf{g}}_{2}+\mathcal{C}\left(\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}},\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}},\frac{{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}\right)

The proof then proceed by estimating the magnitude of the three terms. To start, we need to bound
T_{1}
and
T_{2}
. In particular, since
\bm{\Phi}_{1}
is the CDF, its magnitude must be bounded by
1
. Therefore

0\leq T_{1}\leq\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}}{2}\right);\quad 0\leq T_{2}\leq\exp\left(-\frac{b^{2}}{2}\right)

Therefore, the
\ell_{\infty}
norm of
{\mathbf{g}}_{2}
is bounded by

\displaystyle\left\|{\mathbf{g}}_{2}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\kappa}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\left(\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}}{2}\right)+\exp\left(-\frac{b^{2}}{2}\right)\right)\right)+\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}\left\|\bm{\mu}\right\|_{2}\left(\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}}{2}\right)+\rho\exp\left(-\frac{b^{2}}{2}\right)\right)

(77)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\kappa}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}\left\|\bm{\mu}\right\|_{2}\left(\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}}{2}\right)+\exp\left(-\frac{b^{2}}{2}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}\left\|\bm{\mu}\right\|_{2}\left(\phi\left(\frac{a}{2}\right)+\phi\left(\frac{b}{2}\right)\right)

Next, for
E
, we have

\displaystyle E

\displaystyle=\frac{1-\rho^{2}}{2\pi}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}-2\rho ab+b^{2}}{2\left(1-\rho^{2}\right)}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1-\rho^{2}}{4\pi}\left(\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}-2\rho ab+\rho^{2}b^{2}}{2\left(1-\rho^{2}\right)}-\frac{b^{2}}{2}\right)+\exp\left(-\frac{\rho^{2}a^{2}-2\rho ab+b^{2}}{2\left(1-\rho^{2}\right)}-\frac{a^{2}}{2}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{4\pi}\left(\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}}{2}\right)+\exp\left(-\frac{b^{2}}{2}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{4\pi}\left(\phi\left(\frac{a}{2}\right)+\phi\left(\frac{b}{2}\right)\right)

Therefore, the magnitude of
{\mathbf{g}}_{1}
can be bounded by

\displaystyle\left\|{\mathbf{g}}_{1}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\kappa^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}\left(\left|E\right|+\frac{\left|a\right|T_{1}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}+\frac{\left|b\right|T_{2}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\right)

(78)

\displaystyle\leq\kappa^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}\left(\frac{1}{4\pi}\left(\phi\left(\frac{a}{2}\right)+\phi\left(\frac{b}{2}\right)\right)+\frac{\left|a\right|}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}}{2}\right)+\frac{\left|b\right|}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{b^{2}}{2}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\kappa^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}\left(\frac{1}{4\pi}\left(\phi\left(\frac{a}{2}\right)+\phi\left(\frac{b}{2}\right)\right)+\psi\left(\frac{a}{2}\right)+\psi\left(\frac{b}{2}\right)\right)

Moreover, by the bound of the Gaussian Copula function, we have that

\left|\mathcal{C}\left(a,b,\rho\right)\right|\leq\frac{1}{4}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}+b^{2}}{4}\right)

Therefore, we have that

\mathcal{C}\left(\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}},\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}},\frac{{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}}{\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}}\right)\leq\frac{1}{4}\exp\left(-\frac{a^{2}}{4}\right)\exp\left(-\frac{b^{2}}{4}\right)=\frac{1}{4}\phi\left(\frac{a}{2}\right)\phi\left(\frac{b}{2}\right)

Combining the results gives

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{c}}}\left[{\mathbf{c}}{\mathbf{c}}^{\top}\mathbb{I}\left\{z_{1}\geq 0;z_{2}\geq 0\right\}\right]{\mathbf{v}}-\left(\bm{\mu}\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}+3\kappa^{2}{\mathbf{v}}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}}{\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}},\right)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\leq\kappa^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}\left(\frac{1}{4\pi}\left(\phi\left(\frac{a}{2}\right)+\phi\left(\frac{b}{2}\right)\right)+\psi\left(\frac{a}{2}\right)+\psi\left(\frac{b}{2}\right)\right)+\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}\left\|\bm{\mu}\right\|_{2}\left(\phi\left(\frac{a}{2}\right)+\phi\left(\frac{b}{2}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\frac{1}{4}\left(\left|\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}\right|\left\|\bm{\mu}\right\|_{\infty}+3\kappa^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}\right)\phi\left(\frac{a}{2}\right)\phi\left(\frac{b}{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad=\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}\left(\left(\kappa^{2}+\kappa\left\|\bm{\mu}\right\|_{2}\right)\left(\phi\left(\frac{a}{2}\right)+\phi\left(\frac{b}{2}\right)\right)+\left(\kappa^{2}+\kappa\left\|\bm{\mu}\right\|_{\infty}\right)\left(\psi\left(\frac{a}{2}\right)+\psi\left(\frac{b}{2}\right)\right)\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\leq 2\kappa\left\|{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{2}\left(\left\|\bm{\mu}\right\|_{2}\left(\phi\left(\frac{a}{2}\right)+\phi\left(\frac{b}{2}\right)\right)+\left\|\bm{\mu}\right\|_{\infty}\left(\psi\left(\frac{a}{2}\right)+\psi\left(\frac{b}{2}\right)\right)\right)

∎

D.1.5
Other Results

Lemma D.13
.

Let
{\mathbf{c}}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(\bm{\mu},\kappa^{2}{\mathbf{I}}\right)
, and let
{\mathbf{u}}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
be a vector. Define
z={\mathbf{c}}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}
. Then we have that
z\sim\mathcal{N}\left(\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}},\kappa^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)
.

Proof.

Since
z={\mathbf{c}}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}
where
{\mathbf{c}}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(\bm{\mu},\kappa^{2}{\mathbf{I}}\right)
. Then the moment generating function of
z
is given by

\displaystyle M_{z}(t)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(zt\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\prod_{j=1}^{d}\exp\left(c_{j}u_{j}t\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\prod_{j=1}^{d}\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(c_{j}u_{j}t\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\prod_{j=1}^{d}\exp\left(u_{j}\mu_{j}t+\frac{1}{2}u_{j}^{2}\kappa^{2}t^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\exp\left(\left(\sum_{j=1}^{d}u_{j}\mu_{j}\right)t+\frac{1}{2}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{d}u_{j}^{2}\right)\kappa^{2}t^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\exp\left(\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}\cdot t+\frac{1}{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\kappa^{2}t^{2}\right)

Therefore,
z\sim\mathcal{N}\left(\bm{\mu}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}},\kappa^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)
.
∎

Lemma D.14
.

Let
\kappa,\mu,a\in\mathbb{R}
be given such that
\kappa>0
. Then we have that

\int_{a}^{\infty}z\exp\left(-\frac{\left(z-\mu\right)^{2}}{2\kappa^{2}}\right)dz=\kappa^{2}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(\mu-a\right)^{2}}{2\kappa^{2}}\right)+\kappa\mu\sqrt{2\pi}\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\mu-a}{\kappa}\right)

Proof.

We use a change of variable
z^{\prime}=\frac{z-\mu}{\kappa}
. Then we have that
z=\kappa z^{\prime}+\mu
, and
dz=\kappa dz^{\prime}
. Therefore

\displaystyle\int_{a}^{\infty}z\exp\left(-\frac{\left(z-\mu\right)^{2}}{2\kappa^{2}}\right)dz

\displaystyle=\int_{\frac{a-\mu}{\kappa}}^{\infty}\left(\kappa z^{\prime}+\mu\right)\exp\left(-\frac{z^{\prime 2}}{2}\right)\kappa dz^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\kappa^{2}\int_{\frac{a-\mu}{\kappa}}^{\infty}z^{\prime}\exp\left(-\frac{z^{\prime 2}}{2}\right)dz^{\prime}+\kappa\mu\int_{\frac{a-\mu}{\kappa}}^{\infty}\exp\left(-\frac{z^{\prime 2}}{2}\right)dz^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\kappa^{2}\exp\left(-\frac{z^{\prime 2}}{2}\right)\mid_{\infty}^{\frac{a-\mu}{\kappa}}+\kappa\mu\sqrt{2\pi}\left(1-\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{a-\mu}{\kappa}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\kappa^{2}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(\mu-a\right)^{2}}{2\kappa^{2}}\right)+\kappa\mu\sqrt{2\pi}\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\mu-a}{\kappa}\right)

∎

Lemma D.15
.

Let
\kappa,\mu,a\in\mathbb{R}
be given such that
\kappa>0
. Then we have that

\int_{a}^{\infty}z^{2}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(z-\mu\right)^{2}}{2\kappa^{2}}\right)dz=\kappa^{2}\left(a+\mu\right)\exp\left(-\frac{\left(\mu-a\right)^{2}}{2\kappa^{2}}\right)+\sqrt{2\pi}\kappa\left(\kappa^{2}+\mu^{2}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\mu-a}{\kappa}\right)

Proof.

To start, let
z^{\prime}=\frac{z-\mu}{\kappa}
. Then we have that
z=\kappa z^{\prime}+\mu
, and
dz=\kappa dz^{\prime}
. Therefore

\displaystyle\int_{a}^{\infty}z^{2}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(z-\mu\right)^{2}}{2\kappa^{2}}\right)dz

\displaystyle=\kappa\int_{\frac{a-\mu}{\kappa}}^{\infty}\left(\kappa z^{\prime}+\mu\right)^{2}\exp\left(-\frac{z^{\prime 2}}{2}\right)dz^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\kappa^{3}\int_{\frac{a-\mu}{\kappa}}^{\infty}z^{\prime 2}\exp\left(-\frac{z^{\prime 2}}{2}\right)dz^{\prime}+2\kappa^{2}\mu\int_{\frac{a-\mu}{\kappa}}^{\infty}z^{\prime}\exp\left(-\frac{z^{\prime 2}}{2}\right)dz^{\prime}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\kappa\mu^{2}\int_{\frac{a-\mu}{\kappa}}^{\infty}\exp\left(-\frac{z^{\prime 2}}{2}\right)dz^{\prime}

Notice that for the third term, we have that

\int_{\frac{a-\mu}{\kappa}}^{\infty}\exp\left(-\frac{z^{\prime 2}}{2}\right)dz^{\prime}=\sqrt{2\pi}\left(1-\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{a-\mu}{\kappa}\right)\right)=\sqrt{2\pi}\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\mu-a}{\kappa}\right)

For the second term, we can directly apply Lemma
D.14
with
\kappa=1,\mu=0
to get that

\int_{\frac{a-\mu}{\kappa}}^{\infty}z^{\prime}\exp\left(-\frac{z^{\prime 2}}{2}\right)dz^{\prime}=\exp\left(-\frac{\left(a-\mu\right)^{2}}{2\kappa^{2}}\right)

For the first term, we apply integration by parts with
u(z^{\prime})=-z^{\prime}
and
v(z^{\prime})=\exp\left(-\frac{z^{\prime 2}}{2}\right)
. In particular, notice that
v^{\prime}(z^{\prime})=-z^{\prime}\exp\left(-\frac{z^{\prime 2}}{2}\right)
and
u^{\prime}(z^{\prime})=-1
. Therefore

\displaystyle\int_{\frac{a-\mu}{\kappa}}^{\infty}z^{\prime 2}\exp\left(-\frac{z^{\prime 2}}{2}\right)dz^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\int_{\frac{a-\mu}{\kappa}}^{\infty}u(z^{\prime})dv(z^{\prime})

\displaystyle=u(z^{\prime})v(z^{\prime})\mid_{\frac{a-\mu}{\kappa}}^{\infty}-\int_{\frac{a-\mu}{\kappa}}^{\infty}v(z^{\prime})du(z^{\prime})

\displaystyle=-z^{\prime}\exp\left(-\frac{z^{\prime 2}}{2}\right)\mid_{\frac{a-\mu}{\kappa}}^{\infty}+\int_{\frac{a-\mu}{\kappa}}^{\infty}\exp\left(-\frac{z^{\prime 2}}{2}\right)dz^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\frac{a-\mu}{\kappa}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(\mu-a\right)^{2}}{2\kappa^{2}}\right)+\sqrt{2\pi}\left(1-\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{a-\mu}{\kappa}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{a-\mu}{\kappa}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(\mu-a\right)^{2}}{2\kappa^{2}}\right)+\sqrt{2\pi}\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\mu-a}{\kappa}\right)

Putting things together, we have that

\displaystyle\int_{a}^{\infty}z^{2}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(z-\mu\right)^{2}}{2\kappa^{2}}\right)dz

\displaystyle=\kappa^{3}\left(\frac{a-\mu}{\kappa}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(\mu-a\right)^{2}}{2\kappa^{2}}\right)+\sqrt{2\pi}\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\mu-a}{\kappa}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+2\kappa^{2}\mu\exp\left(-\frac{\left(a-\mu\right)^{2}}{2\kappa^{2}}\right)+\kappa\mu^{2}\sqrt{2\pi}\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\mu-a}{\kappa}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\kappa^{2}\left(a-\mu\right)+2\kappa^{2}\mu\right)\exp\left(-\frac{\left(\mu-a\right)^{2}}{2\kappa^{2}}\right)+\sqrt{2\pi}\left(\kappa^{3}+\kappa\mu^{2}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\mu-a}{\kappa}\right)

\displaystyle=\kappa^{2}\left(a+\mu\right)\exp\left(-\frac{\left(\mu-a\right)^{2}}{2\kappa^{2}}\right)+\sqrt{2\pi}\kappa\left(\kappa^{2}+\mu^{2}\right)\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\mu-a}{\kappa}\right)

∎

Lemma D.16
.

Let
z_{1},z_{2}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(0,1\right)
with
\text{Cov}\left(z_{1},z_{2}\right)=\rho
. Then we have that

\int_{a}^{\infty}f\left(z_{1}\mid z_{2}\right)dz_{1}=\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho z_{2}-a}{\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}}\right)

Proof.

Since
z_{1},z_{2}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(0,1\right)
with
\text{Cov}\left(z_{1},z_{2}\right)=\rho
, we have that
z_{1}\mid z_{2}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(\rho z_{2},1-\rho^{2}\right)
. Therefore, using a change of variable
z^{\prime}=\frac{z_{1}-\rho z_{2}}{\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}}
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{a}^{\infty}f\left(z_{1}\mid z_{2}\right)dz_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi\left(1-\rho^{2}\right)}}\int_{a}^{\infty}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(z_{1}-\rho z_{2}\right)^{2}}{2\left(1-\rho^{2}\right)}\right)dz_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\int_{\frac{a-\rho z_{2}}{\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}}}^{\infty}\exp\left(-\frac{z^{\prime 2}}{2}\right)dz^{\prime}

\displaystyle=1-\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{a-\rho z_{2}}{\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}}\right)

\displaystyle=\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho z_{2}-a}{\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}}\right)

∎

Lemma D.17
.

Let
z_{1},z_{2}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(0,1\right)
with
\text{Cov}\left(z_{1},z_{2}\right)=\rho
. Then we have that

\bm{\Phi}_{2}\left(-a,-b,\rho\right)=\int_{a}^{\infty}\int_{b}^{\infty}f\left(z_{1},z_{2}\right)dz_{2}dz_{1}=\int_{b}^{\infty}\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho z_{2}-a}{\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}}\right)f\left(z_{2}\right)dz_{2}

Proof.

Let
z_{1}^{\prime},z_{2}^{\prime}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(0,1\right)
with
\text{Cov}\left(z_{1},z_{2}\right)=\rho
, and define
z_{1}=-z_{1}^{\prime},z_{2}=-z_{2}^{\prime}
. Then we have that
z_{1},z_{2}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(0,1\right)
with
\text{Cov}\left(z_{1},z_{2}\right)=\rho
. By symmetry, we have
f\left(z_{1},z_{2}\right)=f\left(-z_{1},-z_{2}\right)=f\left(z_{1}^{\prime},z_{2}^{\prime}\right)
. Moreover,
dz_{2}^{\prime}dz_{1}^{\prime}=\left(-dz_{2}\right)\left(-dz_{1}\right)=dz_{2}dz_{1}
. Thus

\bm{\Phi}_{2}\left(-a,-b,\rho\right)=\int_{-\infty}^{-a}\int_{-\infty}^{-b}f\left(z_{1}^{\prime},z_{2}^{\prime}\right)dz_{2}^{\prime}dz_{1}^{\prime}=\int_{a}^{\infty}\int_{b}^{\infty}f\left(z_{1},z_{2}\right)dz_{2}dz_{1}

Recall that
f\left(z_{1},z_{2}\right)=f\left(z_{1}\mid z_{2}\right)f\left(z_{2}\right)
. Then we can apply Lemma
D.16
to get that

\int_{a}^{\infty}\int_{b}^{\infty}f\left(z_{1},z_{2}\right)dz_{2}dz_{1}=\int_{b}^{\infty}\left(\int_{a}^{\infty}f\left(z_{1}\mid z_{2}\right)dz_{1}\right)f\left(z_{2}\right)dz_{2}=\int_{b}^{\infty}\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\rho z_{2}-a}{\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}}\right)f\left(z_{2}\right)dz_{2}

∎

Lemma D.18
.

Let
z\sim\mathcal{N}\left(\mu,\kappa^{2}\right)
, and let
a\in\mathbb{R}
. Then we have

\mathbb{E}\left[z\mathbb{I}\left\{z\geq a\right\}\right]=\frac{\kappa}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(\mu-a\right)^{2}}{2\kappa^{2}}\right)+\mu\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\mu-a}{\kappa}\right)

Proof.

Define
\hat{z}=\frac{z-\mu}{\kappa}
. Then we have that
\hat{z}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(0,1\right)
. Since
z=\kappa\hat{z}+\mu
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[z\mathbb{I}\left\{z\geq 0\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\kappa\hat{z}+\mu\right)\mathbb{I}\left\{\hat{z}\geq\frac{a-\mu}{\kappa}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=\kappa\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{z}\mathbb{I}\left\{\hat{z}\geq\frac{a-\mu}{\kappa}\right\}\right]+\mu\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{I}\left\{\hat{z}\geq\frac{a-\mu}{\kappa}\right\}\right]

Notice that
\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{I}\left\{\hat{z}\geq\frac{a-\mu}{\kappa}\right\}\right]=\Pr\left(\hat{z}\geq\frac{a-\mu}{\kappa}\right)=\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\mu-a}{\kappa}\right)
. Moreover

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{z}\mathbb{I}\left\{\hat{z}\geq-\frac{\mu}{\kappa}\right\}\right]=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\int_{\frac{a-\mu}{\kappa}}^{\infty}\hat{z}\exp\left(-\frac{z^{2}}{2}\right)dz=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(a-\mu\right)^{2}}{2\kappa^{2}}\right)

Therefore

\mathbb{E}\left[z\mathbb{I}\left\{z\geq 0\right\}\right]=\frac{\kappa}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(a-\mu\right)^{2}}{2\kappa^{2}}\right)+\mu\bm{\Phi}_{1}\left(\frac{\mu-a}{\kappa}\right)

∎

Lemma D.19
.

.
Let
x\in[-1,1]
. Then we have that
|\arcsin x|\leq\frac{\pi}{2}\cdot|x|
.

Proof.

To start, consider the case
x>0
. Define
f(x)=\frac{\arcsin x}{x}
. Then we have that

f^{\prime}(x)=x^{-2}\left(\frac{x}{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}-\arcsin x\right);\quad\quad f^{\prime\prime}(x)=x^{-3}\left(\frac{3x^{3}-2x}{\left(1-x^{2}\right)^{\frac{3}{2}}}+2\arcsin x\right)

For all
x\in(0,1]
, we have that
1-x^{2}\geq 0
. Notice that by the Taylor expansion of
\arcsin x
, we have

\arcsin x\leq x+\frac{x^{3}}{6}

when
x\in(0,1]
. Therefore

\frac{3x^{3}-2x}{\left(1-x^{2}\right)^{\frac{3}{2}}}+2\arcsin x\geq\frac{3x^{3}-2x+2x\left(1-x^{2}\right)^{\frac{3}{2}}+\frac{x^{3}}{3}\left(1-x^{2}\right)^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\left(1-x^{2}\right)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\geq\frac{3x^{3}-2x\left(1-\left(1-x^{2}\right)^{\frac{3}{2}}\right)}{\left(1-x^{2}\right)^{\frac{3}{2}}}

Since
\left(1-x^{2}\right)^{\frac{3}{2}}\geq\left(1-x^{2}\right)^{3}\geq 1-3x^{4}+2x^{6}
, we must have that

3x^{3}-2x\left(1-\left(1-x^{2}\right)^{\frac{3}{2}}\right)\leq 3x^{3}-6x^{5}+4x^{7}=3x^{3}\left(1-2x^{2}+x^{4}\right)+x^{7}=3x^{3}\left(1-x^{2}\right)^{2}+x^{7}\geq 0

Thus, we must have that
f^{\prime\prime}(x)\geq 0
. Therefore, for any
\epsilon\in(0,0.1]
, we have that for
x\in[\epsilon,1]

\displaystyle f\left(x\right)

\displaystyle\leq f\left(\left((1+\epsilon)x-\epsilon\right)\cdot 1+(1-x)\cdot\epsilon\right)

\displaystyle\leq(1-x)\cdot f(\epsilon)+\left((1+\epsilon)x-\epsilon\right)\cdot f(1)

\displaystyle=(1-x)\cdot f\left(\epsilon\right)+\frac{\pi}{2}\cdot x-\frac{\pi}{2}\cdot\epsilon\left(1-x\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\pi}{2}\cdot x+1.002\left(1-x\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\pi}{2}

This gives that
f\left(x\right)\leq\frac{\pi}{2}
for all
x\in(0,1]
. Since
f\left(x\right)
is an even function, we have
f\left(x\right)\leq\frac{\pi}{2}
for all
x\in[-1,0)
. Therefore,
|\arcsin x|\leq\frac{\pi}{2}|x|
when
x\in[-1,1]\setminus\{0\}
. When
x=0
, we have
\arcsin x=0
. This completes the proof.
∎

Lemma D.20
.

Let
\mathbf{u}:=(|e_{1}|,...,|e_{n}|)\in\mathbb{R}^{n}
and
\mathbf{1}:=(1,...,1)\in\mathbb{R}^{n}.
Then

\sum_{i=1}^{n}|e_{i}|=u^{\top}\mathbf{1}.

By the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality,

u^{\top}\mathbf{1}\leq\|u\|_{2}\|\mathbf{1}\|_{2}.

Moreover,

\|u\|_{2}=\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}u_{i}^{2}\right)^{1/2}=\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}|e_{i}|^{2}\right)^{1/2}=\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}e_{i}^{2}\right)^{1/2},\qquad\|\mathbf{1}\|_{2}=\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}1^{2}\right)^{1/2}=\sqrt{n}.

Combining the above gives

\sum_{i=1}^{n}|e_{i}|\leq\sqrt{n}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}e_{i}^{2}\right)^{1/2}.

Lemma D.21
.

Assume that Assumption
3.1
holds. Then, we have:

\left\|\nabla_{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}}{\mathcal{L}}_{{\mathbf{C}}}\left(\mathbf{W}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}\leq\frac{\sqrt{n}}{\sqrt{m}}{\mathcal{L}}_{{\mathbf{C}}}\left(\mathbf{W}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Proof.

By the form of
\nabla_{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}}{\mathcal{L}}_{{\mathbf{C}}}\left(\mathbf{W}\right)
in (
2
), we have:

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla_{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}}{\mathcal{L}}_{{\mathbf{C}}}\left(\mathbf{W}\right)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\frac{a_{r}}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(f\left(\mathbf{W},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\odot{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\right)-y_{i}\right)\left({\mathbf{x}}_{i}\odot{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\right)\underbrace{\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}^{\top}\left({\mathbf{x}}_{i}\odot{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\right)\geq 0\right\}}_{\leq 1}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\left|a_{r}\right|}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|f\left(\mathbf{W},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\odot{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\right)-y_{i}\right|\cdot\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\odot{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sqrt{n}}{\sqrt{m}}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}(f\left(\mathbf{W},{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\odot{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\right)-y_{i})^{2}\right)^{1/2}\cdot\|{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\|_{\infty}\|{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\|_{2}\kern 5.0pt\text{using \ref{lem:CS}}

\displaystyle\leq C\frac{\sqrt{n}}{\sqrt{m}}\left(2{\mathcal{L}}_{{\mathbf{C}}}\left(\mathbf{W}\right)\right)^{1/2}\kern 5.0pt\text{using \ref{lem:c_infty_bound} with }\|{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\|_{\infty}\;\leq\;1+\kappa\sqrt{2\log\!\Big(\frac{2d}{\delta}\Big)}=C\kern 5.0pt\text{and}\kern 5.0pt\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\right\|_{2}\leq 1

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{2}\frac{\sqrt{n}}{\sqrt{m}}{\mathcal{L}}_{{\mathbf{C}}}\left(\mathbf{W}\right)^{1/2}

where, in the first inequality, we use the fact that the indicator function is upper-bounded by 1 and in the second inequality, we use the fact that
a_{r}=\pm 1
.

and so,

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla_{{\mathbf{w}}_{r}}{\mathcal{L}}_{{\mathbf{C}}}\left(\mathbf{W}\right)\right\|^{2}_{2}\leq 2C^{2}\cdot\frac{n}{m}{\mathcal{L}}_{{\mathbf{C}}}\left(\mathbf{W}\right)

∎

Lemma D.22

(High-probability
\ell_{\infty}
bound for Gaussian masks)
.

Fix
\delta\in(0,1)
. Let
{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
be a Gaussian mask with independent coordinates

{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\sim\mathcal{N}(\bm{1},\kappa^{2}{\mathbf{I}}_{d}),\qquad\text{i.e.,}\qquad c_{i,j}=1+\kappa g_{i,j},\;\;g_{i,j}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{i.i.d.}}}{{\sim}}\mathcal{N}(0,1).

Then, with probability at least
1-\delta
, we have

\|{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\|_{\infty}\;\leq\;1+\kappa\sqrt{2\log\!\Big(\frac{2d}{\delta}\Big)}.

Proof.

Since
{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\sim\mathcal{N}(\bm{1},\kappa^{2}{\mathbf{I}}_{d})
with independent coordinates, each coordinate can be written as

c_{i,j}=1+\kappa g_{i,j},\qquad\text{where }g_{i,j}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,1)\kern 5.0pti.i.d.

Hence

\|{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\|_{\infty}=\max_{j\in[d]}|c_{i,j}|=\max_{j\in[d]}|1+\kappa g_{i,j}|.

We observe that

\displaystyle|c_{i,j}|

\displaystyle=|1+(c_{i,j}-1)|

\displaystyle\leq|1|+|c_{i,j}-1|\kern 5.0pt\text{triangle inequality}

\displaystyle=1+|\kappa g_{i,j}|

We will first bound
\max_{j}|c_{i,j}-1|=\max_{j}|\kappa g_{i,j}|
, and then convert this into a bound on
\|{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\|_{\infty}
.

For brevity, we denote
g_{i,j}
as
g\sim\mathcal{N}(0,1)
and we are going to show that

\boxed{\Pr(|g|\geq t)\leq 2e^{-t^{2}/2}}

(79)

By symmetry of the standard normal distribution,

\Pr(|g|\geq t)=\Pr(g\geq t)+\Pr(g\leq-t)=2\Pr(g\geq t).

So it suffices to upper bound
\Pr(g\geq t)
.

For any
\lambda>0
, since the exponential is monotone increasing we have:

g\geq t\Rightarrow\lambda g\geq\lambda t\Rightarrow e^{\lambda g}\geq e^{\lambda t}

and so

\displaystyle\Pr(g\geq t)=\Pr(e^{\lambda g}\geq e^{\lambda t})

By Markov’s inequality, for any nonnegative random variable
X
and any
a>0
,

\Pr(X\geq a)\leq\frac{\mathbb{E}[X]}{a}

Applying this with
X=e^{\lambda g}
and
a=e^{\lambda t}
gives

\Pr(g\geq t)=\Pr\big(e^{\lambda g}\geq e^{\lambda t}\big)\leq\frac{\mathbb{E}[e^{\lambda g}]}{e^{\lambda t}}=\mathbb{E}[e^{\lambda g}]e^{-\lambda t}

(80)

Computation of the moment generating function
\mathbb{E}[e^{\lambda g}]
:

The standard normal density is

\varphi(x)=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}e^{-x^{2}/2},\qquad x\in\mathbb{R}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[e^{\lambda g}]

\displaystyle=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}e^{\lambda x}\varphi(x)dx=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\exp\!\Big(\lambda x-\frac{x^{2}}{2}\Big)dx.

(81)

We now study the exponent:

\lambda x-\frac{x^{2}}{2}=-\frac{1}{2}\big(x^{2}-2\lambda x\big)=-\frac{1}{2}\big((x-\lambda)^{2}-\lambda^{2}\big)=\frac{\lambda^{2}}{2}-\frac{(x-\lambda)^{2}}{2}.

Plugging this into (
81
) yields

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[e^{\lambda g}]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\exp\!\Big(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{2}-\frac{(x-\lambda)^{2}}{2}\Big)dx

\displaystyle=e^{\lambda^{2}/2}\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\exp\!\Big(-\frac{(x-\lambda)^{2}}{2}\Big)dx.

We set
u=x-\lambda
(change of variables with
dx=du
)

\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\exp\!\Big(-\frac{(x-\lambda)^{2}}{2}\Big)dx=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\exp\!\Big(-\frac{u^{2}}{2}\Big)du=\sqrt{2\pi}.

Hence

\mathbb{E}[e^{\lambda g}]=e^{\lambda^{2}/2}.

(82)

Substituting (
82
) into (
80
) gives

\Pr(g\geq t)\leq\exp\!\Big(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{2}-\lambda t\Big),\qquad\forall\lambda>0.

The right-hand side is a valid bound for
every

\lambda>0
, so we choose
\lambda
to make it as small as possible.
Define

f(\lambda):=\frac{\lambda^{2}}{2}-\lambda t.

Then
f^{\prime}(\lambda)=\lambda-t
, so the unique minimizer is
\lambda=t
(and
f^{\prime\prime}(\lambda)=1>0
confirms it is a minimum).
Plugging
\lambda=t
gives

\Pr(g\geq t)\leq\exp\!\Big(\frac{t^{2}}{2}-t^{2}\Big)=e^{-t^{2}/2}.

Using
\Pr(|g|\geq t)=2\Pr(g\geq t)
proves (
79
).

Now, we focus on one of the mask coordinates by fixing a coordinate
j\in[d]
. Since
c_{i,j}-1=\kappa g_{i,j}
with
g_{i,j}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,1)
, for any
u\geq 0
we have

\Pr\big(|c_{i,j}-1|\geq u\big)=\Pr\big(|g_{i,j}|\geq u/\kappa\big)\leq 2\exp\!\Big(-\frac{u^{2}}{2\kappa^{2}}\Big),

where we applied (
79
) with
t=u/\kappa
.

Let’s define the event

\mathcal{E}_{i}(u):=\Big\{\max_{j\in[d]}|c_{i,j}-1|\leq u\Big\}.

Its complement is the event that at least one coordinate deviates by more than
u
:

\mathcal{E}_{i}(u)^{c}=\Big\{\exists j\in[d]\ \text{s.t.}\ |c_{i,j}-1|>u\Big\}.

By the union bound,

\displaystyle\Pr\big(\mathcal{E}_{i}(u)^{c}\big)

\displaystyle=\Pr\Big(\bigcup_{j=1}^{d}\{|c_{i,j}-1|>u\}\Big)\leq\sum_{j=1}^{d}\Pr\big(|c_{i,j}-1|>u\big)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{d}2\exp\!\Big(-\frac{u^{2}}{2\kappa^{2}}\Big)=2d\exp\!\Big(-\frac{u^{2}}{2\kappa^{2}}\Big).

Choose
u
so that the right-hand side is at most
\delta
:

2d\exp\!\Big(-\frac{u^{2}}{2\kappa^{2}}\Big)\leq\delta\quad\Longleftrightarrow\quad-\frac{u^{2}}{2\kappa^{2}}\leq\log\!\Big(\frac{\delta}{2d}\Big)\quad\Longleftrightarrow\quad u\geq\kappa\sqrt{2\log\!\Big(\frac{2d}{\delta}\Big)}.

Set

u:=\kappa\sqrt{2\log\!\Big(\frac{2d}{\delta}\Big)}.

Then
\Pr(\mathcal{E}_{i}(u)^{c})\leq\delta
, i.e.
\Pr(\mathcal{E}_{i}(u))\geq 1-\delta
, and on
\mathcal{E}_{i}(u)
we have

|c_{i,j}-1|\leq u\quad\forall j\in[d].

On
\mathcal{E}_{i}(u)
, for each coordinate
j
,

|c_{i,j}|=|1+(c_{i,j}-1)|\leq|1|+|c_{i,j}-1|\leq 1+u.

Taking the maximum over
j
yields, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\|{\mathbf{c}}_{i}\|_{\infty}=\max_{j\in[d]}|c_{i,j}|\leq 1+u=1+\kappa\sqrt{2\log\!\Big(\frac{2d}{\delta}\Big)}.

∎
